BODY:
- **What:** `apply.always_available` (bool, `ApplyConfig` field, default False, config FILE only: not in `SETTING_KEYS`, no route writes it; only a real YAML `true` unlocks, "true"/1/"yes" stay False). `apply_flow.effective_policy` returns strict + `POLICY_NOT_RELEASED` ("fill_policy always ignored: not released on this install") when configured `always` but not released; `choose_policy` logs it once per attempt as for the other two reasons. Web: `fill_state` (status + settings) reports effective strict + `INACTIVE_NOT_RELEASED` while locked; `GET /api/apply/settings` gains `always_available`; `PUT fill_policy:"always"` while locked is 422 `{field: fill_policy, message: "Always answer isn't available yet: it is still being checked."}` with nothing stored. `ApplySettings.vue`: locked (server says `always_available !== true`) shows "Stop when unsure", "Always answer" radio `disabled` + `aria-disabled` + the sentence, nothing sent.
- **Every place the policy is read:** `apply_flow.effective_policy` is the only one that decides (`choose_policy` is its only caller; `self.policy` is assigned only there; `at.always`, the trial counter, compose's `forced = at.always and f.required`, `ResolveContext.fill_policy` / `model_sees_sensitive` and `pagefill._policy(rctx)` all derive from `self.policy`). The apply queue worker goes through `apply_flow.run_queue` -> same attempt code. No src code other than these read `acfg.fill_policy`, except: `cli.py::_fill_policy_lines` (doctor display; I added a "not released" branch, edited), `web/routers/apply.py::fill_state` (display; edited, web may not import apply_flow). `pagefill.py` untouched.
- **Red tests (the tester; all because they switch to `always` without the unlock):**
  0. `-k always` is 528/564 with my change: 36 reds (a1_w 1, always_flow 8, always_flow_fixes 6, always_flow_stops 5, always_flow_trial 12, q127_close 1, web_always 3). Verified WITHOUT editing tests (a stdin script patched `always_flow_support.acfg_for` as in item 1, then ran the 5 modules): a1_w 18/18, always_flow_fixes 12/12, always_flow_stops 17/17, always_flow 12/13, always_flow_trial 11/14; the 4 left are items 2 and 2b.
  1. `tests/always_flow_support.py::acfg_for`: add `always_available=True` to `base` (fixes every always-flow suite built on `run_batch` / `run_q` through `dataclasses.replace`; strict tests unaffected).
  2. `tests/test_always_flow.py::test_always_is_effective_only_with_a_local_model_and_the_llm_filler`: the `from_raw({"fill_policy": "always", "fill": "llm"})` lines (82, 88) need `"always_available": True`; add a new pin for the locked case: `(STRICT, POLICY_NOT_RELEASED)`, and that a stored settings row `always` cannot unlock.
  2b. `tests/test_always_flow_trial.py::_doctor` (line 270): start `apply_block` with `{"always_available": True, **apply_block}` (fixes `test_doctor_says_which_fill_policy_applies_and_why_always_is_off`, `..._shows_the_trial_and_warns_about_a_model_that_is_down`, `..._warns_when_the_profile_has_no_work_rights_rows...`); add a pin: locked doctor prints "always answer is not released on this install".
  3. `tests/test_web_always.py`: `_world(apply_block=...)` callers that PUT `always` (`test_policy_and_trial_round_trip_and_defaults_stay_as_config_has_them`, `test_always_that_is_configured_but_not_in_force_says_why_in_plain_words`, `test_the_status_block_reports_the_policy_in_force_and_the_trial_that_is_left`) need `apply_block={"always_available": True, ...}`; `test_the_routers_effective_policy_agrees_with_apply_flow_effective_policy` (lines ~384-420) passes today because both sides are locked; give its `ab` dicts `always_available` True/False cases so the agreement is pinned for both. Add: locked PUT always is 422 with the sentence and the settings row unchanged.
  4. `tests/test_web_apply_settings.py::test_apply_settings_get_returns_six_keys_and_a_put_moves_values_not_defaults` (line 123): the key set gains `always_available` (add it to `READ_ONLY`).
  5. `tests/test_q127_close.py::test_q127_close_compose_off_always_composes_a_required_cover_letter` (~line 151): its config must pass `always_available=True` with `fill_policy always`.
  6. `web/tests/q121-assumed.test.js` (4 reds: the Always confirm, going back to Stop when unsure, the confirm text, "configured but not in force"): add `always_available: true` to the `W` fixture (line 168). Add vitest pins for locked: the radio is `disabled`, `aria-disabled="true"`, shows the sentence, clicking sends nothing, value reads "Stop when unsure".
- **The later unlock (two lines):** `config/config.yaml`: `always_available: false` -> `true` (under `apply:`), and `apply.fill_policy: always` there if the owner wants it on by default (the code default stays `strict`). No code change. A stored settings row `always` from before the lock is neutralised while locked (the row picks Stop when unsure: the Vue rewrites it to strict on the owner's click).

### 2026-11-15 software-engineer → coordinator, tester
TYPE: handoff
RE: merge `personal` (523a1d5, Q-126 model auto-start, D-214) into `int/Q-121` (f9c361b); branch `eng/Q-121-merge-personal` (never push: history holds a deny-listed name); design `ollama_autostart_design.md` §10
BODY:
- **Conflict hunks, production code (all four resolved by keeping both sides; no behaviour of either changed):**
  - `apply/contracts.py` (1 hunk, the version comment): int's v8 + v9 blocks kept, then personal's Q-126 block (text verbatim; it still says "v8 after the later merge": architect's comment, not edited). `CONTRACT_VERSION` stays **9**. Everything else merged without a conflict and is a union: personal's six `QueuePause` model reasons, `MODEL_PAUSES`, `MODEL_PAUSE_VALUES`, `MODEL_CLICK_RESUME`, `MODEL_PAUSE_BY_CODE`, `RETURNS_ITEM`, and the reworded `QueuePause.MODEL_UNAVAILABLE` / `ReasonCode.MODEL_UNAVAILABLE` hints; int's FieldClass, v8/v9 names and the three reworded hints (`UNANSWERABLE_REQUIRED`, `DOCUMENT_MISSING`, `OWNER_FACT_MISSING`). The two sides reworded DIFFERENT hints, so no sentence needed merging.
  - `backends.py` (4 hunks): `Backend.warm`, `OllamaBackend.warm` and module `warm()` are all `warm(keep_alive=None, messages=None, *, timeout=None)`. `OllamaBackend.warm` posts to int's `route` (`/api/chat` with a prefix, else `/api/generate`) with `timeout=self.timeout if timeout is None else timeout`. Module `warm()` passes `messages=` and `timeout=` each only when given (so a backend whose `warm` takes only `keep_alive` is still called as before). `ensure_model_running`, `find_ollama`, `_spawn_detached`, the lock, `Probe.installed`, `reset_probe` are untouched (they merged clean).
  - `apply_flow.py` (2 hunks): the contracts import is the union (`MODEL_PAUSE_BY_CODE` added to int's import); `_Run.__init__` keeps int's `policy` / `compose_assumed` AND personal's `model_autostart`, `model_ensurer`, `model_pid_alive`, `model_retried`, `model_retries`. `run_queue`, `precheck`, `ensure_model`, `model_retry`, `pause_for` merged without a conflict and were read after the merge: pre-take ensure, `should_stop` re-check before `queue_take`, retry cap 2 / once per role, int's `is_content_failure` / `_PER_ROLE_NAMED` branches all present.
  - `web/routers/apply.py` (2 hunks): imports are the union (`backends`, `EXTRA_EMPLOYER_FIELD`, `REMEMBER_SCOPE_TEXTS`, `RememberScope`, `remembering` from int; `MODEL_CLICK_RESUME`, `MODEL_PAUSE_VALUES`, `fill_detail` from personal); `queue_view` keeps int's `assumed` / `notes` / `paths` reads and personal's `model = _model_view(store, slot)` (returned as `"model"`). Click rule (`_lift_model_pause`, `lifted`, `queue_resume_if(..., keep_window=MODEL_PAUSE_VALUES)`, Resume) and int's `details_needed` / `details_needed_by` / remember routes / `fill_policy` merged clean and were read.
  - Outbox files: both sides kept, int's entries on top, a `STATUS: open` line added after the int-side entry that git had shared (reviewer.md hunk 1 also needed a second header).
- **Merged without a conflict, read once:** `store.py` (personal's `queue_resume_if` / `queue_model_state` / `queue_model_view` block and `queue_pause_if_waiting(replace=)` beside int's v7-v9 migrations: no overlap, SCHEMA_VERSION still 9), `cli.py` (doctor line, `_ensure_model`, `_model_autostart`, `apply queue` kwargs; no overlap with int's doctor additions), `web/applyqueue.py`, `web/connections.py`, `pipeline.py` (personal only), `ApplyQueuePanel.vue` (int's `withAssumed` line + personal's model-state block), `ConnectionsHub.vue`, `ApplySettings.vue`, `App.vue` (no overlap). The committed bundle `src/jobscraper/web/static/` came from personal as merged (no conflict) and does NOT contain int's Vue changes (Q-127 Profile rebuild): the Coordinator rebuilds it in the main checkout.
- **F5 / retry interaction (found by reading, then by running):** a `model_unavailable` produced inside the always filler (a transport failure inside a vote, `always.py` -> `PageOutcome.transport_failed` -> `page_result` -> `ApplyFailure(MODEL_UNAVAILABLE)`) is an ordinary `failed/model_unavailable` row during FILLING, before any Submit click. `model_retry_wanted` reads the row from the store: retried only when `state == failed` and `submit_clicked_at is None`, with a real `OllamaBackend`; after a Submit click the row cannot be `failed` (NF-06), so no retry is possible there. K1 prompt refusals end `error` "prompt check" (not `model_unavailable`), so they are never retried. Run on the merge: the first such ending is retried once (the ensurer says ready), the second ends `model_not_answering` (role returned, paused).
- **For the tester (pins that both sides changed, or that the merge made stale); I cannot edit `tests/`:**
  1. `tests/test_q126_store.py:60` `test_no_schema_change` asserts `SCHEMA_VERSION == 6`; the merged product is 9 (`test_q127_store.py` pins 9). Retarget to "personal's Q-126 adds no schema change of its own", e.g. `>= 6` or compare to the v9 pin.
  2. `tests/always_flow_support.py` `run_q` runs `run_queue` with a REAL `OllamaBackend` over a mock transport. Since Q-126 the precheck calls `backends.ensure_model_running` for such a backend, and with no `model_ensurer` it pings the REAL port 11434 (refused here, autostart off) and pauses `model_unavailable` before any take. That turns **12 pins red** in `-k always` (a1_w: `test_a1_29_a_combined_box...`, `test_a1_29_the_consent_refused_capture...`, `test_a1_a_content_failure_ends_unanswerable_required...`, `test_a1_c8_consent_refused_is_per_role...`; flow_stops: `test_a_model_that_died_mid_attempt...`, `test_an_always_stop_whose_detail_lacks_the_marker...`, `test_document_missing_is_per_role...`, `test_more_guesses_than_the_page_cap...`, `test_owner_fact_missing_waits...`, `test_the_per_attempt_guess_limit...`, `test_the_same_fact_missing_on_three_roles...`; flow_trial: `test_the_trial_holds_in_the_queue_too...`). Fix: `run_q` passes `model_ensurer=lambda *a, **k: backends.ModelReady("ready")` (the design's seam). Verified: with `backends.ensure_model_running` stubbed to ready, all 12 pass.
  3. After (2), two pins in `tests/test_always_flow_stops.py` assert the pre-merge behaviour and must be retargeted to the merged one: `test_a_transport_failure_on_a_page_is_model_unavailable_pauses_and_returns_the_role` (expects ONE attempt row and pause `model_unavailable`; merged: the first `failed/model_unavailable` is retried once, so two rows, role back at the front, pause `model_not_answering`; to keep the old assertion use a `model_ensurer` that returns a not-ready `ModelReady("not_running")`, which pauses `model_unavailable` with no retry) and `test_a_warm_up_that_fails_is_model_unavailable_before_edge_opens_under_both_policies` (same: pause is `model_not_answering` after the one retry; first row is still `failed/model_unavailable`, step planning, Edge never opened). Add a pin for the F5 case: a vote transport failure retries once, a row with `submit_clicked_at` never.
  4. Any remaining reds from the full suite are listed in the report.
STATUS: open

### 2026-11-15 software-engineer → coordinator
TYPE: handoff
RE: Q-127 polish 2 (D-215), branch `eng/Q-127-polish2` on `int/Q-121` (31568c9); never push (history holds a deny-listed name)
BODY: Files: `web/src/attempts.js`, `components/WhatWasSent.vue`, `components/RememberedAnswers.vue`, `components/ApplySettings.vue`.
(1) Unlabelled field: title = `label`, else "Field with no label N (on “{step}”)" (`step` is the only grouping text the answers payload carries; there is no per-field section or group key; N numbers the unlabelled rows over the whole list), else "Field with no label N". A `too_general` refusal with no label reads "This field had no label on the form, so an answer can't be saved for it." plus the same "Change it in your details instead." link; the server's own sentence is ignored then (it quotes an empty label). New `fieldTitle()` in attempts.js; `refusalCopy` handles the empty label. Missing from the payload: a per-field section/group/index (the form's own place for a sub-field of a repeating block); a real fix needs a key in `ANSWERS_FIELD_KEYS` (contract).
(2) Answers you corrected: title = `title` if it differs from `key`, else the first of `labels` (the route does NOT send `labels` today; harmless), else the key as words ("Location city"). The scope line "Every application, with 1 employer answered differently" comes from the SERVER (`_scope_text` in `routers/apply.py`); left as is. Suggested server wording: "Every application; 1 employer has its own answer".
(3) Focus: NOT a bug (the Coordinator misread: `.setting-main` is the row button's own class, so focus already lands on the row). The `holdFocus()` re-check I had added was reverted in the next commit; ApplySettings.vue is back to the base version.
Tester pins (items 1 and 2 only): an empty-label row (title, numbering, no quotes, link); title fallback with and without `step`; `fieldTitle`; a raw-key entry ("location_city" -> "Location city"). No existing vitest went red.

### 2026-11-15 software-engineer → coordinator, tester
TYPE: handoff
RE: Q-127 U3 (D-215; q127_profile_questions_design B.1, B.2b, B.4 copy table, B.6, B.8 row U3, pin 5-UI); `web/src/components/RememberedAnswers.vue` (new), `components/ApplySettings.vue`, `tabs/Overview.vue`, `overview.js`, `App.vue`, `tabs.js`, `api.js`, `privacy.js`
BODY: Branch `eng/Q-127-U3` (from int/Q-121 32d47c1). NEVER push (int/Q-121 history). The shell, Overview and Settings no longer read a question list.
**Shell.** `App.vue`: `getQuestions` / `loadQuestions` / `openQuestions` removed. Badge data is `detailsNeeded` = `applyStatus.details_needed.length` (the status the shell already reads; null when the status failed or the key is absent = no badge). The status is re-read in `onChanged` and on leaving Profile (`refreshApplyStatus`, which also feeds the mode chip). `tabs.js` Profile: `badge: ({detailsNeeded}) => detailsNeeded`, `badgeLabel: 'details needed'`. The attention banner / Settings badge are untouched.
**Overview.** `overview.js`: `NO_FIELD_FACT_TITLES` (mirror of the contract's four), `stoppedCounts(rows)` -> `{waiting, noField: [{fact, n}], stuck}` counted from `GET /api/applications` rows through `attempts.rowState` (so only To apply, not queued, not "ready"); `nextUp({newRoles, waiting, noField, stuck, followUps, run, jobRunning})` ids `details`, `no-field` (one per fact, carries `fact`), `stuck`, then `inbox`, `follow-up`, `run`; the `questions` id and param are gone. `Overview.vue` no longer calls `/api/questions`; rows (`li.todo-row[data-kind]`): `details` "{n} applications are waiting for a detail" + `button` "Add details" (sets `profile.view=details`, goes to `#/profile`); `no-field` "{n} applications stopped: their forms ask for your {fact}" ("1 application stopped: its form asks for ...") + `a.button` "See applications" (`#/applications/active`); `stuck` "{n} applications stopped on a question JobScraper can't answer" + "See applications". The fact is one of the four fixed titles found inside `last_attempt.text`; a no_field stop whose text names none counts as `stuck`. An applications failure shows "Could not check which applications are waiting: ..." with Try again (was the questions notice) and blocks "caught up". Release 2 item not built.
**Settings -> Apply.** New row `#setting-answers-corrected` (row id `answers-corrected`; hash `#/settings/apply/answers-corrected` opens it, scrolls to it and focuses it; DOM editor `#setting-answers-corrected-editor`), in its own group "Saved answers". Label "Answers you corrected", value "{n} saved" / "None yet" / "Unavailable" (blank until the first read). It is never read-only (it is the owner's own list), hidden on a server whose `GET /api/apply/remembered` answers 404. `RememberedAnswers.vue` (emits `count`, `missing`; exposes `reload`): `ul.rem-list > li.rem-item[data-key]` with `.rem-title`, `.rem-detail` ("{answer} · {scope_text}"), `.ui-badge.rem-attention` ("Needs attention" for `needs_attention`), `button.rem-forget[data-key]` (aria-label "Forget {title}"); `.rem-more` "Show all {n}" after 20; confirm `.rem-confirm` (role group, text "Forget this answer? JobScraper will answer this question from your details again.", `button.rem-confirm-yes` "Forget", `button.rem-keep` "Keep"; focus lands on Keep; Esc / Keep return focus to that Forget button; after a Forget focus goes to the next item's Forget, else the one before, else the empty text); DELETE `apply/remembered/{key}?etag=`; 409 / 404 reload the list and `.rem-note` says "The saved answers changed since this list was loaded. ..." / "That answer was already gone. ..."; other errors `.rem-note` role alert; `.rem-empty` "None yet. To save one, open an application and choose What was sent."; `.rem-unreadable` (role status, link to `#/profile/details`); `.rem-retry` on a load error; skeleton `.rem-loading`.
**Fill policy (D2).** `ApplySettings.vue`: constant `FILL_POLICY_LABELS = {always: 'Always answer', strict: 'Stop when unsure'}`; the radios, the row value, the description and the inactive line use them; "Stop when unsure" sub = "It stops when your details don't cover a question. You answer them in that application's What was filled, then try again."; the confirm text now says What was sent / What was filled (no "Answered for you"); "Keep asking me first" -> "Keep stopping when unsure". Stored values and the default are unchanged (`always` / `strict`); nothing switches the policy.
**Removed.** `api.js`: `getQuestions`, `answerQuestion`, `keepAssumed`, `changeAssumed`, `dismissQuestion`, `retryQuestions` (nothing in `web/src` imports them now; `grep getQuestions|QuestionsInbox|QuestionCard web/src` = no match). `privacy.js`: stale comment only; `setModelSeesPrivate` / `modelSeesPrivate` stay because `web/tests/q74-private.test.js` imports them (no caller in `src`).
**Pin 5-UI (tester).** Shell: fake `GET api/apply/status` with `details_needed: [a, b, c]` -> `#tab-profile .badge` reads "3 details needed"; no `api/questions` call anywhere; the badge is absent when the key is missing or the status fails. Overview: rows as in `stoppedCounts` (a needs_input `owner_fact_missing` + `consent_refused` = waiting; `no_field` passport x2 / bank x1; a strict `unanswerable_required` = stuck; a `ready_to_retry` row and an `applied` row count for nothing; a no_field text with an unknown fact never shows its words). Settings: `#setting-answers-corrected .setting-value`, the 20 / "Show all 25", the confirm copy, DELETE url `api/apply/remembered/<key>?etag=<etag>`, focus after Keep / Forget, 409 note, empty / unreadable / error states, hidden on a 404, opens from the item `answers-corrected`; radios `.fill-options .mode-name` = ["Always answer", "Stop when unsure"]. My throwaway mount check (7 tests, all of the above) passed and was deleted.
**Red vitest beyond the known 40 (whole suite 53 failed | 916 passed | 23 skipped; 12 files; the known files are unchanged):** all "retired behaviour (expected)": `overview.helpers.test.js` 2 (`nextUp` with `questions` / the old order), `Overview.test.js` 5 (the Questions card: "renders a card per pending item", "does not claim to be caught up when the questions check failed", "Answer questions stores profile.view=details", "one question reads in the singular", "a failed questions check skips only that card"), `q75-shell.test.js` 3 (the Profile badge counts open question groups: now `details_needed`), `q100-settings.test.js` 3: "mode comes from /apply/status when /apply/settings is absent", "read-only when the server does not say writable: true", "writable: true shows the controls; mounting sends nothing at all" - the panel now sends ONE read-only `GET api/apply/remembered` on mount (the row's count) and the row list gains `answers-corrected`; the page's other rows still send nothing on mount and nothing is written. `QuestionsInbox.test.js` still skips itself.
**Departures / payload gaps.** (1) The mount-time GET above breaks the ApplySettings header's "loads nothing" rule on purpose: no existing payload carries the saved-answer count. If the Settings owner prefers, `loadApplyData()` in `Settings.vue` could fetch it and pass it down (then ApplySettings' own read can go). (2) Overview: `last_attempt` carries no `fact` key; the fact is matched from `text`; suggest `no_field_fact` on `attempt_view`. It also has no not-answered count (the strict "stuck" line counts roles, not questions). (3) `details_needed` counts the same field twice for salary (path `pay.expected_salary` and `.amount`), as U1 noted: the shell badge and the Profile tab label can differ by one. (4) The Profile pointer and WhatWasSent link to `#/settings/apply` without the row id: use `#/settings/apply/answers-corrected` to land on the row (their files are not mine). (5) "Write cover letters" description still says "Off: you're asked instead" (a card-era sentence, ApplySettings text outside this brief): the tester / architect should confirm what happens now. Browser checks at 1440 / 390 were not run (the Coordinator's).

### 2026-11-15 software-engineer → coordinator, tester, always.py owner
TYPE: handoff + request
RE: Q-127 F1 (D-215; q127_profile_questions_design B.2b, B.5 "Contract v9 as built", B.8 row F1); `src/jobscraper/apply_flow.py`, `apply/resolve.py`, `apply/briefing.py`, `web/routers/apply.py`, `applicant.py`
BODY: Branch `eng/Q-127-F1` (from int/Q-121 32d47c1). NEVER push (int/Q-121 history). Contracts, store, `always.py`, `pagefill.py`, `facts.py` untouched.
- **REQUEST (always.py / pagefill.py owner; I did not edit either): stamp the decided class on the `Answer` under `always`.** `audit_row` now writes `Answer.field_class` verbatim when it is set (and derives `declaration:<label>` from a strict tier-4 `declaration:<name>` source when it is not), but nothing sets it under `always`: `always.to_answer` builds the `Answer` without the class, and the class only reaches `pagefill._AlwaysRun.classes` (the cache rows). `audit_row` cannot get it from what it receives (`Answer`, `FormField`: the class is not in either). The least invasive single place is `pagefill._always_outcome` (pagefill.py ~line 1428), one line after `answers = dict(run.answers)`: `answers = {k: (_replace(a, field_class=al.classes[k]) if al.classes.get(k) and not a.field_class else a) for k, a in answers.items()}` (`al.classes[k]` = `states[k].model_class or class_string(keys[k].klass, keys[k].which)`, set by `fill_page_always(classes=)` for every open field). The alternative is `always.to_answer` (always.py:5850): add `field_class=st.model_class or class_string(...)` to the `Answer(...)` there, but it needs the code key (`keys[k]`), which `to_answer` does not have, so pagefill is the simpler place. Until it lands, `always` rows are stored with `field_class` "" unless the source is a declaration; W1's `follows_your_details` still refuses them by source (`profile:*`, `standing:*`, `default:*`, `field_map*`, `declaration`), but a model-answered declaration / work-rights / consent row (source `llm` / `assumption`) is NOT refused by class until this is done: H3 is only half-closed under `always` without it. Pin for the tester when it lands: a decided class `work_right` / `declaration:pep` / `consent_privacy` on an `always` fill reaches `apply_answers.field_class` (through `audit_row` or a fake page).
- **Built.** `apply_flow.stored_class(a)` + `audit_row` key `field_class`: `Answer.field_class` wins; else a strict `declaration:<name>` source gives `declaration:<name>` ("a+b" takes its first label with a class, a label with no class of its own `declaration:other`, `disability` -> `self_id:disability`); else "" (never None: the column is NULL only on rows written before v9). Fake-driven: strict "Are you a politically exposed person?" -> `declaration:pep`; strict work rights (source `profile:...`) -> "" (refused by source); strict ordinary (`memory:extra.*`) -> ""; an `Answer` stamped `declaration:pep` / `work_right` / `ordinary` is stored as given.
- **Scope.** `resolve._extra_answer` (tier 3) skips an entry when `extra_entry_applies(entry, ctx.employer_name, ctx.employer_aliases)` is false, and skips a `promoted: true` entry always (H1: never matched by wording). `briefing._answers` (the saved-answer list for `build` and `answers_block`) skips such an entry without listing or counting it as left out. New helper `resolve.entry_applies_here(entry, ctx)`. DEPARTURE (defence in depth, same lane): the other readers of `ctx.profile["extra"]` by key (`facts.fact_catalogue` / `_extra_usable`, `guards`, `always` refs, `facts` 574/816/839) are NOT in my files, so `apply_flow.profile_here(employer, aliases)` builds the `ResolveContext` with the entries of other employers' scopes removed from `extra` (the same object when nothing is dropped). A direct call of `facts.fact_catalogue(profile, rctx)` with a hand-built rctx would still list a scoped entry for another employer: if you want that closed at the source too, `facts._extra_usable` needs one line at its top: `if not extra_entry_applies(entry, rctx.employer_name, rctx.employer_aliases): return None`.
- **Follow-ups.** (a) `GET /api/apply/status` `details_needed` is de-duplicated by form item (`applicant.dedupe_by_item`, `applicant.form_item_key`: a path under an `entries` item keeps its own key, `pay.expected_salary` and `pay.expected_salary.amount` are one item, the first path kept, order unchanged); `details_needed_by` collapses the same way (first path of the item, the larger count). The U1 `detailsNeeded` and the shell badge now agree. (b) `last_attempt.not_answered`: SKIPPED. The store has no method that counts a set of attempts' not-answered fields in one query (`answers(attempt_id)` returns every row per attempt: N queries and full rows per list call, and `attempt_view` is the list's hot path). It needs `store.not_answered_counts(attempt_ids) -> {attempt_id: n}` beside `assumed_counts` (required = 1 AND status IN ('unknown', 'ungrounded')); then `attempt_view(..., not_answered=)` is one line. Request for the store owner (store.py is S1's). (c) nothing else; `refusal_text` not added.
- **Tester pins.** `-k q127`: `_RED_UNTIL_F1` is green (rename it); the generic-label pin stays red (fixture). New: (1) tier 3 with a scoped entry: same name, alias and a different case match; Northwind does not; an unscoped entry matches both; a promoted entry with `labels: ["x"]` is never matched; (2) the briefing text of a job at Northwind holds neither the scoped entry's key nor its value, and its `answers_left_out` is unchanged; (3) `audit_row` carries `field_class` for a strict declaration / an `Answer` stamped by `always`; (4) `profile_here` (or a full attempt on fakes) leaves the scoped entry out of `rctx.profile["extra"]` for another employer; (5) `applicant.dedupe_by_item` / `form_item_key` table and `GET /api/apply/status` with `pay.expected_salary` missing and a waiting role on `pay.expected_salary.amount` (one entry in `details_needed`).
- Known reds at my base, not mine: `-k resolve` -> `test_web_always::test_keep_saves_each_kind_in_the_shape_the_resolver_reads` posts to the retired `/api/questions/{id}/keep` (410): stale by M5, for T0.
STATUS: open

### 2026-11-15 software-engineer → coordinator, U3, tester
TYPE: handoff
RE: Q-127 U2 (D-215; q127_profile_questions_design B.2b-e, B.4 copy table, B.6, B.8 row U2, pins 1-UI, 13-UI); `web/src/components/WhatWasSent.vue` (new), `components/ApplicationRow.vue`, `attempts.js`
BODY: Branch `eng/Q-127-U2` (from int/Q-121 08b16ea = after U1). NEVER push (int/Q-121 history). `api.js` untouched (U0's `getAttemptAnswers`, `rememberAnswer`, `getApplicant` for the etag, `problemOf` were enough).
**Row (ApplicationRow.vue).** Chip + actions per the copy table (`rowState` in attempts.js): needs_input + owner_fact_missing -> "Needs one detail: {fact}" (fact from the contract sentence, else the words of `owner_fact_path`) + `a.app-add` "Add it" (`openAnswer(target)`); consent_refused -> "Needs your agreement: {consent}" + Add it (Consents group); document_missing -> "Needs one file: {detail}" + Add it (`openAnswer('documents')`, the Files group); strict unanswerable_required -> "Stopped: {n} questions had no answer" (n unknown until the panel has loaded: "Stopped: a question had no answer"); `last_attempt.ready_to_retry` on a stopped / needs_input kind -> "Ready to try again" (tone ok) and the existing `button.app-retry` gets class `primary`; `last_attempt.no_field` -> "Stopped: this form asks for your {fact}, which your details can't hold. Nothing was sent." with NO Try again (`noRetry`; nothing can be added); any other needs_input (profile_incomplete) -> "Needs your details" + Add it. New `button.app-sent` "See what was sent" (sent / unconfirmed) / "See what was filled" (dry_run, needs_input, stopped), `aria-expanded`, `aria-controls` = `app-sent-{job_id}`; it opens `WhatWasSent` in `.app-below` (above the slot), not draggable while open. Removed: `.app-answer` "Answer in Profile", `.app-review` "Review answers", `.app-document` "Add it in Profile", `openAssumed`, `ASSUMED_ANCHOR`, `ASSUMED_FOCUS_KEY`. Kept: `answerTarget`, `openAnswer`, `FIELD_FOCUS_KEY`, `CONSENTS_GROUP`, `canRetry`, the Try again call (only the owner's click; no automatic call).
**Panel (WhatWasSent.vue; props `jobId, attemptId, sent, employerName`; emits `close`, `loaded {notAnswered, assumed, all}`, `saved {ready, answerId, key}`).** `section[data-what-was-sent]` with `h4.wws-title` (focused on open; Escape closes the editor, then the panel; Close returns focus to `.app-sent`), `.wws-sub`, `.wws-entries` (entries_note), `.wws-trial`, `.wws-note` (a panel message, `role=alert`), the filter `.ui-segmented[role=radiogroup]` (`All {n}` / `Assumed {n}` / `Not answered {n}`; empty options disabled), `li.wws-row[data-field=<id>][data-status][data-assumed]` with `.wws-label`, `.wws-value`, `.wws-basis`, `.wws-next` ("Next time: {remembered_text}"), badges `.wws-badge-assumed` / `.wws-badge-empty` / `.wws-badge-saved`, `button.wws-correct` ("Use a different answer next time" on a sent row; "Answer for next time" on a not-sent form or a not-answered row), `.wws-refusal` (+ `.wws-refusal-link`: a button -> `openAnswer('')` for Your details, `a[href="#/settings/apply"]` for the shared answer), `.wws-done`, `form.wws-editor` (radios for a choice of up to 6 options, a `select` above that, checkboxes for multi, Yes/No radios for a tick box, `input[type=number|date|text]`, `textarea`), `.wws-scope input` (checked), `.wws-help` (sent only), `.wws-field-error`, `button.wws-save` ("Save for next time"), `button.wws-cancel`; states `.wws-loading` (skeleton), `.wws-empty`, `.wws-error` with `button.wws-retry`. Save = `GET applicant` for the etag, then the POST `{picks | value, only_employer, etag}` (a choice or Yes/No WITH options is sent as `picks:[option label]`; a tick box as `value: bool`; number as a Number). Results: 200 -> `remembered_text`, badge, done line (sent: "This form was already sent. Your answer is used from the next application on."; not sent: "Saved. Try this application again when you're ready.") and `saved` -> the row shows "Ready to try again" when `ready_to_retry`; 409 changed -> the answers reload and the note says so; 409 shared_answer / 422 follows_your_details / too_general -> the row switches to its refusal line (the server's `text` is used for the lead); 422 invalid -> the server's messages under the field; a missing etag read, 404 and other errors say so. No path calls `POST /api/apply`.
**Pins for the tester.** (1-UI): mount `WhatWasSent` with a fake `fetch` (a copy of my throwaway check is at `C:\Users\Admin\AppData\Local\Temp\claude\b3f57f5c5f0c763d\2381e34a-450f-47ab-be3d-d084eda285dc\scratchpad\zz_u2.check.test.js`: 6 tests, all green at 9373342; it needs the vitest config `include` changed to run from `tests/`): every basis word, an assumed row, a not-answered row, `entries_note`, the trial badge, the filter counts, the editor per kind, scope checked, the POST body, the helper and done lines, the 409 / 422 paths, empty and error states. (13-UI): a row with `can_remember: false` has no `.wws-correct` for each refusal code and shows the contract sentence; the pure helpers `refusalCopy`, `fieldKind`, `answerBody`, `startValue`, `sentEntry`, `sentSubLine`, `fieldCounts`, `filterFields`, `correctionLabel`, `rowState` (new labels), `canRetry` (no_field false), `pathWords` are exported from attempts.js. 
**Red vitest beyond the known 37 (whole suite 40 failed | 929 passed | 23 skipped; 8 files):** 3 in `q76-detail.test.js`, "retired behaviour (expected)": `the auto-apply chip on a row > "Needs your answers" carries the reason in its title, with an "Answer in Profile" link` (the strict stop now reads "Stopped: a question had no answer" with "See what was filled"), `> "Answer in Profile" remembers Application details and goes to Profile` and `> end to end in the app: the click lands on the Application details view` (the link is now "Add it" on a needs_input owner_fact_missing row: `.app-add`; the click still sets `jobscraper.profile.field` and goes to #/profile). Hidden by the load failures already known and to be retargeted by the tester: `q121-assumed.test.js` (11 mentions of "Review answers" / "Answer in Profile" / "Needs your input" / `.app-review`) and `q120-queue-ui.test.js` (2).
**Payload gaps and departures.** (1) The answers GET carries only the refusal CODE; the sentences ("This answer follows your details. Change it in Your details." etc.) are copied from `REMEMBER_REFUSAL_TEXTS` in `refusalCopy` (attempts.js), because there is no text key to read. Suggest adding `refusal_text` to the field if the contract is ever to be the only copy. (2) `last_attempt` has no count of not-answered fields, so the strict chip's "{n} questions" appears only after the panel has been opened once (until then "Stopped: a question had no answer"). Suggest `not_answered: n` on `attempt_view`. (3) The POST needs the profile's etag: the panel reads `GET /api/applicant` just before saving (one extra read). (4) The panel opens for the row's LAST attempt only. The older attempts in the details list (`tabs/Applications.vue`, not mine) still show their own "Answer in Application details" link (`answerTarget` / `openAnswer` still work); a per-attempt "See what was sent" there would need a change in that file. (5) "Add it" for `profile_incomplete` has no target (opens Your details). (6) The settings link is `#/settings/apply` (no row id: U3 owns the "Answers you corrected" row; add the hash item if it has one). (7) The refusal sentence is shown on every refused row (the design says so); a form with many profile-fed fields repeats it: say if you want it only on hover or once per panel. Browser checks at 1440 and 390 were not run (the Coordinator's). 
STATUS: open

### 2026-11-15 software-engineer → coordinator, U2, U3, tester
TYPE: handoff
RE: Q-127 U0 + U1 (D-215; q127_profile_questions_design B.4, B.6, B.8 rows U0 / U1, pins 6, 7, 14, 18, 19, 21); `web/src/api.js`, `tabs/Profile.vue`, `components/ApplicationDetails.vue` (rebuilt), `components/DetailSection.vue`, `detailSections.js`
BODY: Branch `eng/Q-127-U1` (from int/Q-121 9ebd658). NEVER push (int/Q-121 history). **U0 = commit 9dc0daf (api.js only), so U2 / U3 start from it:** `getAttemptAnswers(jobId, attemptId)`, `rememberAnswer(jobId, attemptId, answerId, body)`, `getRemembered()`, `forgetRemembered(key, etag)`; comments added to `getApplicant` (sections / needed_by / waiting / extra_errors) and `applyStatus` (details_needed / details_needed_by). The retired `getQuestions` / `answerQuestion` / `keepAssumed` / `changeAssumed` / `dismissQuestion` / `retryQuestions` stay (marked RETIRED) because `App.vue` and `tabs/Overview.vue` (U3) still import `getQuestions`; U3 removes them with those imports.
U1: Your details = a status card (badge, "Auto-apply waits until these are filled: {labels}.", "Fill in", "{n} applications are waiting for a detail below.", the M4 line from `extra_errors`) + ONE card of `DetailSection` rows built from `data.sections` (collapsed, one open at a time; `sectionsOf` falls back to one section per form group on a server without `sections`) + the muted "Answers you corrected for next time are in Settings → Apply." pointer; "Other keys in your file" is a muted `<details>` at the bottom (never `extra.*`). Section 11 is not built. Item markup / `FieldInput` / `entries` are the old block, now inside `DetailSection` (default slot, rendered only while open; a section with several groups heads each group). Save section PUTs the saved profile with only that section's paths from the draft (`takeSection`), other sections' unsaved edits are put back on the reply; 409 / 422 / problem notices as before; a refused save focuses the first error field (the summary banner when it is in another section). Dirty leave = in-row confirm. `Profile.vue`: tab label "Your details {n}", `getQuestions` call removed, sub-line per the copy table, first-visit rule unchanged.
`detailSections.js` exports (pure, no Vue): `NOTHING_YET`, `sectionsOf(data)`, `itemsOfSection`, `blocksOfSection`, `sectionOfPath(sections, path, form)` (a path, sub-path, entry path or form group id), `shortDate`, `sectionSummary(section, profile, form, docs?)`, `pathFilled`, `neededByFor(item, neededBy)`, `sectionState(section, {form, profile, missing, neededBy, errors})` -> `{status: needed|fill|complete, text, tone, needed, toFill, fix, fixText}`, `detailsNeeded(missing, neededBy, form?)`, `labelOfPath`, `statusLine({form, needed, waiting, extraErrors})`, `neededByText`, `withoutExtraErrors`, `sectionDirty`, `takeSection`, `revertSection`, `newlyReady(section, before, after)`, `waitingNoticeText(n)`, `applyAgainText(n)`.
Tester, pin: (6) / (14) / (18) mount `ApplicationDetails` with an employer-named `extra` entry, an `extra.*` error and open / assumed rows in the questions fakes: the DOM holds no employer name or question label and no `QuestionsInbox`; the status card says "A saved answer needs attention. …"; (7) `detailSections.js` (summaries incl. "{n} entries · {first title}", "Nothing filled yet", states Needed / {n} to fill / Complete, "Fix 1", `needed_by` -> "Needed" on the right section); (19) a section save issues PUT only, never `POST api/apply`, and "Apply again to n applications" posts `api/apply` with exactly the ready job ids only on click; (21) already green (`-k q127-other-keys` 3/3). Selectors: row `#ds-<section id>-button` (`aria-expanded`, `aria-controls` `ds-<id>-panel`), `.ds-save` / `.ds-cancel`, `.ds-confirm`, `.status-card`, `.fill-in`, `.waiting-notice` (`.apply-again`, `.dismiss`), `.other-keys`.
Red vitest after this lane (whole suite 37 failed | 932 passed | 23 skipped, 7 files): all "retired behaviour (expected)", none mine: `ApplicationDetails.q71.test.js` 36 of 44 (fields now sit behind a collapsed row and the save button reads "Save section": the field behaviours (country select, pruned blank entries, Needed clearing, checks on blur, private tags, from-resume, doc status) still work when the section is opened, checked by a throwaway mount; the rail / Next needed / "Save details" tests are retired); `q100-shell.test.js` 1 (reads `.save-bar` in ApplicationDetails.vue: the sticky bar is `.ds-actions` in `DetailSection.vue`); load failures because they import the deleted components: `ApplicationDetails.test.js`, `QuestionCard.test.js`, `q121-assumed.test.js`, `q74-private.test.js` (its Details-form cases need to move off the import), `q120-queue-ui.test.js` (one dynamic `QuestionsInbox` import fails the file; its other tests are fine). `QuestionsInbox.test.js` skips itself (file absent). `Profile.test.js` and `q118-profile.test.js` are green.
Outside my lane that still names the deleted components: `src/privacy.js` comment only (`setModelSeesPrivate` has no caller now; U3 / tester decide); no other import. `components/SectionRail.vue` + `sectionStatus` (applicantForm.js) are now unused by the page (left in place: components.test may import them).
Departures: (1) L5: `applicantForm.js` is unchanged (pin 21 already holds; section state lives in `detailSections.js`). (2) The dirty-leave confirm has a third button "Keep editing" beside the copy table's "Save" / "Discard changes" (a mis-click must be undoable). (3) `detailsNeeded(.., form)` counts "pay.expected_salary" (missing) and "pay.expected_salary.amount" (waiting) once, per form item; the server's `details_needed` (shell badge) lists them as two paths: the Profile tab label and the shell badge can then differ by one. Suggest the server de-duplicates by form item. (4) The page reads the saved profile for the status and the row states, the draft for the field badges ("Needed" clears as the owner types, as before). No dev server was started; the browser checks are the Coordinator's.

### 2026-11-15 software-engineer → coordinator, F1, U0-U3, tester
TYPE: handoff
RE: Q-127 W1 (D-215; q127_profile_questions_design B.2b-e, B.4, B.5, B.8 row W1, B.11 H1-H3 M4 M5); `src/jobscraper/web/remember.py` (new), `web/routers/{applications,apply,applicant,questions}.py`, `applicant.py`
BODY: Branch `eng/Q-127-W1` (from int/Q-121 68ce9cf), commit d5b9e8d + the outbox commit. NEVER push (int/Q-121 history). Contracts and store untouched. Routes as built (all under `/api`; U0 builds `api.js` on these):
- **GET `/applications/{job_id}/attempts/{attempt_id}/answers`** -> `{attempt: {id, kind, state, finished_at, trial, entries_note}, employer, fields: [{id, step, label, type, required, value_text, basis_text, assumed, reason_text, status: sent|not_answered|not_sent, remembered_text, can_remember, refusal: ""|follows_your_details|shared_answer|too_general, options: [labels]}]}` (exactly `ANSWERS_ATTEMPT_KEYS` / `ANSWERS_FIELD_KEYS`; no other key, so the "needs one detail" path is NOT here: it is `last_attempt.owner_fact_path` on the Applications list). 404 when the attempt is not that job's. Hidden, skipped and blank-optional rows are left out. `value_text` of a file is its name only. `reason_text` only for assumed rows (redacted reasons read ""). `can_remember` false + `refusal` set per H3 / H2 / too-general (the GET passes the profile's `extra`, so `shared_answer` shows here too). `remembered_text` = the answer the owner saved ("" none): U2 shows "Saved for next time".
- **POST `/applications/{job_id}/attempts/{attempt_id}/answers/{answer_id}/remember`** body `{value | picks: [option labels as offered], only_employer: true (default), etag}` -> 200 `{key, remembered_text, ready_to_retry, etag}`. Errors are `{detail: code, text?, errors?: [{path, message}]}`: 404 `not_found` (answer not of that attempt / job); 422 `follows_your_details` / `too_general` (with `text`), 422 `invalid` (+ `errors`: wrong value for the kind, picks not among the options, choice needs exactly one, employer unknown); 409 `shared_answer` (+ `text`), 409 `changed` / `unreadable` (the profile router's: stale etag). Writes `extra.<slug_key(label_norm, existing keys)>` = `{title (<=60 chars of the label), description "", kind, sensitive, labels: [label_norm], value | picks+seen, employer?}`; `employer` = the employer's display name when `only_employer`. Entry for the same exact label that is its own (labels exactly [label], not promoted, no `by_employer`) is replaced in place (key unchanged, scope replaced); any other holder -> 409. Never touches another entry. Under `PROFILE_WRITE_LOCK`, `applicant.save` (atomic writer), then `store.mark_answer_corrected`. Never starts an application.
- **GET `/apply/remembered`** -> `{items: [{key, title, value_text, scope_text, employer, kind, sensitive, needs_attention}], count, etag, unreadable}` (newest first; non-promoted `extra` entries, so the old cards' answers are listed too; `needs_attention` = fails validation and tier 3 skips it). **DELETE `/apply/remembered/{key}?etag=`** -> `{key, forgotten: true, etag}`; 404 `not_found`, 409 `changed` / `unreadable`, 422 `invalid`. DEPARTURE: the list is an object with `etag` (Forget needs it), not a bare array.
- **GET `/applications`** additive `last_attempt.ready_to_retry` (bool) and `last_attempt.no_field` (bool; true for `owner_fact_missing` whose `reason_detail` is a `NO_FIELD_FACT_TITLES` title: kind `stopped`, `text` = `OWNER_FACT_NO_FIELD_TEXT`, `hint` = `OWNER_FACT_NO_FIELD_HINT`, `owner_fact_path` null). `attempt_view` (also on `GET .../attempts`) carries both keys; a queued row has both false. `ready_to_retry`: a role in `waiting_on_details()` -> every waited path now filled (`applicant.path_filled`: consents need True); any other stop with captured questions -> `retry_candidates` open = 0 and dismissed = 0; never for a job with a submitted / submitted_unconfirmed attempt; false unless the attempt is failed.
- **GET `/apply/status`** additive `details_needed: [path]` (= `missing` then waiting paths still empty, de-duplicated) and `details_needed_by: {path: n applications}`. DEPARTURE from the brief's "each item names the path and the count": `details_needed` stays a list of path strings (the tester's pin 5 and B.5 say so); the counts are in `details_needed_by`.
- **GET / PUT `/applicant`** (and the POST `/applicant/refresh` reply) additive: `sections: [{id, title, paths}]` (`applicant.SECTIONS`, the ten of B.4, every FORM item in exactly one), `needed_by: {path: n}`, `waiting: [{job_id, paths, ready}]` (for the "Saved. {n} applications were waiting" notice: the roles whose paths intersect the saved section and are `ready` after the PUT), `extra_errors: n` (invalid saved answers; the status line text only). `errors` no longer lists `extra.*` (M4): GET, PUT and `POST /applicant/check` all leave them out, and PUT / refresh / remember / forget pass `tolerate_unchanged_extra=<profile on disk>` to `applicant.save`, so an unchanged invalid entry never blocks a save while a changed one still 422s. `applicant.validate` accepts `extra.<k>.promoted` (bool), `employer` (str) and `labels: []` ONLY with `promoted: true` (H1); both join `EXTRA_META_FIELDS`.
- **GET `/questions`** (M5): `{mode, groups: [], counts: {open: 0, assumed: 0, answered: 0, dismissed: 0}, ready: [], waiting: [], busy, model_sees_private, retired: true}` for every `status`; POST `/questions/answer`, `/questions/retry`, `/questions/{id}/keep`, `/questions/{id}/change`, `/questions/{key}/dismiss` -> 410 `{detail: "retired", text}`; no row is touched, nothing is queued. `routers/questions.py` is now ~100 lines.
- Strict policy (D2): no policy branch anywhere; the same routes work. No log line is written by any new code.
DEPARTURES / pins I did not satisfy by bending code: (1) `tests/test_q127_web.py::test_q127_a_generic_label_with_a_captured_row_is_remembered_under_its_qualified_label` stays RED on purpose: its fixture captures "Please specify" with the BARE label_norm (`question_row` does not qualify it), but a real captured generic label is `norm | context` (plan.qualified_label). I refuse any label that is still generic (422 `too_general`) even with a captured row, because an entry for the bare label would be a saved answer for every "Please specify" on every form. Fix the fixture (give the captured row `label_norm="please specify | @s1#0"`) or tell me the intended rule. (2) `..._RED_UNTIL_F1` stays red until F1's `extra_entry_applies` is applied in resolve.py / briefing.py.
For F1: nothing from W1 blocks you. The entry's scope field is `employer` (display name); apply `extra_entry_applies(entry, ctx.employer_name, ctx.employer_aliases)` in tier 3 and the briefing. A remembered answer's `labels == [label_norm]` is the captured row's `label_norm` when one exists, else the audit row's.
For U0-U3: `api.js` names are yours; `POST /apply` is still the only starter ("Apply again"); `ready_to_retry` is also in the remember reply. The Profile page must hide `extra.*` (it gets none in `errors`; `extra_errors` only for the one status line).
For the tester (T0 + pins): (a) `tests/test_connections.py`: add `/api/apply/remembered` to `SWEEP_GET_ALLOWLIST` (read-only, parameter-free; `/api/questions` stays allowlisted). (b) parity pins for the three copies in `web/remember.py` of stage code (web may not import it): `norm_label`, `is_generic` (+ `_STOP_WORDS`), `slug_key` against `apply.plan` / `apply.questions` over a table of labels (options too). (c) pins for `applicant.SECTIONS` (every FORM item once; B.4 titles), `path_filled`, `drop_unchanged_extra_errors`, `labels: []` only with promoted, `GET /apply/remembered` + DELETE (404 / 409 / 422, a promoted entry is not listed or forgettable). (d) Old suites now red (classify stale or defect, do not keep old behaviour): see the totals in the report.
Limits: `ready_to_retry` for a waiting-path role follows B.2e literally (every waited path filled); an attempt that stopped on a path-bearing question AND other open questions reads ready once the path is filled. `answer_row` / `attempt_field_question` are one query per row on the answers GET (fine for <= a few hundred fields). A dev DB copy and the real model were not touched; no server was started.
