### 2026-11-15 software-engineer → coordinator, F1, W1, tester
TYPE: handoff
RE: Q-127 S1 (D-215); `src/jobscraper/store.py`; q127_profile_questions_design B.2b, B.3, B.5, B.8 (20-a)
BODY: Branch `eng/Q-127-S1` (from int/Q-121 0ddd971), one commit on `store.py` only. Never push (int/Q-121 history).
- **Schema v9** (`SCHEMA_VERSION = 9`): `apply_answers.field_class TEXT`, nullable, added in `_migrate` by `PRAGMA table_info` + `_add_column` like v7/v8 (not in the CREATE TABLE, same as `assumed`). Additive only; older rows stay NULL. Checked on temp DBs: v6 -> v9 and v8 -> v9, row count per table equal, `integrity_check` ok, `schema_version` 9, second open identical.
- **`add_answers`**: `field_class` is now one of the row dict's keys (missing = NULL). F1 passes the class string (`class_string(...)`, `declaration:<label>` under strict, "" when none; "" is stored as given, so W1 must treat NULL and "" alike: `contracts.field_class_of` does).
- **`answers(attempt_id) -> list[dict]`** (existing, SELECT *): now carries `field_class`, `corrected_json`, `corrected_at`. This is the What was sent read; with `attempt(attempt_id)` (has `mode`, `state`, `trial`, `entries_note`, `finished_at`, `job_id`) W1 builds `ANSWERS_ATTEMPT_KEYS` / `ANSWERS_FIELD_KEYS`. `options_json` / `value_json` are JSON text as stored (text values are stored as written, not JSON: see `assumed_answer`'s `_JSON_FIELD_TYPES` note).
- **`answer_row(answer_id: int) -> dict | None`**: the `apply_answers` row (all columns) plus `job_id`, `company_id`, `attempt_state`, `attempt_mode` from its attempt. None when absent.
- **`attempt_field_question(attempt_id: int, field_key: str) -> dict | None`**: the `questions` row the attempt captured for that field (via `question_sightings` on attempt_id + field_key; newest sighting wins), as `_question_dict` (so `options` parsed, `sensitive` bool; has `target_path`, `key`, `status`, `reason`) plus `sighting_label` and `sighting_required` (bool). None when nothing was captured. `target_path` is where "Add it" opens; `key` is never an `extra` key to write to (H2).
- **`mark_answer_corrected(answer_id: int, value_json, now: str) -> int | None`**: stamps `corrected_json` (text as given; a non-text value is JSON-encoded) and `corrected_at`, and sets every `questions` row that attempt captured for the field (status other than `answered`, i.e. open, assumed AND dismissed) to `answered` with `answered_at = now`. One commit. Returns the number of question rows marked (0 = none captured), None when the answer id is unknown. Only that attempt's field rows are touched; because a `questions` row is shared by sig, that row reads answered for other attempts too (same as the old cards).
- **`waiting_on_details() -> list[dict]`**: `[{job_id, attempt_id, paths: [str]}]`, oldest attempt first. A job qualifies when its NEWEST attempt is `failed` with reason `unanswerable_required` / `owner_fact_missing` / `consent_refused` (same three as `retry_candidates`), it has no `submitted` / `submitted_unconfirmed` attempt, and its sightings join a `questions.target_path` that is non-empty and whose reason is not `assumed`; `paths` = those distinct target paths in sighting order. It does NOT look at the profile: W1 compares `paths` with the stored profile for `ready_to_retry`, `details_needed` and `needed_by`. A `document_missing` stop captures no row, so it is never listed (as the design says).
- **Departure from B.5**: none in names. Added no extra method: `answers()` and `attempt()` already give the What was sent data. `mark_answer_corrected` also reopens-to-answered a dismissed row (the owner answered it); say so if you want dismissed rows left alone.
- **For the tester**: three pins assert the old number and are stale by design: `test_apply_queue_store::test_a_v5_database_opens_at_the_current_version...` (`SCHEMA_VERSION == 8`), `test_store_always::test_the_schema_version_is_8`, and `test_store_always::test_a_v6_database_opens_...` (expects version "8" and compares every apply_answers row, which now also has `field_class` = NULL: pop it like `assumed`). Pin (20-a): v8 -> v9, and v6 -> v9 in one open, counts equal, second open no change; plus the four methods above.
STATUS: open

### 2026-11-15 software-engineer → coordinator, tester
TYPE: handoff
RE: Q-126 WP-U1 (D-214, D-217; design §4.1, §4.5, §8, §9); `web/src/{connections.js,connections_api.js}`, `web/src/components/{ApplyQueuePanel,ConnectionsHub,ResumeRefresh}.vue`
BODY: Branch `eng/Q-126-U1` (from `int/Q-126` 103cc21). Web only; no bundle built.
- ApplyQueuePanel: `normalise` keeps `model` only for state `starting`|`loading`. With no `now` and no stored pause, the "Now applying" slot (inside the existing aria-live region) holds one `li.aq-model[data-state]`: title "Starting the local AI model…" or "Loading the model…" (+ `.aq-model-note` "The first load can take a minute." when loading), elapsed time from `model.since` (aria-hidden, ticks once a second), no Stop button, no banner (no pause is stored then). Header Pause stays available. Polling and `empty` unchanged apart from `model` counting as activity. Paused text/hint are shown as the server sends them.
- connections.js: `startsItself(facts)` (autostart && autostart_here && installed === true). Model card line: an attention card with `message` shows it (any code); code `unreachable`: installed false -> "Ollama isn't installed. Install it from ollama.com to use the local AI."; startsItself -> "Ollama isn't running. JobScraper starts it when a task needs it."; else today's copy. `needsAttention`: unreachable + startsItself follows the server `state` (need_now ignored). New COPY keys `autostartLabel/Desc/NotHere/NoRoute`.
- connections_api.js: `putModelAutostart(enabled)` -> PUT `connections/model/autostart {enabled}`; null when the route is absent (404/405).
- ConnectionsHub: the switch row (reuses `SettingRow` kind switch + `useRows`) in the Local AI card body, above "Check again"; label/description as §4.5; saves on change, not optimistic, "Saved" flash, "Couldn't save: …" via `saveErrorText`, reloads the hub after a good save; `autostart_here === false` -> static row (value On/Off, description + " Not in this copy of JobScraper."); hidden when `facts.autostart` is not a boolean (older server) or `facts.local === false`.
- ResumeRefresh: unavailable line now "Couldn't compare with your application details: the local AI model couldn't start. See Settings → Accounts → Local AI."
FOR TESTER (vitest pins): panel starting and loading rows (copy, elapsed, no Stop, no `.aq-paused`, `aria-live`); a pause during the wait shows the banner with Resume and no model row; model pause text+hint rendered as given; card lines (neutral autostart line, not installed, attention with `message`); `attentionCount` 0 for unreachable+startsItself+need_now true, 1 when state attention; switch saves (PUT body `{enabled}`), flash, 422 error words and the unchanged value, static row with the suffix, hidden without the fact. Verified by a throwaway test (deleted): all of these passed.
RED NOW: `web/tests/q44-refresh.test.js` "says the model is not running when the profile job logged the skip line" (lines 370-372; designed retarget: new wording, no "Start Ollama" / "Re-read resume"). `web/tests/connections.test.js` is NOT red (its fixtures have no autostart facts, so today's copy still applies); add pins for the new facts. Vitest in web: 1070 passed, 1 failed, 1 skipped (1072).
DEPARTURES: (a) a stored pause beats the starting row (design §4.1 says no banner while starting, but an owner Pause pressed during the wait must keep its Resume button). (b) The auto-apply click's `lifted` reply is not shown: nothing in §4 asks for it and the click is the Inbox's. (c) `tabs/Profile.vue:98` still matches the backend log line "application details check skipped: the AI model isn't running" (not my file; the line itself is the backend's). (d) The not-installed card line applies whenever `installed === false`, regardless of the switch.

RE: Q-126 WP-E2 (D-214, D-217); `src/jobscraper/apply_flow.py`, `cli.py`, `pipeline.py`, `config/config.yaml`; ollama_autostart_design §3, §4, §5, §10
BODY: Branch `eng/Q-126-E2` (from `int/Q-126`, which was merged in again for the tester's pins). What each call site does:
- Worker (`apply_flow._Run`): new methods `real_model`, `model_pause(r)`, `ensure_model(load=True)`, `model_retry_wanted`, `model_retry`, `clear_model_state`; new fields at the end of `__init__` (`model_autostart`, `model_ensurer`, `model_pid_alive`, `model_retried`, `model_retries`). `precheck` (fill == llm AND a real `OllamaBackend`) calls the service with `load=True`, the llm_fill keep-alive, `should_stop`, `on_state` -> `store.queue_model_state`; `cancelled` -> `(APP_CLOSED, None)`; not ready -> `MODEL_PAUSE_BY_CODE[code]` with `r.detail` (None when empty). A fake backend keeps the old `available` rule. `run_queue` re-checks `should_stop` right after the precheck and before `queue_take` (C1). After an item whose `pause_for` is `MODEL_UNAVAILABLE` with a `failed` row, no `submit_clicked_at`, real backend: `model_retry` runs the service, re-checks `should_stop`, then retries once per job and twice per worker run (note exactly "the local AI model restarted"; a repeat or a third -> `model_not_answering`; not ready -> mapped pause; stop -> `app_closed`); the role is returned in every case. No timer or tick anywhere. The model state is cleared at worker start, after each service call that set it, and in the `finally`. `pause()` formats `{detail}` in the hint too (try/except). Module `run_queue(..., model_autostart=False, model_ensurer=None, model_pid_alive=None)`. The ensurer gets `pid_alive` only when one was given.
- `cli.py`: helpers `_pid_alive()`, `_model_autostart(cfg, args, store=None)` (process rule AND the owner's switch; opens the DB only when the rule allows), `_ensure_model(cfg, store, args, backend=None)` (silent unless Ollama was started or the model is not ready; only when starting is allowed here), `_doctor_autostart_line(cfg, args=None, store=None)` (the whole line `autostart      ...`, as the tester's pin expects: on (found: default install location | PATH | configured location), on but not installed, off (Settings → Local AI), off in this copy (JOBSCRAPER_DB, worktree or test), off when the model is off in config). Calls: `cmd_run` (passes `model_autostart`, `model_pid_alive` to `pipeline.run`), `cmd_apply` (fill == llm, with the backend), `apply queue` (`model_autostart`, `model_pid_alive`), `cmd_tailor`, `titles suggest`, `cmd_profile` (unless `--show`/`--bump`, or with `--plan-applicant`), `applicant refresh-resume`; `cmd_doctor` prints the autostart line after the model block.
- `pipeline.py`: `run(model_autostart=False, model_pid_alive=None)` -> `_funnel` -> `_bring_model_up`, called only when postings are waiting (no cached decision from this model, not parked), the model is on, the backend is a real Ollama and its probe says unavailable; a failure only changes the words of the existing skip line ("decide: skipped (Ollama isn't installed); N postings wait for a model"); never raises.
- `config/config.yaml`: `budget.ollama_autostart: true` and a commented `# ollama_path:` (nothing else).
Departures from the design: (1) the post-pre-take `should_stop` re-check is a separate `if` after the precheck block, not only after a `ready` result (same effect). (2) `model_unavailable` pause detail is `r.detail or None` (the pin wants `detail == "d"` for every code). None otherwise.
Evidence (JOBSCRAPER_OLLAMA_AUTOSTART=0 throughout): `-k q126` 89/100 (every red is in `test_q126_web.py`, the W1 lane), `-k q126_worker` 14/14, `-k apply_queue` 47/47, `-k layering` 13/13, `-k cli` 109/109, `-k pipeline` 28/28, `-k apply_flow` 149/149 and `-k apply_e2e` 13/13 (each alone, before the W1 merge). The full suite was NOT completed: it ran >15 min (35 of 129 files) with several other sessions' suites running; I stopped it. Please run it on the integration tree.
`--config` gap (Coordinator, W1): `--config` did NOT set `JOBSCRAPER_CONFIG` (it only passes the path to `load_config`; the web's children get `--config` through `cli_command`). `cmd_web` now sets `os.environ["JOBSCRAPER_CONFIG"] = args.config` right after loading the config, before the app, the mail timer and the queue worker are created, so the card's `autostart_here` and `autostart_allowed_here` see a dev setup. Only `cmd_web` (an in-process test of another verb would leak the variable); the `apply queue` worker already gets `--config` from the web and `_model_autostart` passes `config_given`. Seam check: patch `uvicorn.run` and `web.api.create_app`, call `cli.main(["--config", f, "web"])`: the variable is set when `create_app` runs and `autostart_allowed_here(config_given=False)` is then False; with no `--config` it stays unset. FOR TESTER: pin that (restore the variable after).
REVIEW M1 FIXED (Q-126 code review): `_applicant_refresh(cfg, save, args=None)` now gets `args` from `cmd_applicant`; `_ensure_model` never raises. The `except Exception` that turned the NameError into "the resume could not be read" is in `cli.py` `_applicant_refresh` (~line 927); left as it is. An AST scan finds no other function in `cli.py` that uses `args` without receiving it. Seam-driven calls (fakes, `JOBSCRAPER_OLLAMA_AUTOSTART=0`, a temp DB, downstream stages patched to raise a sentinel): `profile`, `tailor`, `titles suggest`, `applicant refresh-resume`, `apply --dry-run <unknown id>`, `apply queue`, `run`, `doctor` all reach their next stage with no NameError. L5 closed: `cmd_apply` clears the model state row at its start. M2: `cmd_web` sets `JOBSCRAPER_CONFIG` only when `--config` was given. TESTER: a pin that runs `applicant refresh-resume` with `_ensure_model` patched would have caught M1; the broad except can hide any later NameError there.
FOR TESTER: T18 (pipeline: the service only when postings wait) is not pinned yet beyond the signature default; a pin can call `pipeline._bring_model_up` with fakes or patch `backends.ensure_model_running`. A `cmd_*` pin can patch `cli._model_autostart`.
STATUS: open

### 2026-11-15 software-engineer → coordinator, U1 (Vue), tester
TYPE: handoff
RE: Q-126 WP-W1 (D-214, D-217; design §4.1, §4.4, §4.5, §5); `src/jobscraper/web/{applyqueue.py,connections.py}`, `web/routers/{apply.py,connections.py}`
BODY: Branch `eng/Q-126-W1` (from `int/Q-126`, merged again at 2143763). No schema change, no new GET route (nothing to add to `SWEEP_GET_ALLOWLIST`). PAYLOADS (all additive):
1. `GET /api/apply/queue` gains `model: null | {state: "starting"|"loading", since: <utc ts>}`. Null unless `slot == "apply"` (a row left by a dead worker never shows) and a worker has written the state. No copy in the payload: U1 owns "Starting the local AI model…" / "Loading the model…" / "The first load can take a minute." `GET /api/apply/status` is UNCHANGED (design names only the queue view; its exact-keys pin stays green).
2. Paused copy: `paused: {reason, text, hint, since}` now has `{detail}` filled in BOTH text and hint (`web/applyqueue.fill_detail`; a template that won't format is shown raw, never raises). The seven model reasons are the contract's `MODEL_PAUSES`.
3. `POST /api/apply` (Auto-apply click) response gains `lifted: null | "<the model reason it cleared>"`. It lifts a stored pause only when its reason is in `MODEL_CLICK_RESUME`, the runner is enabled and the restart gate is open: one `store.queue_resume_if({values}, now, keep_window=MODEL_PAUSE_VALUES)` after the queue rows are committed, before `kick()`. `resumed_at` is never written for a model pause. `POST /api/questions/retry` passes `lift_model_pause=False` (default) and lifts nothing. The route never waits for the model.
4. `POST /api/apply/queue/resume`: `queue_resume_if(None, now, keep_window=MODEL_PAUSE_VALUES)`; if that returns None (nothing paused) it falls back to `queue_resume(now)` (today's stamp, Coordinator ruling). A model pause is cleared with `resumed_at` untouched; any other pause cleared and stamped as before. Start-up hook: `queue_pause_if_waiting(RESTARTED, now, replace=MODEL_PAUSE_VALUES)`.
5. Local AI card (`GET /api/connections`, item id `model`): `facts` gains `autostart: bool` (settings row over config over default True), `autostart_here: bool` (`backends.autostart_allowed_here(db_path=cfg.db_path)`, read through the module attribute; false in a dev copy/worktree/test), `installed: true|false|null` (from the probe, null = not asked or remote). Rule (C9): code `unreachable` + `autostart` + `autostart_here` + `installed is True` -> `state: "neutral"` (no attention count) while no model pause is stored; with a stored model pause -> `state: "attention"`, `code` unchanged, `message` = pause text + " " + hint (detail filled in). Every other case is as before (attention only when `need_now`). `need_reason` rule (c): a `model_unavailable` attempt stops counting once a later-finished attempt is in `ready_to_submit`/`submitted`/`submitted_unconfirmed`. `compose(..., autostart_here=None)` gained a last optional argument (a test passes a bool).
6. `PUT /api/connections/model/autostart` body `{enabled: bool}` (a real JSON boolean only; extra keys ignored). 200 `{enabled: bool, default: bool}` (`default` = config.yaml alone); 422 `{detail: "invalid", errors: [{field: "enabled", message: "Start the local AI model must be true or false"}]}`; changes nothing on 422. Writes `settings` key `budget.ollama_autostart` = "true"/"false". Cross-site writes: 403 by the existing guard. The program's path is not settable from the web, and nothing under `web/` mentions it or calls `ensure_model_running`.
DEPARTURES: (a) `running_from_worktree` is imported from `backends` into `applyqueue.py`; `_REPO_ROOT` stays there and `apply_queue_wanted` passes it as `source_dir`, because `tests/test_apply_queue_cli.py` swaps `applyqueue._REPO_ROOT`. (b) `config_given` (a `--config` web start) is not visible to the web: the card's `autostart_here` passes False for it; the worker CLI still applies its own check, so a `--config` instance may show a neutral card but never starts anything. A one-line `app.state.config_given` in `api.py` would close it (not my file). (c) `lifted` key in the POST body, and no lift when every id was refused (nothing queued). (d) `model_pause` is not a card fact (kept the fact set to the three names the pins expect).
FOR TESTER: stale pin (red by design): `test_connections::test_connections_model_facts_are_fixed_words_and_numbers_only` asserts the six old fact keys; add `autostart`, `autostart_here`, `installed`. `-k q126_web` 18/18, `-k apply_queue_web` 17/17 (after the retarget on int/Q-126).
STATUS: open

### 2026-11-15 software-engineer → coordinator, E2 / W1 engineers, tester
TYPE: handoff
RE: Q-126 WP-E1 (D-214, D-217); `src/jobscraper/backends.py`, `src/jobscraper/store.py`; ollama_autostart_design §2, §4.4, §5, §6, §10
BODY: Branch `eng/Q-126-E1` (from `int/Q-126`), commits 944d2e2 (store), 066e43c (backends). Signatures AS BUILT (all in the new block at the end of `backends.py` unless noted):
- `ensure_model_running(budget, *, autostart, backend=None, load=False, keep_alive=None, on_state=None, should_stop=None, start_wait_s=60.0, load_wait_s=330.0, spawn=None, find=None, http=None, pid_alive=None, clock=time.monotonic, sleep=time.sleep, lock_dir=None, wall=time.time) -> ModelReady`. `autostart` has no default. `on_state(s)` hears "starting" then "loading". `spawn(argv, env)` is called with `[exe, "serve"]` and an env copy that adds only `OLLAMA_HOST`; it must raise OSError to fail. `find(budget) -> str|None`. `http` is a client with `.get(url, timeout=)`. `wall` (not in the design) is the wall clock for the lock file's age (mtime), so the 90 s / 2 s lock rules are testable with `os.utime`; `clock` stays the monotonic seam for waits.
- `ModelReady(code, started=False, detail="", seconds=0.0)`, frozen, `.ok`. Codes: ready, off, not_running, not_installed (detail = model tag), start_failed (detail "the program wouldn't start" or "no answer after 60 seconds"), port_taken (detail = port), not_answering (detail ""), model_missing (detail = tag), load_failed (detail = tag), cancelled. Also `not_answering` when `/api/tags` itself can't be read after the server answered `/api/version`. `MODEL_PAUSE_BY_CODE` covers each of these but ready/cancelled.
- `find_ollama(budget, env=None(os.environ), *, cwd=None, checkout=None) -> str|None`; `autostart_allowed_here(*, db_path, env=None, config_given=False, source_dir=None) -> bool`; `autostart_switch(budget, get=None) -> bool` (`get(key)` returns the stored settings string or None); `running_from_worktree(source_dir=None)` (a COPY of `web/applyqueue.py`'s function; W1 should make applyqueue import it from backends and drop its own); `OllamaBackend.reset_probe()`; `_spawn_detached(argv, env)`; `_LAUNCHER`.
- `warm(backend, keep_alive=None, *, timeout=None)`, `Backend.warm` and `OllamaBackend.warm(keep_alive=None, *, timeout=None)`: `timeout` keyword-only; the module `warm` passes it only when given (a fake whose `warm(keep_alive)` takes no timeout still works).
- `Probe` gains `installed: bool | None = None` (last field, defaulted; filled for a local Ollama via `find_ollama(budget)`). `probe(budget)` is otherwise unchanged.
- store: `queue_model_state(state|None, now)`, `queue_model_view() -> {state, since}|None`, `queue_resume_if(reasons|None, now, *, keep_window=()) -> cleared reason|None` (a model reason in `keep_window` is cleared WITHOUT writing `resumed_at`), `queue_pause_if_waiting(reason, now, *, replace=())`. Settings keys `apply_queue.model` / `apply_queue.model_at`. `queue_resume` and `queue_pause` unchanged; no schema change.
DEPARTURES / things to know:
1. `queue_resume_if(None, ...)` with nothing paused returns None and writes nothing (design §5 wording). Today's `queue_resume` stamps `resumed_at` even when not paused. W1: if the Resume route must still start a fresh streak window when the queue is not paused, call `queue_resume` in that case.
2. `reset_probe()` is called as soon as the server answers `/api/version`, i.e. before the model check, so a stale cached failure is dropped even when the result is `model_missing` (design step 6 says after the check). Harmless superset.
3. The lock-file `unavailable` case (temp dir not writable) goes on without a lock rather than not start.
4. `_spawn_detached` refuses on `JOBSCRAPER_OLLAMA_AUTOSTART=0`, a loaded `run_tests`/`pytest`, and, when the variable is unset, a `JOBSCRAPER_DB` copy / `JOBSCRAPER_CONFIG` / a worktree (via `autostart_allowed_here`); `=1` is the one-line override. The `--config` flag can't be seen from the library: cli passes `config_given` to `autostart_allowed_here` itself and then `autostart=` to the service.
5. Wait polls use a 2 s cap per try (`/api/version` in the start wait), so `should_stop` is polled at least every ~2 s. The pre-start ping uses the 5 s cap, 3 tries, 1 s apart.
5b. Later commits (983b5fd, e53bd41; `int/Q-126` merged in): the load wait polls through the `sleep` seam (every 0.5 s while the thread lives); a lock file with no readable PID is stale after 2 s by its mtime OR after the waiter has watched it unreadable for 2 s on `clock` (pin: unreadable-PID lock under a fake clock); `_spawn_detached` also refuses when `Path(sys.argv[0]).name` is `run_tests.py`/`pytest` (C4b). `-k q126` 67/67.
6. `find_ollama` expects the config file's budget: pass the raw `cfg.budget`, never one overlaid with settings rows (rule 2: a stored `budget.ollama_path` must be ignored).
FOR TESTER: `tests/test_backends_probe.py:299` pins the Probe field set `{"ok","code","backend","model","local"}`; it is red by design now (adds `installed`): retarget. `-k apply_contracts` 71/72 (the known "doctor" hint pin). Seam pins T1-T10 can use `http` = a fake with `.get(url, timeout=)` raising `httpx.ConnectError` / `httpx.ReadTimeout`, or returning an object with `status_code` and `.json()`.
FOR E2: `cli.py` passes `autostart=autostart_allowed_here(db_path=..., config_given=...) and autostart_switch(cfg.budget, store.get_setting)`, `pid_alive=web.jobs.pid_alive` (lazy import), `should_stop`, `on_state` writing `store.queue_model_state(s, now)`, and `load=True, keep_alive=llm_fill.keep_alive` on the worker path. `ModelReady.detail` never holds a path or URL.
STATUS: open

### 2026-11-12 software-engineer → coordinator, tester, W1 engineer
TYPE: handoff
RE: Q-120 WP-E2 (D-205; F-30, F-37, F-38, NF-05, NF-06, D-200, D-203); `src/jobscraper/apply_flow.py`, `src/jobscraper/apply/browser.py`, `src/jobscraper/cli.py`; apply_queue_design §2 I5, §3.2, §3.3, §4, §5, §6; review H4, M3, M4, M5, L1, L2
BODY: Branch `eng/Q-120-E2-worker` (from eng/Q-120-E1-store + a merge of `personal` for contract v6). Commits 4e6a25b (worker), e2bb233 (CLI).
- Public: `apply_flow.run_queue(cfg, store, *, backend, apply_cfg_fn, http=None, browser_factory=None, say=print, evidence_dir=None, now=None, should_stop=None, force_dry_run=False) -> QueueOutcome` (`results, lock_refused, yielded, paused, waiting`); `pick_mode(item_mode, effective_mode, force_dry_run) -> str` ("auto" only for exactly "auto"/"auto"/no --dry-run); `pause_for(row, last_cap, streak, *, browser_failed="") -> (QueuePause, detail|None) | None`; constants `DRY_RUN_TABS=3`, `HOLD_SLICE_S=5`, `CONTENT_FAILURE_MARKERS`, `TRANSPORT_FAILURE_MARKERS`. `_Run`: `attempt(job_id, *, attempt_id=None)`, `set_mode`, `begin_item`, `unstarted`, `precheck`, `hold`, `keep_tabs`, `run_queue`, `window_start`; `cap_refusal` sets `last_cap`; `browser()` reopens a closed window (queue only) and sets `browser_failed` on a launch error. `BrowserSession.hold(s) -> bool`, `alive() -> bool` (context "close" event). `run(job_ids)` unchanged in behaviour.
- Pause table as built (top to bottom; returned = RETURNS_ITEM and row `failed` and `submit_clicked_at` null): profile_incomplete -> profile_incomplete, returned · detail with "no usable answers" / "would be guesses" / "last resort" (any reason code, failed row) -> fill_failed, NOT returned (Coordinator 2026-11-12 overrides §4.1's model_unavailable row) · reason model_unavailable or a failed row whose detail has "model unavailable" -> model_unavailable, returned · launch error -> browser_unavailable, returned · cap_reached + daily -> daily_limit, returned · streak 2 captcha/bot_challenge (incl. "captcha/bot check after submit") -> bot_checks · 3 failed `error` -> repeated_errors · 3 failed same reason (not duplicate, posting_closed, cap_reached, cancelled, unsupported_ats, error) -> repeated_failures, detail = the code in words ("login failed") · everything else continues, incl. a single-question `unanswerable_required` (Coordinator 2026-11-12 overrides §4.1's fill_failed row). Pre-take pauses (only while a role waits): profile_incomplete (detail = missing paths), model_unavailable (detail = backend `unavailable_code`), no attempt row. Extra fail-closed: a worker exception pauses worker_failed, a cancel/Ctrl+C pauses cancelled (first reason wins, so the web's own pause stays); a role interrupted while its row is still `queued` ends failed(cancelled, "interrupted before start") and goes back to the front.
- For W1: the JobManager argv is `python -m jobscraper [--config C] apply queue --parent-pid <os.getpid()>` (`--dry-run` optional). Exit codes: 0 every orderly end (drained, paused, yielded, item failures), 3 RunLock held (-> busy_elsewhere), 2 bad arguments (`queue` with ids, `--parent-pid` <= 0 or with `apply <ids>`), 143 cancelled; other = crash. Log lines: per item `queue: job=<job_id> attempt=<n> mode=<auto|dry_run> -> <state>[ reason=<code>]`; `queue: job=<id> no longer To apply; skipped`; `queue: job=<id> is back at the front`; `queue: paused reason=<reason>[ (already paused)] <text> <hint>`; `queue: paused reason=<r>; nothing taken`; `queue: a search run goes first; the queue continues after it`; `queue: closed the oldest dry-run tab (at most 3 stay open)`; summary `queue: N finished, M waiting, paused: <reason>|no`; lock: `queue: another apply run holds the browser; nothing was taken`. The attempt lines are the same as `apply <ids>` (redacted).
- Verified with fakes only (temp DBs, FakeBrowserFactory, FakeAdapter, stdin scripts): 2 auto roles -> live attempts at each open [1, 1], both submitted; H4 dry->auto, auto->dry_run, --dry-run: all `dry_run`, `ready_to_submit`, 0 submit calls; paused -> 0 rows, 0 browsers; NoModel + fill llm -> paused model_unavailable, 0 rows; launch error -> row failed, item back at position 1 `returned: browser_unavailable`, pause detail "the JobScraper Edge profile is in use", 2nd not taken; applied twin -> duplicate, next ready_to_submit, no pause; 3 single-question unanswerable -> repeated_failures "unanswerable required"; 3 errors -> repeated_errors; yield -> 0 rows, no pause, flag cleared; yield mid-item -> item 1 done, 1 waiting; parent gone -> app_closed after item 1; 5 dry runs -> 3 tabs open, 2 closed, an item added during the hold ran in the same session; daily_cap 1 -> daily_limit "1 of 1 today", returned; cancel before start -> failed(cancelled) + returned + paused cancelled; batch `run([j, j])` -> ready_to_submit + duplicate "the same job twice in this batch". CLI on a temp config: exit 2/2/2/3/0 (dead parent -> app_closed)/0 (2 finished).
- cmd_web (1e9a7b7, H2): passes `apply_queue_worker=apply_queue_wanted(os.environ)` to `create_app`, imported inside `cmd_web` from `web.applyqueue` (W1); without that module (this branch alone) no keyword is passed. Exercisable only after E2 and W1 are merged.
- Tests (run one at a time): `-k apply_flow` 148/148, `-k apply_e2e` 13/13, `-k layering` 13/13, `-k q114` 3/3, `-k apply_cli` 23/23, `-k test_cli` 15/15. No stale pins.
- Known limits: `alive()` relies on Playwright's context "close" event (not exercised with a real Edge here: no real browser was run). The backend's availability probe is cached per process (OllamaBackend), so the pre-take model check is per worker run, not per item. The content-failure markers are string matches on `reason_detail`; Q-121 should emit one of them (or a new code) so `pause_for` sees it.
REQUEST (tester): pins P1, P6, P8-P10b, P14, P15, P20 from §8 plus the Coordinator's three cases (content -> fill_failed not returned; transport -> model_unavailable returned; single fact -> continues). FakeSession needs no change (`hold() -> None` counts as open; no `alive` counts as alive).
STATUS: open

### 2026-11-12 software-engineer → coordinator, tester, W1/E2 engineers
TYPE: handoff
RE: Q-120 WP-E1 (D-205; F-30, NF-05, NF-06, D-200); `src/jobscraper/store.py`; apply_queue_design §2, §3.1, §4; review H3, M3, M4, L3, L6
BODY: Branch `eng/Q-120-E1-store`, commit c46b12d (store.py only). SCHEMA_VERSION 6: `apply_queue` table + `idx_apply_queue_pos` exactly as §2, appended to `SCHEMA`; `_migrate` unchanged (its upsert records 6). A v5 DB opens, gains the table, keeps its rows.
Methods (positions 1-based, `(pos, id)` order; pause reasons and modes are strings, enums accepted via `_state`):
- `queue_add(job_id, company_id, mode, source, now) -> Optional[int]` NO commit. Position, or None when already waiting. Also None while the job has a live attempt (`queued` included): an I2 guard inside the same INSERT, for a `queue_take` that commits between the helper's `in_progress` check and this write. A mode/source outside the CHECK raises IntegrityError.
- `queue_return(job_id, company_id, mode, note, now) -> None` commits. Front (`MIN(pos)-1`), source `returned`; `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` (L6: a fresh click's row wins, untouched).
- `queue_items() -> list[{job_id, company, role, mode, position, enqueued_at, source, note}]`; `queue_positions() -> dict[str, int]`; `queue_remove(job_id) -> bool` commits; `queue_clear() -> int` commits. company/role = `COALESCE(applications.company/role, companies.name/jobs.title)` like `applications()`.
- `queue_take(now) -> (Optional[{job_id, attempt_id, item_mode, source}], dropped: list[str])` commits. One `begin_immediate()` (BEGIN IMMEDIATE takes the write lock before the first read; a second process waits on the 5 s busy timeout, then sees the row gone; rollback on any exception). Paused or yield set → `(None, [])`, nothing consumed. Heads with no application row or a status other than `to_apply` are deleted and listed in `dropped`. The head is deleted and `create_attempt(..., mode="dry_run")` is created (fail-safe; E2 sets the `pick_mode` result with `update_attempt(mode=...)`).
- `delete_application(job_id)` (H3): also `DELETE FROM apply_queue` in the same commit.
- `queue_pause(reason, detail, now) -> bool` commits; only when not paused (first reason wins, L3). `queue_pause_if_waiting(reason, now) -> bool` commits; only when a row waits and not paused. `queue_resume(now) -> None` commits; deletes `apply_queue.paused/paused_detail/paused_at`, sets `apply_queue.resumed_at`. `queue_pause_state() -> Optional[{reason, detail, since}]`.
- `queue_request_yield() -> None` (sets `apply_queue.yield`=1), `queue_take_yield() -> bool` (read + clear in one tx), `queue_clear_yield() -> None`; all commit.
- `live_attempt(job_id=None) -> Optional[dict]`: newest non-terminal attempt incl. `queued`: the apply_attempts row (`id`, not `attempt_id`) + `company`, `role`.
- `recent_attempts(limit=10) -> list[dict]`: terminal attempts, newest `finished_at` first, row + `company`, `role`.
- `queue_streak(since, limit=3) -> list[{state, reason_code, reason_detail, submit_clicked_at}]`: terminal attempts with `COALESCE(started_at, queued_at) >= since`, newest finished first.
- Constants on `Store`: `QUEUE_PAUSED`, `QUEUE_PAUSED_DETAIL`, `QUEUE_PAUSED_AT`, `QUEUE_RESUMED_AT`, `QUEUE_YIELD` (the `apply_queue.*` keys). "Cleared" = the settings row deleted (new private `_setting_put`/`_setting_del`; `set_setting` unchanged).
Evidence: an ad hoc stdin script on temp DBs: add 3 → [1,2,3]; dup → None; remove middle → {j1:1, j3:2}; pause → take (None, []), queue intact; yield → (None, []); take → j1 + attempt queued/dry_run; a second connection blocked while the first held the lock, then got j2 (not the j3 the first took); two threads racing → distinct items; return → front, a conflicting return leaves the waiting row unchanged; no-app / applied heads → dropped ['j1','j2']; v5 DB → version 6, table + index, application row kept. Tests: `-k store` 90/90, `-k web_` 133/133, `-k layering` 13/13, `-k q114` 3/3, `-k apply_flow` 148/148 (run alone).
Stale pins: none (`test_store_fill_cache` asserts `>= 3`, `test_store_mail` `>= 5`).
Choices where §3.1 was silent: `queue_remove` commits; `queue_resume` returns None; `live_attempt`/`recent_attempts` return the full row + names; streak/recent order by `finished_at`, then id; the `queue_add` live-attempt guard (above).
STATUS: open

RE: Q-83 D-171; `src/jobscraper/apply/declarations.py`; `tests/test_apply_declarations_d170.py`

Ad hoc replay of the 59 part-D rows (65 sub-rows incl. both halves of the paired rows): all hold. `-k declarations` 122/122 (incl. the tester's d171 file 35/35), `-k apply_contracts` 66/66, `-k llm_fill` 90/90, `-k layering` 13/13. ### 2026-11-02 software-engineer → coordinator
TYPE: handoff
RE: Q-02 · F-01..F-05, F-07 · cloud port C-1..C-8, C-22
BODY: Ready for review. Branch `eng/Q-02-engine-port` (9 commits on `personal`, one per C-item; `git log --oneline personal..`).
- Done: C-1 missed_runs + close after 2 misses, empty/capped closes nothing (logged); C-2 adapters.py verbatim from 019e975 (no personal-only lines lost) + `scrape.locations`; C-3 decide/backends from 019e975 hunks (p1..pN schema, output cap/done_reason, halve-and-retry, decision_failures parking, outage stop, location-evidence guard, think only if /api/show lists it, optional `budget.decide_deadline_s`); C-5 pid_id identity (legacy record: "-m jobscraper" check once, then adopts the identity); C-4 JobCancelled + run marked failed with stats.reason (rolls back uncommitted writes first), CLI SIGBREAK/SIGTERM handler -> exit 143, web: CREATE_NEW_PROCESS_GROUP + CTRL_BREAK_EVENT, grace, taskkill /T /F, proc.kill() fallback, JobStuck -> 500, record `reason: cancelled` (in the view only when set); C-7 grace min(12 h, 10%) + stamps at run start; C-6 configure_logging, UnhandledErrors {detail, request_id}, GET /api/healthz (new routers/health.py, Store.ping); C-8 detect_block + signature in the error text ("HTTP 403 (cloudflare) for ..."), Companies reason sharpened, `kind` unchanged; C-22 http(s) URL validator, DOCX 5 MB unpacked / BadZipFile checks, parse in threadpool.
- Kept: personal SYSTEM_PROMPT, DEFAULT_INTERESTS, judge.interests, rules.yaml lists, batch-size/cycle settings, company_health_view kind/paused, scheduler.status cadence. No Postgres, no env overrides (R-12), no runs_per_cycle, no gate_candidates.
- Decision cache: SYSTEM_PROMPT text unchanged; only the user prompt shows p1..pN ids. Cache key (job_id, profile_version, vital_hash, model) unchanged -> nothing is re-judged. Only cached rejects with the new REJECT_LOCATION_EVIDENCE reason are re-asked when the allow list later covers them.
- Config: `config/config.yaml` gains `scrape: {locations: [Singapore]}`. Optional new key `budget.decide_deadline_s` (unset = no deadline).
- Evidence: `python tests/run_tests.py` -> 299/323 passed. All 24 failures are intended/contract changes for Q-07:
  (a) 17 x `TypeError: complete() got an unexpected keyword argument 'schema'` - test fakes in test_decide (10), test_pipeline (6), test_m17 (1) need `schema=None, timeout=None` (cloud updated its fakes the same way). With only those signatures widened (scratch copy) the suite is 316/323.
  (b) C-1: test_store::test_failed_fetch_must_not_close_jobs, test_shortlist::test_shortlist_keeps_closed_roles_flagged (one miss no longer closes).
  (c) C-4: test_pipeline::test_pipeline_crash_before_stamp_is_a_no_op_and_is_reaped (asserts a crashed run stays `running`; now `failed`. Its no-stamp and re-batch asserts still hold).
  (d) C-5: test_web_control::test_web_jobs_a_recorded_job_still_running_holds_the_slot (records a `python -c` PID with no pid_id; now correctly treated as not ours).
  (e) C-7: test_scheduler::test_scheduler_nothing_rescraped_inside_the_cycle (13 d 23 h is now inside the 12 h grace), test_scheduler::test_scheduler_all_fresh_reports_next_due_date and test_pipeline::test_pipeline_nothing_due_exits_with_next_due_date (next due is 12 h earlier).
  test_layering 7/7; `import jobscraper.web.api` OK; `doctor` exit 0 and `run --dry-run --batch-size 2` exit 0 on a scratch DB copy (migration added missed_runs + decision_failures; log shows "HTTP 403 (cloudflare)" / "(akamai)"). Cloud's test_net.py 6/6 and test_small_model.py 33/36 against this src (3 = env overrides / MU-4 API, not ported).
  Windows cancel checked by hand: a CLI-style child got CTRL_BREAK, ran its cleanup, exit 143 in 0.1 s; a child that ignores the break was killed after the grace period; the parent console was unaffected. Pipeline: cancel -> run `failed`, reason `cancelled`; crash -> reason `ZeroDivisionError: ...`; nothing stamped.
- Known limits: CTRL_BREAK only reaches the child while Python runs bytecode. A run blocked in a long Ollama call ignores it and is hard-killed after `grace_seconds` (5 s), so the run row stays `running` until per_run_timeout reaps it. A longer grace for `run` jobs would fix that (needs a decision). CTRL_BREAK needs the web server to have a console (run.ps1 gives it one). Without one, the kill path is used. The SPA half of C-4 (Runs/JobStatus wording, `job.reason`, `stats.reason`/`stats.message`) is left to M18. Watch `rejected_by_postcondition` on the first real run (C-3 location-evidence guard).

Checkbox, email, phone, date, url and typeahead fields are never sent to the model. (c) The declaration classifier matches phrases as token runs without the one-token ≤ 4 rule, which the oracles need ("relatives", "convicted" in long sentences). (d) `pay.expected_salary.amount: 0` (the template value) counts as not filled, and a negative amount is an error. (e) An optional field whose tier-5 answer fails grounding becomes blank_optional (with a note), not ungrounded. (f) A required cover letter's Answer.value is the letter text (note "cover letter text"). apply_flow writes `cover_letter.txt` and uploads it. (g) Nationality options also match the demonym ("Singaporean"). The ISO table covers 24 countries.

BODY: Ready for review. Branch `eng/Q-36-lenient-profile`, one file: `src/jobscraper/applicant.py`. New `normalise(profile)` (deep copy, idempotent) canonicalises every ENUMS / LIST_ENUMS value: any case and surrounding spaces, spaces or hyphens as underscores, YAML booleans for yes/no enums (`no` -> "no", `true` -> "yes"), and `pay.*.period` synonyms (month, per month, monthly, mth -> monthly; year, yearly, annual, per annum, pa, p.a. -> annual). `load` returns the normalised profile; `validate` and `save` normalise first (so the form API and the CLI check see the same thing; `save` writes canonical spellings). A value that still does not map is left alone and reported as `path: must be one of: <allowed>` (never the value). Template spellings unchanged: `config/applicant.example.yaml` is a fixed point of `normalise` and validates clean.

- Not done (outside the WP): booleans typed as quoted strings ("yes") for BOOLS, "prefer not to say" -> decline, and a looser `singapore_status.ord_date` (accepts YYYY-MM only).
- Owner file: `applicant check --file data/applicant.yaml` still reports `pay.expected_salary.period` (a string that maps to no period) and `singapore_status.ord_date` (string not in YYYY-MM form). Values not read or printed; the owner can fix both, or tell me which spelling to add.

### 2026-11-02 software-engineer → coordinator
TYPE: handoff
RE: Q-39 (Q-30 review F2, F6, F7) - branch `eng/Q-39-q30-fixes` (from `int/Q-36`)
BODY: Fixes built in `src/jobscraper/applicant.py`, `src/jobscraper/web/routers/applicant.py`, `web/src/applicantForm.js`, `web/src/components/ApplicationDetails.vue`. No tests touched.
- F6: new `applicant.load_with_etag(path) -> (profile, etag)` reads the file bytes once and parses and hashes those bytes (`load` is now `load_with_etag(path)[0]`; `etag(path)` stays). On unreadable bytes it raises `ApplicantError` whose new `.etag` is the hash of the bytes read. `applicant_view` (GET and the PUT reply) and `load_for_write` (PUT and `POST /api/questions/answer`) use it. A file that is not valid UTF-8 is now an `ApplicantError` (was an uncaught UnicodeDecodeError).
- F7: `applicant.save(..., keep_previous=True)` keeps the file it replaces as `applicant.prev.yaml` beside the profile (`applicant.previous_path(path)`, `PREVIOUS_NAME`; matches the gitignored `data/applicant*.yaml`). One rolling copy, written atomically (temp `applicant.prev.<rand>.yaml` then `os.replace`). Not rolled when the new text equals the file (a second identical Save must not push the real previous version out), nor on a first save, nor when validation fails (nothing is written then). Applies to every `save` caller (Profile PUT and the Questions answer). The YAML is now dumped to text before any file is touched.
- F2 (fix chosen: leaf-level merge in the form, not a refetch-only): `reloadKeepingEdits` now calls the pure `mergeEdits(before, draft, fromFile)` in `applicantForm.js`. Only leaves the owner changed (draft vs the profile it was made from) come from the draft; every other leaf, including a profile-gap answer saved by the Questions card, comes from the file. A leaf changed both here and in the file keeps the file's value (the answer just saved wins, and the form shows it). Lists merge per entry (appended entries are added at the end); a list the owner shortened is taken whole. The `extra` loop is gone: the same merge handles it per leaf. Also `removeAnswer`: the confirm now says "Your other unsaved changes will be saved too." when the form is dirty.
TESTER ORACLES (please add; I wrote none):
- F6 (python): `load_with_etag` on absent file -> `({}, "")`; on a valid file `etag == applicant.etag(path)` and the profile matches `load`; on a broken-YAML file raises `ApplicantError` with `.etag == etag(path) != ""`; on non-UTF-8 bytes raises `ApplicantError`. Race oracle: monkeypatch `Path.read_bytes` (or `applicant._etag_of`) to change the file after the first read and assert GET `/api/applicant` returns the etag of the bytes it parsed (the old profile with the old etag), so a PUT with that etag after the file changed is 409 `changed`. Existing GET and PUT oracles in `test_web_applicant.py` should be unchanged.
- F7 (python, extends the nf01 backup-path oracle): after `save` over an existing file, `applicant.prev.yaml` is in the same folder and holds the replaced bytes; a second save with a different profile rolls it (prev == the version before the last change); a save whose output equals the file leaves prev as it was; first save (no file) makes no prev; an invalid profile (422) writes neither the file nor prev; a PUT of a partial profile through `/api/applicant` leaves the full old profile in prev; `Questions answer` also rolls prev; `keep_previous=False` writes none; `git check-ignore data/applicant.prev.yaml` is ignored; nothing is written outside the profile's folder.
- F2 (vitest, `web/tests/ApplicationDetails.test.js`): set up GET 1 with `contact.city="SG"`; type a new city (unsaved); the Questions card answers a profile-gap group with `target_path=contact.address_line1` (mock `POST /api/questions/answer` ok; mock the next GET to return `contact.address_line1="1 Road"` and a new etag); then click Save details and assert the PUT body has `contact.address_line1 == "1 Road"` AND the typed city AND the new etag. Variants: same leaf edited both places -> PUT carries the file's value; an edited `education[0].institution` plus a file-side `education[0].grade`; owner appended an entry; an `extra` answer edited here while a new `extra` answer arrived; `removeAnswer` with other edits shows the "Your other unsaved changes will be saved too." line and sends them. Pure-function oracles for `mergeEdits` are cheap: it is exported from `web/src/applicantForm.js`.
KNOWN LIMITS: the window between `load_for_write`'s read and the `os.replace` in `save` is not closed (a hand edit landing inside it is still overwritten; the prev copy now keeps it). `has_comments` and `file.exists` in the GET are still separate reads (they do not pair with the etag). Pre-existing on `int/Q-36`, not from this work: `tests/test_applicant_form.py::test_a_lowercase_country_key_and_a_non_bool_default_are_errors` fails because Q-36's `normalise` reads the quoted `"yes"` in `requires_sponsorship.default` as true; the oracle should use a non-bool such as `"maybe"`.
STATUS: open

### 2026-11-03 software-engineer → software-engineer (lane R), coordinator
TYPE: handoff
RE: Q-35 WP-M3 `guards.decide` result shape (answers lane R's WP-R3 question); branch `eng/Q-35-M-llmfill` commit e3b6208
BODY: Lane R's proposal adopted as is. Module `jobscraper.apply.guards`:
`@dataclass class Decision: answers: dict[str, Answer]; unknown_required: tuple[str, ...]  # field keys, page order (G10); deletes: tuple[str, ...]  # group ids "<section>#<index>" (E3, E6); adds: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...]  # (site section, source entry id) (E5); resets: tuple[str, ...]  # field keys G9 clears/resets/unticks`.
- A key in `resets` has `Answer.note` exactly one of `guards.G9_NOTES` = "G9: clear" | "G9: reset" | "G9: untick"; `guards.reset_action(answer) -> "clear"|"reset"|"untick"|""`. Status BLANK_OPTIONAL, confidence 0. When the widget's `reset` returns False, R3 turns the row into PREFILLED 0.5 with note `guards.UNVERIFIED_NOTE` ("unverified site value"). A RADIO is already decided that way (no reset).
- Entry fields of a deleted group come back SKIPPED (note "E3: junk entry deleted" / "E6: ungroundable entry deleted") and are not in `unknown_required`.
- Signature: `decide(fields, locks, decisions, facts, rctx, *, matches, second, today, rules=None, sections=(), model="", prompt_hashes=None, cfg=None, blocks=None)`. The pinned call works; please pass `rules=services.rules` (tier-2 relevance and G2(d) label matches; without it those fail closed), `sections=` (delete/add controls; without them no deletes/adds), `model=services.model`, `prompt_hashes=stats.prompt_hashes` (from `llm_fill.CallLog`), `cfg=services.cfg` (`second_opinion`). `decisions` = a list of ModelDecision (a later one for the same key wins: append pick/recheck/corrective results) or a dict key -> decision. A lock is final: decide never reads a decision for a locked key.
- D-84 R3 for WP-R3: `guards.recheck_targets(fields, decisions, matches, rctx, *, blocks) -> list[field key]` (every TEXTAREA of a matched entry whose decision is not a fact, imported or program-added, plus entry title TEXT fields that fail G11); call `llm_fill.recheck_entry(backend, pf, blocks[matches[pf.group]], s_values=...)` for each and append the result before `decide`. `blocks = facts.entry_blocks(rctx)`.

BODY: Commits: M1 c478eef (`apply/facts.py`; also the resolver half of M2: F1/F4 in `resolve.py`, `plan.is_generic`/`qualified_label`), M2 2d814cb (`apply/locks.py`), M3 e3b6208 (`apply/guards.py`) + outbox 993cdcc (Decision shape for lane R), M4 e3c88b0 (`apply/llm_fill.py`, `backends.py` M-block), M5 65060f4 (`questions.capture_page`). - Evidence: full suite 813/813 on e3c88b0; `-k layering` 10/10 at every commit; `-k questions` 44/44 on 65060f4. The WP Verify lines can't run yet: `test/Q-35-llm-fill-oracles` (603814c) has no `test_llm_fill_{facts,locks,guards,model}.py` / `test_questions_variants.py`. Scratch checks (synthetic sample profile, tester's FakeFillBackend): no S value in `facts_text` (101 facts); locks on the wd_a fixture steps; decide G2a/b/c/d, G3 copy + compose, G5 off/disagree (R2), G6, G7, G9 clear, G11 recheck target, G12, E3 delete, E5 add, lock kept over a model decision; ask: fixed prompt order, FACTS byte-identical across calls, cut reply split, malformed re-asked once then BackendError, timeout -> BackendError, keep_alive sent only when set; capture_page reasons and qualified labels.
- Invariants, where enforced: (1) no S value in a prompt: `llm_fill.user_prompt` returns `redact(whole prompt)`, `pick_option`/`recheck_entry` redact their prompts, `schema()` is redacted too; private facts appear only as "private, set/empty" (`facts.facts_text`); `current` withheld per §6.5 (`llm_fill._withhold`). In notes: guard/lock notes are fixed strings naming the guard (no value is ever formatted in). In the cache: `facts.facts_used_hash` is an HMAC keyed by the S values; `field_sig` hashes the shown value. (2) the model can't override a lock: locked keys are not in the schema (`llm_fill._open`), and `guards.decide` uses the lock answer and never reads a decision for that key. (3) fact values are the program's: `guards._fact_field` writes `facts.fact_value(...)` (= `resolve.fit_*`); the model's value only has to agree (G2d). (4) model text only for resume/job/keep after G1, G3/G6, G4, G5, G7, G11, G12 (`guards._model_field`, `_keep_field`). (5) no logging anywhere in facts/locks/guards/llm_fill; `CallLog` keeps prompt hashes only.
- Interface notes for WP-R3 (pagefill): `backends.warm(services.backend, cfg.get("keep_alive"))` before Edge opens; `locks.lock_page(..., page_text=<step text>)` (L1's one-FILE clause needs it; without it a lone "Upload a file" stays unknown); an L1 answer with note "L1: kept, uploaded in this attempt" = don't re-upload, any other L1 FILLED = upload `value`; `facts.check_entries(sections, rctx)` raises PROFILE_INCOMPLETE; `llm_fill.chunks(fields, matches, max_fields, locked=lock_keys)` (`Chunk` has a 4th field `locked=()`); `ask(..., cfg=services.cfg, locked=lock_answers, stats=CallLog())` per chunk; `second` = `lambda pf: llm_fill.second_opinion(backend, pf, chunk_of[key], facts, rctx, step_title=..., s_values=..., cfg=..., stats=...)`; widgets get `functools.partial(llm_fill.pick_option, backend, s_values=..., stats=...)`; `stats.followups` counts pick/second/recheck calls for `max_followups`; `PageOutcome.model_calls/model_seconds` = `stats.calls/seconds`. Give transport-failed fields a note starting "transport" (`questions.TRANSPORT_NOTES`).
- Interface notes for WP-N6a: `questions.capture_page(page_fields, outcome.answers, rctx, rules)`; never called when `outcome.transport_failed`. `capture()` is untouched.
- M4 timing note (one-off probe, synthetic text, local qwen3:14b): a 4997-token prompt took 1258 ms prompt_eval cold, 28 ms when the same prefix was repeated (keep_alive 5m): Ollama reuses the FACTS+RESUME prefix.
KNOWN LIMITS: `debug_dump` is not written by llm_fill (no path there; pagefill can write `user_prompt(...)`, already redacted). `apply.llm_fill.num_ctx` isn't applied (the backend's num_ctx is set when it is built). JOB has company + JD only (no `ResolveContext.job_title` until Q-27; read via getattr). Profile-gap capture for paths with no tier-2 row (contact.city, links) now comes out `no_rule` (see the architect question).
STATUS: open

### 2026-11-03 software-engineer → tester
TYPE: request
RE: Q-35 lane N oracles (`tests/test_workday_nav.py`, `test_greenhouse_nav.py`, `test_store_fill_cache.py`, accounts CLI)
BODY: Names and shapes your oracles can rely on, branch `eng/Q-35-N-workday`:
- Workday pure helpers in `jobscraper.apply.ats.workday`: `job_url(url)`, `entry_choice(offered, setting="auto") -> (WorkdayEntry, origin)` (no dialog -> DIRECT/"site_default"; autofill offered and setting != "manual" -> AUTOFILL/"resume_import"; else APPLY_MANUALLY/"site_default"; never Use My Last Application), `step_from_title(raw)` ("(current) step 4 of 7 Application Questions" -> `questions_4`, step 5 -> `questions_5`; unknown titles `custom_<slug>_<N>`; "" for empty), `NAVIGATOR`/`WorkdayNavigator` (`match_url` forms A and B give the same canonical host; `tenant_from_company` external_id wins). The pure helper is deliberately NOT called `choose_entry`: the D-70 oracle `test_workday_apply_choice_rule` (gated on module-level `workday:choose_entry`, asserts Use My Last Application) is superseded by D-78 and should be retired or rewritten against `entry_choice`.
- Stub marks the navigator writes: `data-jsnav-entry`, `data-jsnav-delete`, `data-jsnav-add` on entry containers and buttons (`entry_sections` returns those selectors). Account form beats the progress bar; `beecatcher` is never filled; `next_step` on a page whose button text is Submit returns `StepResult(is_review=True)` with no click.
- Identity: `jobscraper.apply.accounts.choose_identity(primary, backup, rows, has_password) -> Identity(email, action)` (rules AD §10.5 1-4; lower-case compare; raises `ApplyFailure(LOGIN_FAILED)` with `NO_PASSWORD_DETAIL` / `BOTH_NO_PASSWORD_DETAIL` / `SIGNIN_FAILED_DETAIL`).
- Store: `fill_cache_get(job_id, sigs, prompt_version, model, inputs_hash)`, `fill_cache_put(job_id, rows, now=None) -> int` (rows carry `field_sig, prompt_version, model, inputs_hash, facts_used_hash, decision`), `fill_cache_purge(before) -> int`; `accounts_for_tenant(ats, host) -> {email_lower: status}`; `applied_on_tenant(ats, host) -> bool`; `annotate_answers(attempt_id, {(step, key): note}) -> int` (no commit). Old-shape `fill_cache` (with `facts_hash`) is rebuilt on open.
- Greenhouse: `choose_entry` fills `ctx.pre_import` before `prefill` is called; `step_id` "form"; `precheck` re-raises only `posting_closed`.
- Flow: the account terms check needs `consents.privacy_consent` (else `unanswerable_required` "account terms"); a sign-up field error deletes the vault entry and the account row; any other sign-up exception leaves both with status `unknown`; the mail wait is added back to `ctx.deadline`. Workday attempts currently stop before Edge with `unsupported_ats` (interim, until N6a).

RE: Q-35 lane R, WP-R1 + WP-R2 (D-78; LF §3.1–§3.3 amended; F-34) - branch `eng/Q-35-R-page`
BODY: Built `src/jobscraper/apply/page.py` (R1) and `src/jobscraper/apply/widgets.py` (R2); no other src file touched; `personal` (amendment 06f2d4d) and `test/Q-35-llm-fill-oracles` merged in.
- page.py: `read_page(page, *, scope, sections, origin, known=None, step="", pre_import=None, options=True, rng=None)`, `field_errors`, `entry_sections` (generic: numbered headings = entries, Add/Add Another = section add, Delete/Remove in the entry = delete; CSS locators on `data-llmfill-entry/-add/-del` marks), `page_text`, `scan_required(page, scope)` (R3's page piece, done), `audit_keys(page, fields, step)`, `click` (click_filter fallbacks), `shown_contains(shown, values)` (TR-1/H4 helper), `date_parts`/`date_iso`. Reader marks persist across re-reads, so a re-read after our fills (H7) keeps old keys; new fields get new keys.
- widgets.py: `FAMILIES` registry (detect/options/fill/read/reset) for segmented_date, file, native_select, aria_listbox, prompt, react_select, combobox, radio, checkbox_group, checkbox, text; `fill(page, pf, value, *, pick=None, force=False) -> bool` and `reset(page, pf) -> bool`, plus `fill_field`/`reset_field` -> `Filled(ok, shown, error, note)` for R3's audit (the read-back value, H3). Strict read-back for every family (value equal, normalised by AD §7.1 steps 1-4 and 6, and no site error after blur), a search miss clears the typed text, dates by section role, wrong pills out first, upload ok when the name shows within 30 s, Enter only in a prompt search box outside a form with a submit control (M8), `click` re-exported. `pick(wanted, options) -> option|""` is the injected chooser (llm_fill.pick_option); no model call here.
- Interface notes for lane M / R3: DATE fields carry their sections in `field.options` as `Option(label=<shown name>, value="day"|"month"|"year")` (not choices; use `page.date_parts`); a date's `shown` is the section values joined with "/" in page order ("3/2026"; `page.date_iso` -> "2026-03"); `context` is set on every field (previous label in the same entry group, else the same section); a `pre_import` match needs `step` (audit keys). The checkbox group family is `checkbox_group` (kind MULTI_CHOICE).
- Evidence: `python tests/run_tests.py -k apply_page_reader` -> 10/10 passed; `-k apply_widgets` -> 8/9 (the 1 red is the conflict in the next entry); `-k layering` -> 10/10; `-k apply_contracts` -> 49/49.
KNOWN LIMITS: shim and synthetic pages only (no real Workday page was opened); real widget timing is proven in dry runs (TR-13). Options of a search-driven list are empty with `options_complete=False` (each costs ~3 s on a read: one retry). R3 (`pagefill.py`) not started: see the question below.
STATUS: open

### 2026-11-03 software-engineer → tester
TYPE: question
RE: Q-35 WP-T3 `tests/test_apply_widgets.py::test_a_search_driven_prompt_reads_back_the_intended_option_not_the_first_hit` vs LF §3.2 Enter rule (review M8)
BODY: The oracle fills "Country Phone Code" on `stubs/enter_form.html` and expects True. That prompt is search-driven (`"search": {"enter-prompt": true}`: options only after Enter) and sits inside a `<form>` with `<button type="submit">Send</button>`. LF §3.2 says Enter may be sent only when the prompt input is not inside a form with a submit control, so the widget never presses Enter there, finds no options and fails closed (`search_miss`, typed text cleared, `formSubmits` 0). Proposed fix: move the M14 oracle to a search-driven prompt outside any form (e.g. a second prompt in `enter_form.html` after `</form>`, or `prompt_categories.html` with `search`), and keep this stub for the "no Enter, no submit" oracle (which passes). If the design should allow Enter there (the shim's handler calls preventDefault), that is an architect change to M8.

RE: Q-35 T1/T2 oracles for lane M (`tests/llm_fill_support.py`, `test_llm_fill_{facts,locks,guards,model}.py`)
BODY: (1) False positive in `find_secrets`: the digit check compares against ALL digits of the prompt text concatenated, so the schema's ref enums (`"profile:experience[0]..."` x6, then `"maxLength": 1500`) give "0000001500", which contains the sample postal code "000001". Please match digit runs (e.g. digits of each `\d[\d\s\-.()+]*\d` run) instead of the whole-text stream. (2) Reply shapes lane M uses: pick = `{"choice": "<option>" | ""}`; recheck = flat `{"source": "resume"|"unknown", "ref": "", "value", "evidence"}` (a reply keyed by field id is accepted too); the recheck prompt has `RESUME ENTRY:` + block and a one-view `FIELDS (JSON):` array with `group_title`/`question`, so both `match` and keyed replies work. Entry calls carry `RESUME ENTRY <entry id>:`; corrective = a view with `site_error` (via "corrective"). (3) Names to pin: `guards.Decision(answers, unknown_required, deletes, adds, resets)`, `G9_NOTES`, `reset_action`, `UNVERIFIED_NOTE`, `recheck_targets`, `lines_in_block`; `llm_fill.Chunk(id, entry, fields, locked=())`, `CallLog`; `locks.qualified_labels`; `facts.check_entries`, `other_employers(rctx, composed=)`; `questions.capture_page`, `page_reason`, `TRANSPORT_NOTES`. (4) Fixture notes: wd_a `01-autofill_with_resume` "Upload a file" is the resume slot only with `page_text`; a fact cited on a Yes/No without a tier-2/extra label match (G2d) now needs a scripted `second` reply (D-84 R2).
STATUS: open

RE: Q-35 WP-R3 `pagefill.fill_page` - the `guards.decide` result shape is not pinned (LF §7, §8 pinned-signature table)
BODY: LF §8 pins `guards.decide(...) -> Decision` but not `Decision`'s fields, and `llm_fill.Chunk` is pinned only as a NamedTuple. R3 reads the answers, the stop list and the entry actions from it. Proposal (lane M builds it, R3 reads it): `Decision(answers: dict[str, Answer], unknown_required: tuple[str, ...], deletes: tuple[str, ...]  # group ids (E3, E6), adds: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...]  # (section, source entry id) (E5), resets: tuple[str, ...]  # field keys G9 clears/resets/unticks)`, with `Answer.note` naming the G9 action. A pin in contracts or LF §8 avoids lanes M and R guessing; R3 waits for it (or for lane M's M3).

### 2026-11-03 software-engineer (lane R) → coordinator, tester
TYPE: handoff
RE: Q-35 review fixes F1/F6/F9 + WP-R3 first cut; branch `eng/Q-35-R-page` (957b436 F1/F9, 538b7c5 F6, 57dada2 merge of int/Q-35-NR, adc8f08 R3)
BODY:
- F1 (widgets): no near-match substitution anywhere (`_match` lost its "only option containing" branch); an option is taken only when it equals the decided value (on-screen text, else AD §7.1 normalised, else a unique "Category > Leaf" leaf) or the injected `pick` returned it. `fill_field` rejects any set item that is neither (note `not_decided`) and reads back against the DECIDED value (the picked option only where `pick` chose). `Filled.picked` lists the offered options `pick` returned. Tester's F1 pin passes (apply_widgets 13/13).
- F9: chosen "pick goes through the same rule": the widget never chooses; at fill time only the injected `pick` can, and R3's picker runs `llm_fill.pick_option` then `guards.g4_ok(option, evidence or the program's words)` BEFORE the click (fail -> "" -> the field fails, never a guess). Long lists and fact answers whose words name no option get the pick before `decide` (LL2), so G1/G4/G5 guard it there.
- F6: both. Hardened: `read_page(known=...)` also accepts audit keys (reader key wins, audit key is the fallback for a re-created element); a repeated id inside an entry gets `#<entry index>` (AD §6), not a page ordinal. Documented limit (page.py docstring): ungrouped repeats keep an ordinal; slug(label) keys change with the label; R3 keeps each field's first audit key and records origins under both keys (done in pagefill).
- WP-R3 `apply/pagefill.py`: LF §8 steps 1-13 against lane M's branch API (lazy imports of facts/locks/llm_fill/guards; layering 10/10). With lane M's modules loaded in memory from `eng/Q-35-M-llmfill` (no merge), strict oracles: 4/8 pass (no-time-left, reveal, scan_required, fixtures). The other 4 fail on test/fixture gaps, not pagefill: wd_a/wd_b need a scripted `second` reply for "Phone Device Type" (lane M G5, as lane M's outbox already notes); wd_a 03 needs a recheck reply for "Work Experience 1|Job Title" (lane M's `recheck_targets` adds G11 title rechecks; the fixture's one "Work Experience 1" match is used by it); the upload test reads `ctx.resume_path` after `_fill`'s TemporaryDirectory is gone. With a lenient fake backend the upload and My Experience oracles pass.
- Open design points I decided (please confirm): deadline exhausted -> `transport_failed` with note "transport: timed out" (PageOutcome has no other channel); a fill-time pick that fails in transport sets `transport_failed`; the cache stores the latest fresh decision per field except `second`/`corrective`.
STATUS: open

### 2026-11-03 software-engineer → coordinator
TYPE: question
RE: `test_apply_flow_accounts::test_the_greenhouse_code_pattern_reads_the_code_not_a_word` (tester failing pin); AD §4.2 / §9.1 table `otp_pattern` "pending S-4"
BODY: Greenhouse `otp_pattern = r"\b([A-Za-z0-9]{8})\b"` is the design's value; on "...into the security code field..." `find_code` returns "security". Fixing it picks the code format, which S-4 was to confirm, so I left it. Two one-line options (both pass the pin): (a) a code has a digit: `r"\b(?=[A-Za-z]*\d)([A-Za-z0-9]{8})\b"`; (b) the code is alone on its line: `r"(?m)^\s*([A-Za-z0-9]{8})\s*$"`. Say which (or the architect's choice) and I apply it in `apply/ats/greenhouse.py`.

RE: Q-35 LF §4 L1b, §5.1, §7 G2(a), §8 pinned table, §12.1
BODY: Built with these choices; please confirm or amend. (1) L1b required cover letter: no WP owns the compose call, so it is unknown ("L1b: cover letter not composed"; a FILE, so a stop, not captured). (2) G2(a): `links.*` facts carry `Fact.entry` but are allowed in non-entry chunks (Greenhouse asks LinkedIn/GitHub as single questions). (3) F1 makes every one-word label generic ("City", "Gender": < 2 content tokens), so they are captured and saved as `city | @<step>#<n>`; intended? (4) Empty non-private facts are not listed (§5.1), so the model can't cite them and a gap like an empty `contact.city` is captured `no_rule`, not `profile_gap`. I added a fallback (a model unknown whose unique tier-2 row names an empty form path -> profile_gap); tier-1-only paths need a rule (e.g. list them as "empty"). (5) `guards.decide` takes optional `rules, sections, model, prompt_hashes, cfg, blocks` beyond the pinned signature, and `ask`/`second_opinion`/`pick_option`/`recheck_entry` take optional `cfg`, `locked`, `stats`: pin them in §8? (6) A G12 reject has status UNGROUNDED but §11 maps only G3/G6/G11 to `ungrounded`, so it is captured `no_rule`.
STATUS: open

TYPE: handoff
RE: Q-35 lane M review fixes (`docs/reviews/Q-35-lane-M.md` F1-F9, D-84 R4, D-86) + WP-Q5; branch `eng/Q-35-M-llmfill`
BODY: One commit per finding: F1 4de8d8c, F2 0e830e8, F3 abd0834, F4 74c778a, F5 f852165, F6 0873866, F7 793194a, F8 0602e08, F9 e19f49b; WP-Q5 cb82fec. Full suite 813/813 on cb82fec; `-k layering` 10/10 after each; `-k questions` 44/44, `-k store` 57/57, `-k apply_resolve` 45/45.
PROBES for the tester to pin (scratch, synthetic sample profile + `FakeFillBackend`; not in the repo):
- F1: `pick_option(fb, field "Race", wanted "Chinese", [...])` -> None with 0 calls; same on a neutral label with `sensitive=True`, or with `deny=facts.enum_s_values(profile)` holding "chinese" -> None, 0 calls; a neutral wanted value still makes 1 call. Extend the TR-1 deny list with `facts.enum_s_values(profile)` for pick prompts.
- F2: required "Emergency contact name" / "Referee name" / "Next of kin", decision `resume` with value and quote = the applicant's legal first + last name (present in RESUME) -> UNKNOWN, note "R4: applicant's own detail" (same for a `keep` of that name).

- declarations.py: the B1/B2/B3 phrase tables; `classify_all(label_norm, description_norm="") -> (frozenset[DeclarationLabel], unsure)`; R1 longest match; government/NS rule; `STANDING_DECLARATIONS`; `DISABILITY_VALUE_MAP`; agreeing-AMBIGUOUS rule inside `answer()` (rule_id and source suffix = sorted class values joined by `+`, note `agreed: <classes>`); L7 inside `answer()`; `is_negated` strips NS-exclusion runs; the disability answer comes from `us_self_id.disability_status` (no / yes / decline).
- New public helper for lane M (LF L7, E5 in the architect's entry): `declarations.negated_question(label_norm) -> bool` = the L7 token list (not, never, no, none, neither, free of, without), trailing "(Yes/No)" hint stripped (review F6), NS-exclusion runs removed first. locks.py `_negated` can call it instead of its own NEGATIONS/ANSWER_HINTS. locks.py already hands AMBIGUOUS to `declarations.answer()` (no change needed for L3).

(2) When the government rule ends `unsure`, `government_employment` is removed from the returned classes (classes holds only what is established). (3) An UNSURE phrase without a `government_employment` hit (e.g. "state owned" alone) does not make a question unsure. (4) L7 in `answer()` applies when the bool goes through `fit_bool` onto a non-checkbox field (every class; disability only on a bool-shaped or text field); a disability value placed by `fit_enum` on a real option list is not L7-checked (the option wording carries the meaning). (5) The agreement rule also stops at L7 (a negated multi-class label -> unknown). (6) The disability companion list also has the plurals veterans, genders, races, ethnicities; "N/A" with a slash reads as false too (declaration switches only).
- FLAGS for the tester (tests/ is not mine): (a) `test_apply_contracts::test_sample_applicant_has_exactly_the_design_template_keys` reads `tests/fixtures/apply/applicant.sample.yaml`, not `config/applicant.example.yaml`: the FIXTURE needs `declarations.government_employment: false` (the config example has it and matches AD §5.4 except `contact.backup_email`, which was already missing before this WP). (b) `test_apply_contracts::test_unlabelled_attestation_fails_closed_and_never_reaches_the_model` now fails on its second label "Have you been convicted of an offence or declared bankrupt?": criminal_record + bankruptcy are both standing and both false, so the D-87 agreement rule answers "No" (F19 pins only `classify`, which is unchanged: AMBIGUOUS). Change that oracle to a disagreeing profile (criminal_record true -> unknown) or another label.
- GAP in the spec's R1 (built as written, fails closed; architect to decide): R1 drops a class only when EVERY span it matched sits inside a longer span of another class. A prior/current-employment phrase that merely overlaps a government phrase survives, so these natural wordings stay AMBIGUOUS (unknown, not a wrong answer) instead of the owner's "No": "Have you worked for the government?" (prior's "have you worked" sticks out of "worked for the government"), "Have you previously worked for the government?", "Are you currently employed by the government?", "Do you currently work for the government?", "Have you previously been employed by a government agency?", "Have you worked for any government body in the past?". With "ever" ("Have you ever worked for the Government ...", F3) it works. One-line fix if the architect agrees: in `classify_all`, for `prior_employment_at_company` / `current_employee_at_company` drop a hit when its span OVERLAPS (not only sits inside) a `government_employment` span; F18 ("... worked for us or any government agency") still stays AMBIGUOUS because the spans don't overlap.
- D-89 BUILT (Coordinator decision): `classify_all` drops prior / current employment when every span it matched either sits inside a longer span of another class (R1) or overlaps a `government_employment` span. The six labels above now classify as `government_employment`; "Have you worked for Acme before?" / "...for us" keep `prior_employment_at_company`; F18 stays AMBIGUOUS; "worked for the government, including National Service" stays AMBIGUOUS (unsure); "served in the armed forces" stays None.
- D-89 addendum BUILT: `classify_all` / `classify` take `employer_names=()`; the overlap drop is skipped when the label has the token `us` or `our`, or a run of label tokens that `company_match`es a name in `employer_names` (R1 containment is unchanged). `declarations._agreed` passes `(ctx.employer_name, *ctx.employer_aliases)`. CROSS-LANE EDIT approved by the Coordinator (lane M finished): the two call sites `resolve._tier4` and `locks._declaration_lock` (L3) now pass `(ctx.employer_name, *ctx.employer_aliases)` to `declarations.classify`; nothing else in `resolve.py` or `locks.py` was touched. The other `classify` callers (`resolve` D-70 check, `guards` G7, `questions` capture) pass no names: they only test for an employer-specific class, which a label naming this employer plus a government span cannot produce either way.

The F-36 helpers moved here.

- §8 row 6: when `owner_changed_at` > received and `first_event_at` > received, and rows 7–9 would give a forward move, the result is SUGGEST(`backfilled`, to=target). Otherwise it stays NONE `owner_newer`. That covers backwards, same status, any terminal (no reopen offer), an undo, and an application entered before the mail.
- §7 `name_in_sender` (D-157 i): `match.sender_core(name, suffixes)` drops `match.SENDER_SUFFIX_WORDS` as whole words anywhere in the name, then applies `norm_company`. The name is STRONG only when the result equals a company variant. Extra words make it WEAK, and a role or req id can't lift that to STRONG (the same treatment as a D-154 c restricted name). ### 2026-11-07 software-engineer → coordinator (cc tester, architect)
TYPE: handoff
RE: Q-60 lane F: WP-F1, F2, F5a, F3, F4, F5b; D-104 (a), (g); branch `eng/Q-60-F-flow`
BODY: Commits e16bacc (F1 store), bde5bb9 (F2 config + `assessment`), fc350bf (F5a web runner and `/api/mail`), 818abf7 (F3 flow + config booleans), 6414144 (F3 fix), c99c4f2 (F4 CLI), 56c9c97 (F5b `cmd_web`). int/Q-60-mail-status 5409278 is merged (882fe7d).
Interfaces for the tester:
- `mailstatus_flow.sync(cfg, *, trigger, account_id=None, defer_model=False, store=None, transport_factory=None, backend=None, now=None) -> SyncReport`. `transport_factory(account_row: dict, password: Secret)` defaults to `imapread.ImapTransport(host, port, address, password)`; `backend` to `backends.build(cfg.budget)` (not built under `defer_model` or `model: false`); `now` is a TIME_FMT str or a callable. Trigger not in TRIGGERS → ValueError. Disabled → `SyncReport(None, DISABLED)`; lease held → `(None, BUSY)`.
- `connect(cfg, *, address=None, password: Secret=None, use_application=False, consent_version, store=None, transport_factory=None, now=None) -> (result, account_id)`: result is "ok", "exists" or a SyncCode value; ValueError for a bad address/password/consent. `set_password(cfg, account_id, password: Secret, *, store=None, transport_factory=None, now=None) -> SyncCode` (ValueError: unknown id or empty). Also `candidates(st, rules)`, `own_addresses(cfg, st)`, `first_sync_from(cfg, st, now)`, `summary_line(AccountReport)`.
- CLI: `mail sync [--trigger T] [--account N] [--defer-model]` (exit 0 ok/partial; disabled/busy → stderr line, exit 1); `mail accounts list|add|set-password|on|off|remove` as MS §10.3. `--json` prints one line `{"result": code[, "account_id": N]}`; non-SyncCode results: "exists", "bad_input" (consent missing/old, bad address), "not_found". Exit 0 only for "ok".
- Web children (`web/mailsync.cli_mail_command`): `mail sync --trigger T [--account N] [--defer-model]`; `mail accounts add (--use-application | --address A --password-stdin) --consent V --json`; `mail accounts set-password --id N --password-stdin --json`; password = stdin line. `create_app(..., mail_timer=False, mail_command=None)`; `cmd_web` passes `mail_timer = JOBSCRAPER_MAIL_SYNC != "0"` (default on).
- D-104 (a): `AppCandidate.employer_domains` = `match.employer_domains` on the raw hosts, made registrable (`imapread.registrable_domain`), filtered again; store.py has no platform list. ### 2026-11-07 software-engineer → tester (cc coordinator, architect)
TYPE: request
RE: Q-60 `tests/test_mail_d157_pins.py` d157h / d158 backfilled pins: the setup puts the mail outside the first-sync window
BODY: Both pins fail because the 1 Nov 01:00 rejection is stored `skipped / too_old`, so transition never sees it. `_World(seeded_at="2026-11-02T11:00:00")` connects with every application entered at 2 Nov 11:00, so `connect` sets `first_sync_from` = earliest open application − 1 day = 2026-11-01T11:00:00 (MS §5.3, F-50f; nw-dataeng is `to_apply`, so its first event counts). Fix in the test, no code change: after `_World.__enter__` in those two tests, widen the window as an earlier application would, e.g. `w.st.mail_account_set(w.aid, first_sync_from="2026-09-14T00:00:00")`, before `run(...)`. Checked in-process: with that line both pins pass. (Or seed a third, older open application before `connect`.)
Architect note: this is the real-world limit of D-157 h. A backfill entered before the first connect pulls the window to the entry date, so older mail for it is never read; `backfilled` fires only when the window already reaches back (an older application, or a connect made earlier). Say if the window should instead use `first_sync_max_days` when every application was entered within a day of the connect.
Also: `Reason.INTERNAL` added to `SKIP_REASONS` (609ed31), in the MS §3 layout.

A hit adds the violation `private value` and the answer is UNGROUNDED with `sensitive=True` (so it is captured Private). Text: the one retry is told `private value`, never the value. F11: `declarations._nda_consent` now requires every label token outside the NDA object spans to be in the closed set `_NDA_LABEL_VOCAB` (frame, consent verbs, articles, agreement/obligation words; no "and"/"or", "terms", "check", "relocate"). Profile tri-state: not broken. The schema item has `control: yes_no`, `nullable_bool`; unset shows "Not set" with no radio checked and no Clear; `normalise` keeps absent as absent and null as null; an untouched save sends no `false`. New pin in `web/tests/ApplicationDetails.q71.test.js` (unset/absent, untouched save, Yes/No/Clear).
