## 2026-11-07 handoff: Q-74 pre-merge round (R1, R2, Q-77, D-109, section 4 hooks) on eng/Q-74-model-sees-sensitive
TYPE: handoff
Merges: personal (one conflict, this file: kept both sides), then test/Q-74-pins (0302316, clean).
R1/R2/Q-77 (one matcher, `applicant.protected_terms` / `sensitive_terms` / `terms_hit`; `resolve._protected_hit` and `_shows_s_value` call it):
- Terms: stored text, the Profile form's wording (`applicant.form_wording`, so `pr` also gives "Singapore Permanent Resident"), written forms (`date_forms`, moved from facts; `_SYNONYM_GROUPS`: woman/women for female, Buddhism for buddhist ...). Matching ignores case and separators.
- Modes: "any" (whole word anywhere), "exact" (generic status or gender word: counts only as a whole option label or evidence quote), "evidence" (yes/no/decline/other/none: counts only as the whole evidence quote, never as an option, so a protected `yes` does not block a "Yes" pick). A non-contact term that is also in the non-protected corpus (resume, JD, employer, allow-list) is no hit.
- Q-77 tagging: only "any" terms tag (no generic token), non-contact terms in the plain corpus do not, contact details always do; dates match in all `date_forms`.
- `facts.enum_s_values` is untouched; `facts._form_item` / `_date_forms` now delegate to `applicant` (layering stays green).
D-109: the retry prompt still lists token violations; only stored notes are reduced. `test_f1b_retry_instruction_holds_no_s_value` is out of date and left failing (tester to update).
Section 4 hooks (all keyed on `rctx.model_sees_sensitive`, the effective flag; flag off = unchanged bytes): 1 `facts.fact_catalogue` fills the value of a private leaf on `LLM_ALLOW_SENSITIVE` paths only (extra and standing private facts stay "set"); 2 `facts.facts_text(show_private=)` prints `private: <value>`, `llm_fill.user_prompt` passes the flag; 3 pagefill `run.sv_prompt = []` when on for ask / second / corrective / recheck, `run.sv` kept for G7 and `pick_option`; 4 `field_view`/`_withhold` lifted by the empty list (contact and G7 labels still withheld); 5 `recheck_entry` gets `run.sv_prompt`; 6 `pick_option` keeps `run.sv`; 7 guards `_grounded`: a quote with a protected term that the JD / employer / non-private facts do not hold gives unknown (resume exemption not used: a quote is always in the resume, so it would void the check); only when the flag is on; 8 guards `_model_field`: a value (text or option) with a protected term not in resume / JD / facts gives unknown; 9 `facts.inputs_hash(..., sees_private=)` folds the flag; 10 no writer yet.
Open: no pins exist for the hooks (design's four merge pins a-d); tester to add. Known limit: an employer in `declarations.holdings` that is on the resume but not in profile experience and not the target employer still blocks a llm_fill quote (fails closed).

### 2026-11-07 software-engineer → coordinator (cc tester, reviewer)
TYPE: handoff
RE: eng/Q-35-N6a-flow: WP-N6a (the LLM filling loop in `apply_flow`), KA-1
BODY: Branch `eng/Q-35-N6a-flow` (base personal 893125a). Files: `src/jobscraper/apply_flow.py` (N-block), `src/jobscraper/cli.py` (apply backend gets `llm_fill.num_ctx`). Nothing else: `resolve_ctx` and the llm_fill/pagefill/guards areas are untouched (Q-74 lane).
Built (behind `apply.fill: llm`, the default; `rules` keeps the old path; the dispatch is `_is_navigator(adapter)`, so a pre-D-78 adapter or a test fake takes the old path):
- `_Run.body_llm`: precheck -> warm-up (`backends.warm`, `llm_fill.keep_alive` sent only when set; failure -> `model_unavailable`, step planning, before `browser_factory`) -> open -> `choose_entry` -> `ctx.prefill` -> account wall (`choose_entry` again after an email-check reopen, F7) -> one `fill_page` per step -> review. Same states, guards, evidence, submit and NF-06 rules as before; the dry-run/submit tail is the old `submit()`.
- `_fill_services(run, at, ctx, now=)` -> `_FillServices` (one per attempt): `backend`, `model`, `rules`, `cfg` (= `apply.llm_fill` as dict), `today()` from `facts.today_sg(now())`, `now()` (`run(..., now=None)` clock, TR-5), `deadline_left()` (= `ctx.deadline - monotonic`, the IMAP wait is added back by `wait_for_mail` as before), `classify` (= `check_page`, raises), `log`. `cache_get` misses and `cache_put` is a no-op until N6b.
- `page_result`: `transport_failed` -> `model_unavailable` "timed out on <step>", but if `deadline_left() <= 0` -> `error` "timed out on <step>" (N1: an expired deadline is never `model_unavailable`); `unknown_required` -> `_capture_questions(..., page=True)` (capture_page) then `unanswerable_required` with the first label; required `fill_failed` -> `validation_error`; loop guard: same (step, field signatures) twice -> `page_unrecognised` "<step> shown again"; no fields on a non-review step -> `page_unrecognised` "no fields on <step>"; errors after Next -> one more `fill_page` on the same step, then `validation_error`.
- R3 F10: `stage()` writes audit rows under the page's audit keys (`page.audit_keys`); on a stop or a transport failure FILLED answers are written as `skipped` with note "not applied: the page stopped" and no value. `audit_row` now fills `evidence`, `shown_json`, `shown_origin`, `entry`.
- `_capture_questions(store, cfg, backend, at, job, fields, answers, rctx, rules, *, page=False)` (QD §4.1, §5.3): used by the new path (capture_page) and by the old path at both `unanswerable_required` points (capture). Naming only for a new non-sensitive sig without a label match, capped by `naming_max_per_attempt`, `question naming unavailable` once; any exception -> `question capture failed: <Type>`, outcome unchanged.
- `cli.cmd_apply`: the apply backend gets `num_ctx = apply.llm_fill.num_ctx` (only that run's object).
N2/N3 amendment deltas were already merged (`workday_entry`, `step_id` `_N`, `pre_import`): nothing to add.
TESTS: engineer tests may not be written under tests/ (scope hook). A draft with 12 passing tests of the loop (warm-up before the browser, model_unavailable vs deadline error, stop + capture + not-applied rows, fill_failed, error retry, loop guards, happy path, `fill: rules`, old-path capture) is at `C:\Users\Admin\AppData\Local\Temp\claude\b3f57f5c5f0c763d\9038bbe4-7068-4a15-9fba-52e7b34ce938\scratchpad\n6a\test_apply_flow_n6a.py` (scratch, not committed; the tester can copy it to tests/ and adapt it).
REQUESTS (tester): the oracles below cannot pass as written (details and the spec lines in the final report):
1. `tests/test_apply_flow_llm.py`: the 5 gated tests are `_stub(...)` bodies that raise "T4 stub" once `apply_flow._fill_services` exists (it does now). The tester writes the real bodies against `_fill_services`, `_capture_questions`, `apply_flow.fill_page`, `run(..., now=)`.
2. `tests/test_apply_flow_accounts.py::test_a_workday_job_stops_unsupported_before_edge_and_before_any_account_call`: its docstring says "the interim gate until WP-N6a; this pin flips then". Workday is driven now under `fill: llm`; it still stops `unsupported_ats` under `apply.fill: rules`. Flip the pin (e.g. run it with `apply_over={"fill": "rules"}`) and write the Workday flow oracles. The F7 oracle stays gated on `workday:NAVIGATOR.read_step`, which the SiteNavigator contract does not have (the gate never opens); regate it on `fill_page` only.
3. `tests/test_apply_flow_questions.py` `test_naming_uses_the_attempts_backend...` and `test_the_job_log_holds_key_kind_and_reason...`: both pass `extra=extra_block()`, whose `visa_status` has `by_employer.shopee` picks `[citizen]`, so tier 3 answers the VISA field (`memory:extra.visa_status`, "Citizen") and it is correctly not captured; the tests expect it captured (`visa_status`/`visa_type`, reason `no_rule`). Known limits / for N6b: `cache_get`/`cache_put` are stubs; `pagefill` keys the cache with `ctx.job.get("id")` but a job row has only `job_id` (all jobs would share the key ""): N6b must set `job["id"]` or change the key. RE: eng/Q-35-N6a-flow: D2 (hidden Greenhouse resume input not read as FILE under `fill: llm`)
BODY: Cause: `page.py` READ_JS file branch kept a file input only if `vis(el) || vis(containerOf(el))`; the Greenhouse `.field` also holds the visible `resume_text` textarea, so `containerOf` returned the input itself (display:none) and the field was dropped. Fix (page.py only): a hidden file input is read when a visible control opens it (`fileTrigger`: a visible label[for]/`el.labels`, or a visible button/[role=button]/label-for sibling in the same parent); other hidden inputs stay out. When no label[for] points at it, the label is the first plain label/legend in its parent (`fileLabel`, "Resume/CV *"). The field then goes through the normal L1 / upload path with its audit row.
RESULT: `-k apply_n6a_defect_pins` 1/2 (D2 passes; D1 red, architect). `-k pagefill` 19/19, `-k layering` 13/13. `-k apply_e2e` 12/13: the one failure is `test_fill_llm_dry_run_fills_the_greenhouse_form...` stopping `unanswerable_required` on "Location (City)" (react-select typeahead with options, reason no_option_fits) = the D1 typeahead-vs-dropdown fit; the resume no longer stops it (25 fields read).
STATUS: open

### 2026-11-08 software-engineer → coordinator (cc tester, reviewer)
TYPE: handoff
RE: eng/Q-35-N6b-flow: WP-N6b (pre-Submit scan, review check, fill cache, resume entries, other employers), KA-1
BODY: Branch `eng/Q-35-N6b-flow` (base personal 6f78f69), commits f694a14, 0ba2b97, cc2b880 (`git log 6f78f69..`). Files (4): `src/jobscraper/apply_flow.py`, `tailor_flow.py`, `store.py`, `apply/pagefill.py`. Config untouched (`apply.mode` stays `dry_run`).
Built:
- `tailor_flow.resume_entries(cfg) -> tuple[(id, kind, text)]`: cached master only (new private `_cached_master`, shared with `master_text`), never parses, never raises, `()` on a miss. Ids `experience[0]`, `education[0]`, `other[0]` count per kind in reading order (same shape as the profile's `experience[i]`); text = title, organisation, location, dates, subtitle, then bullets, one per line (title first: E2's no-employer score reads line 1). Projects/leadership/awards/certification entries and award/certification lines are kind `other`; contact, summary and skills never appear.
- `rctx.resume_entries` (cached once per run) and `rctx.other_employers` = `_Run.other_employers(job, aliases, groups)`: watchlist names (`store.companies()`) + new `store.sighting_employers()` (display name, else `employer_norm`), minus this employer, its aliases and every spelling in its aliases.yaml group (`declarations.company_match`). Set for both paths (resolve_ctx), harmless on the old one.
- Cache: `_FillServices.cache_get/cache_put` -> `store.fill_cache_get/put` (created_at from the attempt clock, TR-5); a store error is a miss (log line by type only), never a failed attempt. `_Run.purge_fill_cache()` at `run()` start: older than 30 days by the run clock. `llm_fill.cache: false` still disables (pagefill's check). KEY BUG: `pagefill._job_id(run)` = `job["job_id"]` (fallback `job["id"]`, kept because `test_apply_pagefill` builds jobs with `id`); `inputs_hash` untouched, so Q-74's `model_sees_sensitive` addition will merge without a clash.
- `_Run.pre_submit_scan(page, nav, ctx, at)` (the name the waiting oracle gates on), called at the end of `body_llm` after the review screenshot, in dry run AND auto: (1) `P.scan_required(page, nav.scope(...))` hits -> `ApplyFailure(VALIDATION_ERROR, "review: <labels>", step="review")` (labels from a second `read_page`; marks are stable); (2) the resume file name must be on the page (`P.page_text`, case/space-insensitive) when `Path(ctx.resume_path).name in ctx.uploaded`, else `VALIDATION_ERROR "review: resume not shown"` (`REVIEW_RESUME_MISSING`); (3) required TEXT/TEXTAREA answers not on the page: one log line with a count, no values, no outcome change. A scan that raises: auto mode -> `VALIDATION_ERROR` "review: could not check the required fields" (`REVIEW_SCAN_FAILED`, fail closed); dry run -> log line "pre-Submit scan could not run (Type)" and carry on (a dry run sends nothing; this keeps the FakePage-based n6a/flow tests, which have no `evaluate`, green).
- F1 REPLACEMENT: `AUTO_SUBMIT_BLOCKED` is removed. `body_llm` now ends: scan -> dry run: `ready_to_submit`; auto: `self.submit(page, ctx, nav, at, job)` (unchanged: `submit_ready`, `claim_click` with the duplicate and cap re-check, committed `submit_clicked_at` before `nav.submit()`). Auto mode reaches `submit` only through a passing scan and review check.
Verified here (evidence):
  `-k apply_flow` 116/117: the one failure is the gated placeholder `test_apply_flow_llm::test_n6b_the_pre_submit_scan_stops_an_empty_required_field` ("WP-N6b landed: write the oracle", it activates now that `_Run.pre_submit_scan` exists).
  `-k apply_n6a` 5/6: `test_apply_n6a_review_fixes::test_f1_an_auto_mode_llm_attempt_stops_at_review...` fails BY DESIGN (it pins the removed F1 stop; with FakePage, which has no `evaluate`, the auto scan fails closed). Tester: replace it with the new contract (auto + passing scan -> submits once; auto + scan hit or scan error -> `validation_error`, no click).
  `-k tailor` 26/26, `-k layering` 13/13. Full suite and `-k apply_e2e` / `-k apply_pagefill`: see the RESULT line below.
TESTS: my scope hook refuses `tests/**` and the scratch folder (both denied), so I wrote no test file. I ran 14 ad-hoc checks from stdin against the real code (all ok): entries per kind/no contact/skills; `()` on a miss and on a broken master; rctx gets entries and other employers through `run`; other-employers drops this employer, a prefix match ("Fake Co Group") and an aliases group; `sighting_employers`; cache round trip per job and per `inputs_hash`, clock stamp; store error = miss; `_job_id`; 30-day purge at run start (44-day row gone, 25-day row kept); scan hit -> `validation_error` "review: Last name" in dry run and auto with 0 submits and 0 clicks; scan raises (auto stops, dry run goes on); auto with a passing scan submits exactly once (1 click), dry run never; resume-name check (case-insensitive hit passes, absent fails, none uploaded = no check); the not-shown note carries no value.
REQUESTS (tester): (1) write N6b pins for the list above, in particular `pagefill._job_id` (two jobs must not share a cache key) and the flow-level cache replay (zero model calls on a retry; a changed cited fact is a miss); (2) fix `test_apply_n6a_review_fixes::test_f1_*` as above; (3) fill the gated `test_n6b_...` oracle (it needs a page that `evaluate`s: the Greenhouse fixture, or patch `apply_flow.P.scan_required`).
OPEN: Q-74 not yet merged into `personal` when this was written; merge `personal` into this branch when the Coordinator says so (my edits are in `resolve_ctx`, `_FillServices`, the `body_llm` tail and `pagefill._cached/_cache_put` call sites only).
KNOWN LIMITS: the resume-name check assumes the site shows the file name on the review/form page (Workday review and the Greenhouse attach row do in the fixtures; a site that truncates a long name would stop an attempt `validation_error` "review: resume not shown": fail closed, visible in the dry run). Not run against a live Workday review page (no live runs).

TYPE: handoff
RE: eng/Q-80-declarations: D-164 `question_frame` (Q-81 -> Q-80), review Q-48 F12 and F14; KA-2
BODY: Branch `eng/Q-80-declarations` (base personal 2045f06), commit e7fcefe. Files: `src/jobscraper/apply/declarations.py`, `src/jobscraper/apply/locks.py`. Built:
- `question_frame(label_toks, classes, f)` replaces `_status_frame`: strip a trailing ANSWER_HINT, PREFIX runs (<= 3 tokens), optional LEAD (+ if/whether/that), then shape A (status starter, i am/i have/i hold/there is/there are, or a status run anywhere), B (you are/you have/... only after a LEAD), C (`_noun_phrase`: DROPDOWN/RADIO and `bool_shaped`, classes within NOUN_CLASSES, <= 6 tokens, each token in a matched phrase span or NOUN_FILLER, no NDA object span). Constants `_FRAME_*`, `_NOUN_*` sit above `_NEGATIVE_ATTESTATIONS`.
- `_bool_answer(..., classes=())`: runs the frame for every class except NDA willingness, after the freedom inversion too (F12). Class set = `classes` (agreement path, from `answer()`), else `{cls}`; the disability path passes `{DISABILITY}`.
- Gate 1 `_starts_as_question` uses `_strip_frame_prefix` (same PREFIX strip, no LEAD, no shape C).
- F14: removed the dead `locks.NEGATIONS` (grep: no other user, tests included); `declarations.NEGATIONS` is the one list.
Verified by running the pin table through `declarations.answer` (cwd = worktree, sample profile, employer Acme): the 8 labels unknown -> No ("Politically exposed person (PEP)?", "Criminal record", "Bankruptcy", "Disability" no -> No / yes -> Yes, "Declaration: Are you a politically exposed person?", "Note: Do you have a non-compete?", "Please confirm: Are you a PEP?", "Please indicate if you have ever been bankrupt"); "Criminal record or bankruptcy" -> No (rule_id bankruptcy+criminal_record); "Free from any restrictive covenants?" -> unknown "not a status question"; "Confirm you are free from any restrictive covenants" -> Yes; "Non-compete agreement?", "Confidentiality agreement", "Criminal record check", "Former employee?" -> unknown; PEP as TEXT / as a single checkbox -> unknown; "Declaration: Are you a PEP?" as TEXT -> No; "Please indicate if you have ever been bankrupt" as TEXT -> unknown; PREFIX cap: "Please kindly note are you a PEP?" -> No, with "important" added (4 tokens) -> unknown.
TESTS: the scope hook blocks `tests/` for the engineer, so `tests/test_apply_declarations_d164.py` is NOT written. Requests for the tester: pin the table above in that file (helpers `Q._ans`, `Q._filled`, `Q._prof` from `test_apply_declarations_q48`; `Q._ans(label, type=FT.TEXT, options=())` for the TEXT rows, `type=FT.RADIO` for radio), plus `assert not hasattr(locks, "NEGATIONS")` for F14.

(expected Yes, now unknown "not a status question"). Cause: it begins with the modal "can you" before the LEAD verb "confirm", which is none of the D-164 shapes (A needs a status starter, B needs the LEAD at the start), and F12 makes the freedom path run the frame. The other three FREEDOM labels still pass. Does D-164 want "can you / could you / would you" accepted before a LEAD (the owner pinned this label as Yes in D-91), or does the pin change to unknown? I did not choose: one extra PREFIX-like run in `_strip_frame_prefix`/`question_frame` is the whole change if you say yes.

TYPE: handoff
RE: eng/Q-80-flow-fixes, commit 366c098 (tester pins 48bdc98): R3 F12/F14, N6a F3-F6; KA-1
BODY: files `src/jobscraper/apply/pagefill.py`, `src/jobscraper/apply_flow.py`, `src/jobscraper/apply/page.py`. `-k apply_q80` 11/11.
- R3 F12 (`pagefill._delete_entries`): a delete is confirmed per group (clicked and none of its own controls on the page). The section entry count is a logged cross-check only (`E3: <section> has more entries than the confirmed deletes explain`), never a demotion.
- R3 F14 (`pagefill._add_failed`): a failed Add is logged (`E5: add failed in <section>: <why>`) and no longer marks the filled entries `fill_failed`; `entry_failed` and `ENTRY_ADD_FAILED_NOTE` are removed. LIMIT (needs your call): PageOutcome has no entry-level channel, so the flow no longer stops on a missing entry; it proceeds with fewer entries than the source has (nothing false is typed). A contracts field (`entry_failed: tuple[str, ...]`, which apply_flow would turn into a stop or a section note) would restore the stop. Old pin `test_apply_pagefill_review::test_f5_an_entry_added_at_a_deleted_index_...` (second half: swallowed Add -> `unknown_required or fill_failed`) passed on my first F14 variant and contradicts the new pin; see the RESULT line for whether it is red.
- N6a F3 (`apply_flow.body_llm`, ~1153): the warm-up seconds are added back to `ctx.deadline`.

### 2026-11-10 software-engineer -> coordinator/tester: Q-45 WP-K1 compose call and checks (KA-4)
TYPE: handoff
BODY: branch eng/Q-45-compose, commit 550decd; files `src/jobscraper/tailor/compose.py` (new), `src/jobscraper/tailor/validate.py` (`UPGRADE_WORDS` only), `src/jobscraper/tailor_flow.py` (T-block). No contracts or config change; `apply.compose.enabled` untouched (K3).
PUBLIC NAMES (for WP-T7)
- `tailor_flow.compose(cfg, req: ComposeRequest, *, backend, redact) -> ComposeResult`. Cached master only (`_cached_master`), `ComposeResult(False, error="no master")` on a miss (attempts 0). Builds the dictionary like `tailor()` (aliases + applicant skills/attested_skills + master skills); a broken aliases file gives `error="setup: <Type>"`. `redact` is called as `redact(text, req.s_values)` (the `llm_fill.redact` signature), on the whole user prompt including the FIX line.
- `tailor_flow.write_letter(text, path, fmt) -> str`: `fmt` `"docx"` (A4, Calibri 11 via `render._font`, one plain paragraph per blank-line block, no name/contact) or `"txt"` (UTF-8, blank line between paragraphs, trailing newline); creates the folder; returns `str(path)`; any other fmt raises ValueError. Naming `-2`/`-3` is the caller's.
- `tailor.compose.compose(req, master, dictionary, *, backend, redact, attested=()) -> ComposeResult` (pure stage; no cfg), `check(text, req, master_text, dictionary, attested=()) -> list[ValidationIssue]` (C1-C7 only, no model), `system_prompt(req)`, `user_prompt(req, master_text)`, `fix_line(issues)`, `schema(max_chars)`, constants `COMPOSE_SYSTEM`, `FINAL_LINE`, `TEMPERATURE=0.3`, `SEED=7`, `TOP_K=20`, `JD_CHARS=3000`, `MIN_WORDS={"letter":80,"answer":20}`, `LONG_DIGITS=7`. `validate.UPGRADE_WORDS` (frozenset, tailor_design 7.4 list).
- Call: `backend.complete(system, user, 2*max_words+100, format=schema, temperature=0.3, seed=7, top_k=20)` (options passed only if the backend's signature has them; `schema=` otherwise). Reply is JSON `{"text": ...}`; unreadable or non-string text = empty = EMPTY issue = retried. Any exception from the call = `ok=False, error="transport: <ExceptionTypeName>"` (no message text). One retry: `FIX: <code detail>; ...` (max 10; PRIVATE_VALUE adds its code only). Result: `model`, `prompt_hash` (sha256(system+user)[:16], of the last prompt), `attempts` 1 or 2. Logs carry counts and issue codes only.
PROBES (synthetic master: Acme Systems intern, Python/PostgreSQL/Redis, "35%", "12 internal teams"; employer "Orbit Labs", alias "Orbit", title "Software Engineer"; S = phone, email, address; other_employers = "Globex Corp"). Clean = a 54-word answer naming only those facts. Each probe is CLEAN plus one change, and ends `ok=False` after 2 calls:
- C1 `EMPTY`: reply "" (or unreadable JSON). `LENGTH`: 4 words (allowed 20-120); CLEAN with max_words=30; CLEAN with max_chars=100; CLEAN as kind="letter" (54 < 80). C1 returns at once (only that issue).
- C2 `NUMBER_ADDED`: "35%" -> "40%" (detail `40%`); " I graduate in 2030." (detail `2030`).
- C3 `FACT_ADDED`: " I have also worked with Kubernetes." (detail `kubernetes`, a JD-only skill); "at Initech" (detail `initech`). Attested canonical ("Kubernetes" passed in `attested`) is allowed.
- C4 `VERB_UPGRADE`: "I also led efforts that reduced" (detail `led`).
- C5 `PRIVATE_VALUE` (details are only `value`/`email`/`digits`/`url`): the sample phone; an email; the S address; a bare digit run "8812 3344"; "https://github.com/someone/proj". The private spans are blanked before C2/C3/C6/C7 run, so no NUMBER_ADDED/FACT_ADDED detail carries a piece of an S value (the first draft leaked "9123"/"4567"/"example" that way, fixed). The recorded prompts (JD contained the S values) hold `[private]` and no S value.
- C6 `OTHER_EMPLOYER`: " I admire Globex too." (detail is the name from `req.other_employers`; also raises FACT_ADDED "globex").
- C7 `PLACEHOLDER`: " Sincerely, [Name]" (also FACT_ADDED "name"), " <company>", " Lorem ipsum.".
- Retry: bad then clean = ok, attempts 2, second prompt = first + one `FIX:` line; bad,bad,clean = 2 calls, never 3. Transport on call 1 or 2 = `transport: RuntimeError` etc.
EVIDENCE: ad hoc stdin script with a fake backend: 36 PASS, 0 FAIL (clean, call options, prompt order, no S value, 19 probes, retry-rescue, retry-once, transport x2, unreadable reply, letter kind, paragraph start, attested, no-master, tailor_flow ok, write_letter txt/docx/bad fmt). `-k tailor` 26/26, `-k layering` 13/13, `-k tailor_compose` "no tests matched" (skips until T7).
CHOICES THE SPEC LEFT OPEN (please confirm or overrule; each is a one-line change)
1. FINAL LINE of the user prompt is unspecified in LF 19.4: I used `Reply as JSON: {"text": "<the text>"}.` (`compose.FINAL_LINE`).
2. C2/C3/C4 also allow numbers/words that are inside the employer, aliases or job title (the system prompt makes the model name them exactly, e.g. a "Lead Engineer" title or a "2026 Graduate Programme"); C3 additionally allows every word of those names (a title's first word is otherwise not a fact).
3. Paragraph/line breaks are treated as sentence starts for the fact check (`validate.facts` only knows ". "), else every paragraph's first capitalised word after a comma would be FACT_ADDED.
4. C1 minimum is `min(80|20, max_words)` so a max_words below the minimum stays satisfiable. C2 compares as sets (a master number may be repeated).
5. C5 URL: a URL (scheme, www., or a bare domain ending .com/.net/.org/.io/.dev/.sg/.co/.ai/.app) not found in the master text or the allowed names. "Node.js"-style names are not matched.
6. C6 company tokens: lowercase alnum tokens with the legal/filler words dropped (the apply stage's `_COMPANY_NOISE` list, copied, since stages can't import each other); contiguous whole-token match.
OPEN ISSUE (spec): a salutation "Dear Hiring Manager," fails C3 (FACT_ADDED `hiring`, `manager`; probe output), as does any capitalised non-resume word mid-sentence ("Singapore" if the master lacks it). The system prompt doesn't forbid a salutation. Retry's FIX line will name the tokens, but real runs will often use the retry. Options for the architect: tell the prompt "no salutation or sign-off", or add a small letter-frame word list to C3. I did neither.
STATUS: open

Step 7: a stop's required unknowns are split by `_compose_split`: a compose target whose lock note is `compose_needed...` is PENDING when `services.compose_enabled` (getattr, default False; `_FillServices` sets it from `apply.compose.enabled`) or when it is a FILE target with an owner answer (`questions.owner_compose_text`). "off")`; `no master` / a failed check -> capture with note `compose_needed: no master` / `failed check`, ends `unanswerable_required` with the label; `transport...` -> `model_unavailable`, nothing captured. `page_result`: a `compose_needed` still on the outcome after the refill (or in the corrective pass) = `ApplyFailure(ERROR, "compose loop on <step>")`, nothing sent.

## handoff Q-100 lane C (shell, two-part setup, audit fixes), branch eng/Q-100-C-shell
TYPE: handoff
- Files: `web/src/App.vue`, `web/src/tabs.js`, `web/src/components/AttentionBanner.vue` (new), `web/src/components/ModeChip.vue` (new), `web/src/components/ToastHost.vue`, `web/src/setup.js`, `web/src/components/SetupChecklist.vue`, `web/src/tabs/Overview.vue`, `web/src/tabs/Profile.vue`, `web/src/tabs/Applications.vue`, `web/src/components/ApplicationDetails.vue`, `web/src/components/JobStatus.vue`. No lane 0, lane B, Inbox, Runs or Companies file touched; no built files committed.
- Shell (§1): `normaliseHash` uses `parseHash` + `hashFor(tab, view, item)`, so `#/settings/accounts/gmail:3` stays. `loadSignals()` = `getConnections()` + `getApplyStatus()` at boot, after any job ends, on visible after > 60 s, and on leaving Settings (my addition: the owner may just have fixed something). The load before is kept in memory (`connectionsBefore`) for `attentionItems(cur, prev)`. Settings tab: `badge: ({attention}) => attention`, `badgeTone: 'attention'` (amber), label "need attention". A failed/404 load = no badge, no banner, no chip.
- `AttentionBanner.vue` (props `items`, `hidden`): always-present `role="status"` region; one item "{Name} needs attention. {line} [Fix it | See how to fix]" -> `#/settings/accounts/<id>`; several "{n} connections need attention. [Review]" -> `#/settings/accounts`. "See how to fix" for `apply_mailbox` and `employer_accounts` (CLI fixes in W1). Hidden on Settings with no view or the accounts view. Dismiss stores the sorted `id:code` list in sessionStorage `attention.dismissed`.
- `ModeChip.vue` (prop `status`): "Auto-apply on" link to `#/settings/apply` only when `mode === 'auto'`; hidden on dry_run/null/error.
- Bottom bar (A14): `tabs.js` `bar: true` + `icon` on Overview/Inbox/Applications; under 640px the top tab bar is hidden and `nav.bottom-nav` (buttons with `aria-current`, `data-bnav`) shows them plus More. More opens a modal `<dialog class="more-sheet">` (content rendered only while open, so jsdom sees no second tab list) with Runs, Companies, Profile, Settings, Developer and their badges; More sums the hidden tabs' attention badges; Esc/backdrop/choice close it and focus returns to More. `main`, `.save-bar` and `.toast-region` use `--bottom-nav-h + env(safe-area-inset-bottom)`.
- Setup (§4.2): `setupSteps` now takes `unknown: [ids]` and returns `status` (done|todo|unknown) + `required`; new `countSteps(steps)`; resume AND titles hrefs -> `#/profile/resume` (A6; titles live in that view too). `SetupChecklist` props `runsKnown`, `withApply`. Inbox (no props change) keeps part 1 only, title "Get your first matches". Overview always mounts it `with-apply :runs-known="!runsError"`: title "Get set up", progress "Find jobs n of m · Apply for me n of m"; part 1 while no runs (or runs unknown), part 2 while a required step is to do and "Later" (localStorage `setup.apply.later`) isn't chosen. Details = union of GET /api/applicant `missing` and /api/apply/status `missing`. A failed check = "Couldn't check", not counted; all failed -> "Couldn't check your setup just now. [Try again]".
- Profile: `useView('profile', VIEWS, stored ?? 'resume')`; a view in the hash wins and counts as chosen (no auto-switch); localStorage stays the fallback.
- Fixes: A13 row actions in `.row-actions` (gap, 2rem, `--touch-min` under 640px), Remove last after a rule and asks first inline ("Remove {company} from Applications? Its notes go too." [Remove] [Keep], focus to Keep, back to Remove on Keep). A12 "Nothing saved yet. Saving keeps your details on this PC." A15 "No job is running. Past runs are in the Runs tab."; Overview failed-run card prefixes "Reason: {stats.reason}." ("It was cancelled." for cancelled).
- Evidence: `cd web; npx vitest run`: Test Files 5 failed | 28 passed (33), Tests 6 failed | 637 passed | 1 skipped (644); all 6 are design changes (request below). `npx vite build --outDir <scratch>/dist`: built OK. Smoke on DB copies (8771 dev copy, 8772 empty first-run DB, JOBSCRAPER_MAIL_SYNC=0, the scratch bundle served by create_app(static_dir=...)), Edge headless at 1440/390, light and dark; both stopped by PID (taskkill /PID 8336, 47616), ports free. Real /api/connections 404 -> no badge/banner/chip, only the 404 console line. With a mocked /api/connections (fixture rows) + mode auto: badge "1 need attention", banner + chip, More "1", banner hidden on `#/settings/accounts/gmail:3` (hash kept), Dismiss holds across reload. 390: save bar bottom 788 = bar top 788; toast bottom 764 < 788. All 500s -> "Couldn't check your setup just now. Try again". First run: "Find jobs 0 of 4 · Apply for me 1 of 2"; Upload -> `#/profile/resume` on "Resume and titles". Screenshots stay in the scratchpad (never committed).
- Known limits: (1) a view switch inside a tab uses replaceState (no event): the shell re-reads the hash after a click or keyup in the panel, so the banner follows Settings view switches made by mouse or keyboard only. (2) Before lane A, the Local AI / mailbox / Gmail setup steps read "Couldn't check" (404), so "Apply for me" counts 0 of 1 (details only). (3) The save bar is sticky inside its form: near the form's top it scrolls with the page (unchanged behaviour), it never sits under the bottom bar once stuck. (4) The `.setup-step` "○" mark for a to-do required step and "–" for an optional one are my reading of the mock.

BODY: branch `eng/Q-111-apply-settings` (from personal 9681490), commit 09a290c. `routers/apply.py`: `GET/PUT /api/apply/settings` -> `{mode, daily_cap, per_employer_daily_cap, headed, captcha_wait_s, compose_enabled, writable: true, defaults: {same six}}` (effective = `cfg.apply.with_settings(store.get_setting)`; defaults = config.yaml alone). PUT takes any non-empty subset, strict types (an int is not a bool, "5" is refused), all checked before any write; ranges daily_cap 0..500, per_employer_daily_cap 0..100, captcha_wait_s 0..600, mode auto|dry_run; 422 `{detail:"invalid", errors:[{field, message}]}`, empty or non-object body 422. Stored as `apply.<key>` and `apply.compose.enabled` (bools as "true"/"false"). `config.py`: `ApplyConfig.with_settings` now honours `apply.compose.enabled` (new `COMPOSE_SETTING_KEY`); every caller (cli `_effective_apply`, apply router, mail router, mailstatus_flow, connections) sees it. `web/src/api.js` end block: `getApplySettings()`, `saveApplySettings(changes)`.
- Notes for the Vue side: `ApplySettings.vue` imports `getApplySettings`/`putApplySettings` from `connections_api.js` (already present, tolerant of a missing route); the api.js pair is a plain duplicate and can be dropped if unused. Its client-side check still says caps 1-50 (`whole(d.daily_cap, 1, 50, ...)`): the server allows 0..500 / 0..100 (D-204), so that Vue range needs updating by whoever owns the file. apply_design.md §14.3 still says "caps 1-50" and lacks `compose_enabled`/`writable`/`defaults` (request to the architect).
- Not run, by design: no apply job, no POST /api/apply. Checked with FastAPI TestClient on a temp DB; no bundle rebuild needed beyond the api.js change.

### 2026-11-12 software-engineer -> coordinator, tester (Q-119): first live Greenhouse attempt, root causes
TYPE: handoff
BODY: branch `eng/Q-119-greenhouse-live-fixes` (from personal 6a26db1), fix commit 1626279. Reproduced on the saved page (scripts removed, network blocked, headless Edge, dummy sample profile; never the posting URL).
- A CONFIRMED, FIXED (`apply/page.py` READER_JS inputs branch). react-select renders `<input required tabindex="-1" aria-hidden="true" class="…requiredInput">` inside `.select__container` while the select is empty. `reactCtl(el)` (page.py:214) finds the visible `.select__control` through `.select__container`, so `visible` was true and the twin was pushed as a second `react_select` field; it has no aria-labelledby / label / own container label, so its label is "" and `reqOf` sees `required` -> a required, label-less question per empty select (12 of the 43 live fields). Fix: an input with `aria-hidden="true"` and `tabIndex < 0` is a widget helper and is skipped; the select's own field takes `required: true` when such a twin sits in its `.select__container`. Saved page: 37 fields with 7 twins -> 30 fields, 0 label-less.
- C CONFIRMED = A. `country_code` / `location_city` were the twins of Country (decided tier 5 `contact.country`) and Location (City) (tier 5 `contact.city`); audit rows 5 and 8 are the phantoms.
- D CONFIRMED = A, plus a residual: `apply_flow.py:1347-1349` names `first.field.label` of `unknown_required[0]`; the first unknown was a twin, so detail "" and “”. Request (apply_flow is not mine): fall back to the first unknown with a non-empty label, else the audit key, never "".
- B CONFIRMED, NOT FIXED (needs a design decision). Two causes: (1) `page.entry_sections` (generic, which Greenhouse uses per LF §9.2) finds the "Add another" button but no numbered heading (the panel's title is a `<p>Education</p>`), so there are 0 entries; its title is the nearest earlier heading/legend anywhere ("Phone" legend -> `other_phone`), so the education fields are ungrouped. (2) Ungrouped fields may not cite entry facts (`guards.allowed_refs`, guards.py:346), so a model citing `profile:education[0].*` gets G2a (repro) and a live model citing anything else gets G2b. Even with (1) fixed, the blank pre-rendered entry would be unmatched (E2 needs a shown school/degree), and E3 with no delete control stops it. Options: (a) a Greenhouse `entry_sections` row (`.education--container` = section education, each `.education--form` = one entry, index from the `--N` id suffix, add = `.add-another-button`) plus a new E-rule "an entry whose fields are all empty is a free slot: bind it to the first unmatched source entry of its kind (as E5 forced binding does after Add)"; (b) the same E-rule with a generic structure rule (the Add button's container holds unnumbered panels); (c) leave it: education stays a captured question. Recommend (a). Also: the generic section title should come only from a heading inside the section; today an Add button can borrow a far heading (a "…job…" heading would make it `work_experience`).
- E(i) observed, not fixed: (1) `02-failed.png` shows values the attempt never applied (a resume file not from JobScraper, names, contact, some education fields), while `01-open.png` is empty and the audit shows no site values at read time: the site restored data asynchronously after our read. Suggest a settle wait / re-read before deciding; needs the Coordinator's look. (2) G2b is loose: a single shared token such as "i" (from "i.e.") makes `consents.certify_true` "relevant" to the GPA text label (`facts.relevant` / `_title_words` keep one-letter and stop-like words such as i, any, as, by, can). A real model would rarely cite it; a relevance-rule change is a design call.
- E(ii) rightly the owner's (normalised keys): how_did_hear_about (no profile value is an offered option), immigration_status_…_hong (label names another territory, D-180/181), indicate_grade_point_average (also asks the scale), if_last_professional_experience (compose off), if_currently_employed_reasons, if_currently_employed_length, region_currently_located (AMER/APAC/EMEA), preferred_…_office_location, specifically_be_advised_that (recording opt in/out).
- After the fix, saved page with a fake model citing the dummy profile: 30 fields; 13 filled (9 by locks), 2 optional blank, 15 unknown-required = 7 education (B) + the 8 E(ii) fields that remain on this page state. (Live it would be 31 fields, the resume field included.)
- Fixture for the tester (structure only, generic labels): a form with (1) two react-select fields as Greenhouse renders them: `div.select > div.select__container > label#x-label[for=x] "Label*"` + `div.select-shell > div > div.select__control > div.select__value-container > div.select__input-container > input#x.select__input[role=combobox][aria-labelledby=x-label][aria-required=true]`, and as the select-shell's LAST child `input[required][tabindex=-1][aria-hidden=true]` with an inline style `opacity:0;position:absolute;bottom:0;left:0;right:0;width:100%` (no height:0; that is why the old fixtures missed it); one of the two without `aria-required` on the combobox and no `*` in the label, to pin that the twin keeps it required; (2) an education block: `div.education--container > div.education--form > (hr, div.education--header > p "Education", react-selects School/Degree/Discipline/Start date month/End date month with ids `school--0` etc., number inputs Start/End date year)` + `button.add-another-button "Add another"`, with an earlier `fieldset > legend "Phone"` elsewhere on the page. Expected: one field per select, no "" labels, required kept; the education fields' group stays "" until B is decided (pin as a known gap).
- Guards that must keep rejecting a wrong-entry or unrelated fact after any B fix: G2a/G6 entry scoping (`tests/test_llm_fill_guard_table_entries.py`), G2b (`tests/test_llm_fill_guards.py`, `tests/test_llm_fill_guard_table.py`), Q-46(a) home location in an entry section (`tests/test_llm_fill_q46.py`).

### 2026-11-12 software-engineer → coordinator, tester, U1/U2/U3 engineers, E2 engineer
TYPE: handoff
RE: Q-120 WP-W1 (D-205; F-30, NF-05, NF-06, D-200); apply_queue_design §3.4, §3.5, §4.2, §6; review H1, H2, H3, M1, M2, M3, L2, L3
BODY: Branch `eng/Q-120-W1-web` (from `eng/Q-120-E1-store` + `personal` merged for contracts v6). Commits cfa3bd9 (runner, switch, jobs.py argv) and d7a10a5 (routes). Files: `web/applyqueue.py` (new), `web/jobs.py`, `web/api.py`, `routers/apply.py`, `routers/questions.py` (`retry_applications` only), `routers/jobs.py` (run, cancel), `routers/applications.py` (list + untrack).
Runner (`app.state.apply_queue`, `ApplyQueueRunner(app_state, enabled=)`): looks up `app_state.jobs` and `app_state.store_factory` at every kick (H1); `kick()` = §3.4 steps 1-6, never raises; judges only the job id it started (exit 3 → `busy_elsewhere`, not in {0,143} and not reason `cancelled` → `worker_failed`). `create_app(apply_queue_worker=False)` default: no start-up hook, no tick, no worker; with True it registers `startup` (clear yield + `queue_pause_if_waiting(restarted)`; the in-memory gate opens only if that succeeded, M2), then `start_timer` (5 s), and `stop` on shutdown. Resume opens the gate after its commit; a cancel whose pause write fails closes it.
**E2 / cmd_web:** `from .web.applyqueue import apply_queue_wanted` and pass `create_app(..., apply_queue_worker=apply_queue_wanted(os.environ))` (=1 on, =0 off, unset → off when `JOBSCRAPER_DB` is set). JobManager argv for `("apply", {"queue": True})` = `apply queue --parent-pid <os.getpid()>`; `queue` is a reserved id (`APPLY_RESERVED`).
Routes and shapes (UI lanes build from these):
- `POST /api/apply` body `{job_ids: [1..20], source?: "inbox"|"applications"}` → 202 `{queued:[{job_id, position}], accepted:[job_id], refused:[{job_id, reason_code, text, hint}], mode, paused: bool, started: bool, disabled: bool, job: JobView|null}` (`job` = the apply job when the worker holds the slot); 422 `{detail:"profile_incomplete", missing}`; 422 `{detail:"refused", refused}`. Never 409. Refusal codes: `not_found`, `already_queued`, `in_progress`, `duplicate`, `cap_reached` (auto only), `queue_full` (100). A role with no application row gets `to_apply` (company/role/url from the job) in the same commit as its queue row.
- `GET /api/apply/status` → `{mode, missing, busy, queue:{waiting:int, applying:bool, paused: reason|null}}` (`busy` = a job holds the slot; `applying` = the slot's kind is `apply`; `paused` includes view-only `disabled`/`restarted`).
- `GET /api/apply/queue` (no side effects) → `{paused: null|{reason, text, hint, since}, now: null|{attempt_id, job_id, company, role, mode, state, started_at, interrupted}, slot: "free"|"apply"|"run"|"other", run_pending: bool, waiting:[{job_id, company, role, mode, position, enqueued_at, source, note}], recent:[{attempt_id, job_id, company, role, mode, kind, text, finished_at}]}` (recent = 10; `kind`/`text` as `attempt_view`).
- `DELETE /api/apply/queue/{job_id}` → 204; 404 `{detail:"not_waiting"}`.
- `POST /api/apply/queue/pause` → 200 view (reason `owner`, idempotent; an existing pause keeps its reason).
- `POST /api/apply/queue/stop` `{attempt_id:int}` → pause `cancelled` committed, then 200 view if that attempt is the live one (worker cancelled); 409 `{detail:"already_finished"}` otherwise (also when the slot is idle); 409 `{detail:<text>}` JobNotOurs; 500 JobStuck.
- `POST /api/apply/queue/resume` → 200 view (then kick); 409 `{detail:"disabled"}`; 422 `{detail:"profile_incomplete", missing}`.
- `POST /api/apply/queue/clear` → 200 `{removed:n, queue: view}`.
- `POST /api/questions/retry` → 422 `not_ready` as today, else the enqueue helper (source `retry`): the same 202 body as `POST /api/apply` (or its 422s, including `profile_incomplete`). No 409.
- `POST /api/jobs/run` while the slot's kind is `apply` and the runner is enabled → 202 `{job:<apply view>, deferred:true}` (pending run + `queue_request_yield`); other busy → 409 as today.
- `POST /api/jobs/cancel` on an `apply` job: `queue_pause(cancelled)` first; response unchanged.
- `DELETE /api/applications/{job_id}` while `live_attempt(job_id)` → 409 `{detail:"in_progress", text, hint}`, nothing deleted; else as today (queue row deleted by the store).
- `GET /api/applications` rows gain `queue: null|{position}`; a waiting role's `last_attempt` is `{kind:"queued", state:"queued", id:null, ...same keys}` (no attempt row exists while waiting, I2).
Evidence (ad hoc stdin script, temp DB, real `create_app(job_command=<raises if called>)`, fake JobManager swapped in after it): two POSTs while a fake `run` holds the slot → 202 positions 1, 2, `started:false`; repeat → 422 `already_queued`; GET queue twice → identical, settings/attempt rows/fake starts unchanged; DELETE → 204 then 404 `not_waiting`; free slot → kick starts `("apply", {"queue": True})`; `/jobs/run` during it → 202 `deferred:true`, yield 1; worker exit 0 → run starts, yield cleared; run ends → worker again; untrack in flight → 409 `in_progress`, untrack waiting → queue row gone; stop stale id → 409 `already_finished`, 0 cancels, paused `cancelled`; stop live → 200, 1 cancel; pause twice → `owner`; clear → removed 1; exit 1 → `worker_failed`; exit 3 → `busy_elsewhere`; cancel on apply → paused `cancelled`; restart with 1 waiting → `restarted`, kick False, 0 starts; store raising at start-up → gate closed, enqueue 202 `paused:true`, no start, view `restarted`, Resume → start; `JOBSCRAPER_APPLY_QUEUE=0` → 202 `disabled:true`, view `disabled`, Resume 409, `/jobs/run` 409, no timer; default app → `started:false, disabled:true`, 0 starts. Real job_command calls: [].
Tests: `-k web_` 129/133, `-k q114` 2/3, `-k web_questions` 18/20, `-k web_control` 37/37, `-k connections` 37/38, `-k layering` 13/13, `-k apply_cli` 23/23, `-k q76` 15/15, `-k test_h1` 7/7. All 7 failures are stale pins (below).
REQUEST (tester): update these stale pins (design §8 "Changed pins"):
- `tests/test_connections.py` SWEEP_GET_ALLOWLIST: add the line `    "/api/apply/queue",` between `"/api/applications",` and `"/api/apply/settings",`.
- `test_q114_caps.py::test_q114_b_…` line 156 `len(fake.started) == 1 and fake.started[0][0] == "apply"` → default app: `fake.started == []` and `len(body["queued"]) == 1`; line 171 `len(fake.started) == 1` → `fake.started == []`. Variant with `create_app(cfg, apply_queue_worker=True)` + startup: `fake.started == [("apply", {"queue": True})]` (the fake needs `current()` idle, as it has).
- `test_web_apply_settings.py::test_apply_settings_mode_switch_is_seen_by_status_effective_config_and_preflight` line 158 `fake.started == [("apply", {"job_ids": [target]})]` → `fake.started == []` and `body["queued"] == [{"job_id": target, "position": 1}]`; line 167 `len(fake.started) == 1` → `== 0` (or count queued rows).
- `test_web_apply_settings.py::test_apply_settings_stored_caps_are_the_caps_the_preflight_enforces` line 252 `len(fake.started) == 1` → `fake.started == []` + one queued; line 260 likewise.
- `test_web_questions.py::test_answering_the_last_open_question_…_retry_takes_only_ready_jobs` line 361 expects 202 but gets 422 `profile_incomplete` (`documents.resume` missing): the enqueue helper checks the profile (§3.5); the fixture needs a resume in `resume_dir`. Then lines 363-366 → `fake.started == []`, `ok.json()["queued"] == [{"job_id": ids["j3"], "position": 1}]`, source `retry`.
- `test_web_questions.py::test_retry_while_a_job_runs_is_409_busy` line 375 (409 `busy`) → 202 with `queued` (same resume fixture need).
Choices where the design was silent: `source` optional on `POST /api/apply` (for Applications "Try again" → `applications`); the waiting role's synthetic `last_attempt`; checks + writes per id in one `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` and one commit per id (not one per batch: `set_application_status` commits); the start-up hook only when the worker is enabled (a disabled app shows `disabled` anyway, and fake stores in tests never see a start-up write); `/jobs/run` is deferred only when the runner is enabled (else nothing would ever start it: 409 as today).
Not done: no dev server smoke (the design's smoke needs E2's `cmd_web` change; with `JOBSCRAPER_APPLY_QUEUE=0` the check is GET queue `paused.reason == "disabled"` and an enqueue 202 `disabled:true`, which the script above shows on the real app object).
STATUS: open

### 2026-11-12 software-engineer → coordinator, tester, Q-121 engineer
TYPE: handoff
RE: Q-120 code review `docs/reviews/Q-120-apply-queue-code.md` F3, F4, F5, F6; branch `eng/Q-120-review-fixes` (from int/Q-120 585ed8a)
BODY: One commit per finding: F4 3c40ea9, F6 3eebd4a, F5 78297f7, F3 73b639d.
- F4 (`web/applyqueue.py`): `apply_queue_wanted(env, *, config_path=None, port=None, source_dir=None) -> bool`. `JOBSCRAPER_APPLY_QUEUE=1` forces on, `=0` forces off. Unset: on only if `JOBSCRAPER_DB` and `JOBSCRAPER_CONFIG` are unset, `config_path` is None (no `--config`), the port is 8765 (`port` when given, else `JOBSCRAPER_PORT` unset or 8765; a non-number is off), and `running_from_worktree(source_dir)` is False. `running_from_worktree` (new, exported): the repo root this code was imported from (default `Path(__file__).parents[3]`) has a `.git` FILE (a linked worktree) or its path contains `/.claude/worktrees/`. The main checkout reads False, this worktree True.
- F6 (`routers/apply.py`): Stop cancels only when `attempt_id` is the live attempt AND `slot_of(jobs)` is `apply`; otherwise 409 `{detail:"already_finished"}`, no cancel (the `cancelled` pause is still written first). Residual: the slot is read just before `jobs.cancel()`, which has no expected-kind argument; a worker ending and a run starting inside those microseconds is not covered.
- F5 (`routers/applications.py`, no store change): untrack runs `begin_immediate()` before `live_attempt(job_id)`; 409 rolls back (releases the lock); otherwise `delete_application` deletes and commits inside that same transaction. A concurrent `queue_take` (BEGIN IMMEDIATE) waits on the lock and then finds the role gone.
- F3 (`apply_flow.py`): constants `FILL_NO_USABLE_ANSWERS`, `FILL_WOULD_BE_GUESSES`, `FILL_LAST_RESORT`, `CONTENT_FAILURE_DETAILS` (the same three phrases; `CONTENT_FAILURE_MARKERS` kept as an alias), helper `content_failure_detail(marker, rest="") -> "marker" | "marker: rest"`, predicate `is_content_failure(row)`: a `failed` row whose `reason_detail` STARTS with a marker (case-insensitive, leading spaces ignored), never a `validation_error` row (its detail is page text). `pause_for` uses it. Transport (`"model unavailable"`) unchanged (out of this round's scope; the flow writes it as a suffix after a label).
Evidence (stdin scripts, fakes, temp DBs, no real job): F4 main-like dir {} → True; `JOBSCRAPER_PORT=8768` from a main-like dir → False (review scenario); port=8768 / `JOBSCRAPER_CONFIG` / `config_path` / `JOBSCRAPER_DB` → False; `.git` file → False; this worktree default → False; `=1` → True; `=0` → False; the real main checkout path → not a worktree. F3: "the model returned no answer, last resort used" → None (was fill_failed); validation_error "Last resort contact is required" → None (was fill_failed); `content_failure_detail(FILL_LAST_RESORT, "3 fields")` and each constant at the start → fill_failed. F6: live attempt id + slot `run` → 409 `already_finished`, 0 cancels (before: the search run was cancelled); slot free → 409, 0; slot `apply` → 200, 1 cancel. F5: untrack in flight → 409 and a following write succeeds (lock released); untrack waiting → 200; a connection holding untrack's BEGIN IMMEDIATE blocks a concurrent `queue_take`, which after the delete returns (None, []).
Tests: `-k apply_queue` 20/21, `-k web_` 133/133, `-k web_mail` 8/8, `-k connections` 39/39, `-k store` 98/98, `-k layering` 13/13, `-k apply_cli` 23/23, `-k apply_flow` (alone) 148/148.
REQUEST (tester): stale pin `tests/test_apply_queue_web.py::test_p18_h2_a_dev_copy_or_the_switch_off_records_clicks_but_never_starts_the_worker` line 316 `apply_queue_wanted({}) is True`: false whenever the suite runs from a worktree. Pin with `source_dir=<tmp dir with a .git directory>` for True and `source_dir=<tmp dir with a .git file>` for False; add the F4 cases above; the line 319-320 `=1` case still holds.
REQUEST (E2 / cli owner): `cmd_web` should call `apply_queue_wanted(os.environ, config_path=getattr(args, "config", None), port=port)` (port = the resolved `cfg.web["port"]`), so `--config` and a config-file port count too. Until then `--config` from the main checkout on port 8765 is not detected (a worktree still is).
REQUEST (Coordinator, docs): CLAUDE.md's dev command should add `JOBSCRAPER_APPLY_QUEUE=0` (review F4, H2).
For Q-121: write content-failure stops as `content_failure_detail(FILL_…, note)` imported from `apply_flow`; never retype the phrases.
STATUS: open

### 2026-11-12 software-engineer → coordinator, tester
TYPE: handoff
RE: Q-120 review F4, `cmd_web` (cli.py); branch `eng/Q-120-review-fixes` (int/Q-120 57d1d92 merged in), commit 2b8e69b
BODY: `cmd_web` now calls `apply_queue_wanted(os.environ, config_path=getattr(args, "config", None), port=port)`, where `port` is the resolved `cfg.web["port"]` it binds (config `web.port`, default 8765, with `JOBSCRAPER_PORT` applied by `load_config`). `web` has no `--port` argument (argparse exits 2), so only the env var and config `web.port` (via `--config`, which is off anyway) can change the port.
Evidence (stdin script; `uvicorn.run` and `web.api.create_app` patched as in test_apply_queue_cli; `applyqueue._REPO_ROOT` pointed at a temp dir with a `.git` directory so this worktree acts as the main checkout): no args → port 8765, True; `--port 8768` → argparse exit 2; `JOBSCRAPER_PORT=8769` → 8769, False; `--config <temp>` → False; `JOBSCRAPER_APPLY_QUEUE=1` + port 8768 → True; no args with the real worktree root → False.
Tests: `-k apply_queue` 38/41, `-k apply_cli` 23/23, `-k test_cli` 15/15, `-k web_mail` 8/8.
REQUEST (tester), stale pins:
- `test_apply_queue_cli.py::test_cmd_web_passes_the_queue_switch_to_create_app` line 112 `({}, True)`: the call passes `--config`, so it is False now. Use no `--config` (or `JOBSCRAPER_APPLY_QUEUE=1`) for True, and point `applyqueue._REPO_ROOT` (or a `source_dir`) at a non-worktree dir.
- `test_apply_queue_web.py::test_p18_h2_…` line 316 (already reported).
- `test_apply_queue_flow.py::test_content_failure_pauses_fill_failed_not_returned_and_transport_returns` line 254 (from F3, 73b639d): its details "the model gave no usable answers" / "3 answers would be guesses" put the marker mid-text, which F3 no longer matches. Use `content_failure_detail(FILL_NO_USABLE_ANSWERS, …)` and `content_failure_detail(FILL_WOULD_BE_GUESSES, …)` from `apply_flow`.
STATUS: open

### 2026-11-12 software-engineer -> coordinator, lane engineers LA-LE, tester (Q-118 L0): foundation as built
TYPE: handoff
BODY: branch `eng/Q-118-L0-foundation` (from personal af7d7fb). Files: `web/src/style.css`, `web/src/ui/{Segmented,Switch,MenuButton,MoveMenu,KpiCard,ProgressSegments,SortHeader}.vue` (new), `web/src/stages.js`, `web/src/components/Icon.vue`. No other file, no test. Verify: `cd web; npx vitest run` = 45 files, 997 passed | 1 skipped (one earlier run of the unchanged baseline showed 1 flaky failure, the next was green); `npx vite build --outDir %TEMP%/q118-l0-dist` ok. Mounted every component in an in-memory jsdom script (nothing written): 32 checks green (keyboard, emits, aria).
**style.css as built**
- Value changes: `.num` and `input[type=number]` = body font + `tabular-nums lining-nums` (`.mono` split off and keeps `--mono`); `.surface` shadow `--shadow-sm`; `.chip` = `line-height 1.6; padding 2px --space-2; radius --radius-pill`, `--text-xs` and `--text-sm` at >= 641px.
- Tokens: `--text-3xl`, `--leading-tight`, `--weight-medium/semibold/bold` (500/600/650), `--radius-pill`, `--row-h` 2.75rem, `--row-h-compact` 2.25rem, `--motion` 120ms, `--ease-out`, `--stage-withdrawn(-soft)`, `--shadow-md` (light + both dark blocks).
- Classes: `.text-link` · `.ui-card` + `.ui-card-head/-title/-actions`, `.ui-count` · `.ui-grid` + `.span-3/4/5/6/7/8/12` as DIRECT children (`.ui-grid > .span-N`) · `.ui-list` (bleeds to the card edge inside a `.ui-card`), `.ui-list-row`, `.ui-list-row-title/-desc` · `.ui-badge[data-tone=ok|info|warn|danger|neutral]` · `.ui-table` (+ `.col-num/.col-select/.col-action`, `.is-compact`, `[data-stack]`) · `.ui-check` + `.ui-check-box` · `.ui-select` · `.ui-segmented` · `.chip.is-removable` + `.chip-remove`, `.chip.is-suggested`, `button.chip.is-filter[aria-pressed]` · `.ui-bulkbar` · `.ui-drop[data-drop]` · `.ui-skeleton`.
- DOM details to code against: `.ui-check` is `label.ui-check > input[type=checkbox] + span.ui-check-box[aria-hidden] + span`; `.ui-bulkbar` is self-contained (it works with or without `.surface`), its LAST child is laid out as a button row; `.ui-table[data-stack]` at <= 640px: `thead` hidden, `tr` flex-wrap, the select cell and the first other cell share line 1, `td[data-label]::before` leads the rest, `.col-action` goes right; `.ui-skeleton` has `min-height: 1rem` (set the row height yourself); `.ui-segmented` works on plain `button`s too (selected = `aria-pressed|aria-checked|aria-selected = true`).
**Components as built** (all in `web/src/ui/`, scoped styles, theme tokens only)
- `Segmented`: props `modelValue`, `options [{value,label,ariaLabel?,disabled?,title?}]`, `label`; emits `update:modelValue`; `div.ui-segmented[role=radiogroup]` > `button[role=radio][aria-checked]`, roving tabindex, arrows/Home/End move + select, disabled skipped.
- `Switch`: props `modelValue`, `label`, `description`, `disabled`, `busy`; emits `update:modelValue`; `button.ui-switch[role=switch][aria-checked]` named by the label only (the On/Off text and track are `aria-hidden`), `aria-describedby` = description, `aria-busy`; busy ignores clicks but keeps focus; disabled = native `disabled`.
- `MenuButton`: props `items [{id,label,checked?,disabled?,danger?,hint?}]`, `label` (trigger's aria-label), `kind 'menu'|'radio'`, `align 'start'|'end'`, `triggerClass`, `disabled`, `disabledReason`, plus `heading` (ADDED, see below); emits `select(id)`; slot `trigger` (props `{open}`; default = `label`); exposes `focus()`, `close()`. DOM: `span.ui-menu-wrap > button.ui-menu-trigger[aria-haspopup=menu][aria-expanded][aria-controls]` + (only while open) `div.ui-menu-panel > p.ui-menu-heading? + ul.ui-menu[role=menu] > li[role=none] > button.ui-menu-item[role=menuitem|menuitemradio][aria-checked]`. Inline, no teleport. Esc/Tab/outside click/focus-out close; Esc returns focus to the trigger and stops propagation; a pick closes and returns focus to the trigger. A disabled trigger is `aria-disabled` (still focusable), not `disabled`; the reason is a visually hidden span in `aria-describedby` and the `title`. A disabled item has both `disabled` and `aria-disabled` and is skipped by the arrows.
- `MoveMenu`: props `status`, `targets` (default `MOVE_TARGETS`), `name`, `disabled`, `disabledReason`; emits `move(status)`; root has class `ui-move`, trigger class `ui-move-trigger` (pill + chevron icon); name "Move {name}, now {Stage}"; heading "Move to"; current stage checked + disabled.
- `KpiCard`: props `label`, `value` (number|string|null), `caption`, `hint`, `href`; `a.ui-kpi` (with `aria-label` "{label}: {value}, {caption}", `aria-describedby` -> hidden hint) or `div.ui-kpi`; `.ui-kpi-label`, `.ui-kpi-value.num` (absent when value is null/''), `.ui-kpi-caption`.
- `ProgressSegments`: props `segments [{id,label,n,tone,href?,note?,apart?}]` (`note` and `apart` ADDED), `mode 'link'|'filter'`, `modelValue` (chosen id, '' = all), `label`, `allLabel`; emits `update:modelValue`. `tone` = a stage name (`to_apply, applied, assessment, interviewing, offer, rejected, withdrawn, closed`) or `ok|info|warn|danger|neutral`. DOM: `div.ui-progress-wrap > div.ui-progress[aria-hidden] > span.ui-progress-seg[data-tone]` (n = 0 not drawn) + `ul.ui-progress-legend[aria-label] > li > a.ui-progress-item[aria-label="{label}, {n} {note}"]` (link; a `›` span is drawn inside, `aria-hidden`) or `button.ui-progress-item[aria-pressed]` (filter). Filter: the "All" item shows the sum of the `n`; pressing the chosen item again emits ''; unchosen slices dim.
- `SortHeader`: props `label`, `column`, `sort {key,dir:'asc'|'desc'}`, `numeric`; emits `sort(column)` (the caller flips or starts the direction); renders the `th` itself (`th.ui-th[scope=col][aria-sort]` > `button.ui-th-sort`), so use it directly inside `thead > tr`. Chevron hidden until sorted, up = ascending.
**stages.js / Icon.vue**: added `STAGE_TABS`, `MOVE_TARGETS`, `stageOf(status)`, and `stageTabLabel(tab)` (All / Active / `statusLabel`). Icon: `grip`, `more`, `info`, `alert` (Lucide, ISC notice in the file header).
**Interpretations**
1. `LABELS.to_apply` is STILL "Saved". The design makes it "To apply", but the task said not to change anything existing in stages.js and 5 existing tests spell "Saved" (Applications.test.js x3, overview.helpers.test.js, Overview.test.js); with the change vitest showed 5 failed. REQUEST (Coordinator, lanes LB and LA): flip the one word in `web/src/stages.js` `LABELS.to_apply` in the same commit that retargets those tests (the lane engineers cannot edit stages.js).
2. `heading` prop added to MenuButton (the design shows "Move to" but lists no prop); the `p.ui-menu-heading` sits above the `ul`, inside a `div.ui-menu-panel` that carries the absolute position and shadow, because a `p` cannot be a child of a `ul`. The `ul` is named by the trigger.
3. `.span-3` added to the grid (the KPI row uses it); at <= 900px `.span-3` becomes 6 (2x2), the others 12.
4. `.chip.is-suggested` draws no plus of its own: today's `.te-suggestion` buttons already contain an `Icon plus`; render one in the chip (or put "+ " in the text), not both.
5. `.chip-remove`: 24px look, `::after` 44px hit area at <= 640px; the caller puts the `Icon x` inside.
6. Menu items use native `disabled` plus `aria-disabled`, so the current stage in MoveMenu is not focusable (focus lands on the first enabled item instead of the checked one).
7. `.chip` base also affects FieldInput's chips (their scoped styles still override gap, padding-right, font-size).
**Not done / for later**: no visual check on the dev server (8768) was made (not asked in this task; the Coordinator's step 1 visual check of Settings in both themes is still open); the selected `Segmented` option is bolder, which can shift widths by a pixel or two.

### 2026-11-12 software-engineer → coordinator, tester
TYPE: handoff
RE: Q-120 WP-U1 (D-205, D-203; F-30); apply_queue_design §3.5, §7.1; review Q-120 question 9 (b)(c)(d)(e); `web/src/api.js`, `tabs/Inbox.vue`, `components/JobPill.vue`, `components/QuestionsInbox.vue`
BODY: Branch `eng/Q-120-U1-inbox` (from `int/Q-118`), commits 7a38ccb (api.js + Inbox), ab58d87 (JobPill + QuestionsInbox). Coded against the W1 handoff shapes; no server was started and no `POST /api/apply` was sent.
- api.js: `startAutoApply(jobIds)` same body, now documented as the 202 `{queued, accepted, refused, mode, paused, started, disabled, job}`; new `applyQueue()` (GET apply/queue), `removeFromApplyQueue(jobId)` (DELETE apply/queue/{id}, 204 -> null, 404 -> ApiError), `ordinal(n)`, `enqueueMessage(r)` (the shared toast sentence: disabled > paused > "Applying now." (started, one role, position 1) > "Added to the auto-apply queue: 2nd in line." > "Added N to the auto-apply queue."). `retryQuestions` comment updated, same call.
- Inbox: button disabled only when `missing` is non-empty, the status failed to load, a Save/dismiss write is in flight (`busy`), or THIS role's enqueue is in flight (`applying` set per role). `applyState.busy` is no longer read; "Another job is running." is gone. Title and the note under the button: auto "Adds this role to the auto-apply queue. JobScraper fills the form and submits it." / dry_run "... Dry run: the form is filled, never submitted." On click the role is added to `pending` and the selection moves on (same local removal as Save, no status write); on a 202 that queued it: toast (message from `enqueueMessage` + any refusal texts), link "See the queue" (#/runs), action "Undo"; then `quiet`, `emit('changed')`, `window` event `jobscraper:apply-queue`. On 422, a network error, or a 202 that queued nothing: the role is put back and the refusal text / "Could not add this role to the auto-apply queue: ..." shows. Undo (toast or Z) = `DELETE apply/queue/{id}` (404 -> "It has already started; stop it in Runs.", role stays out), then `DELETE applications/{id}` (409 in_progress -> same message; 404 ignored) so the role is New again, then put back + `changed` + the event. Design §7.1 says Undo is the applications DELETE; the task says the queue DELETE; I do both (queue first), because the queue DELETE alone leaves the role tracked as To apply and it would vanish again at the next reload. Not interested: for a role this page queued (`queuedIds`), `DELETE apply/queue/{id}` after the dismissal, 404 ignored; in practice a queued role has already left the list, so this is a safety net.
- JobPill: for kind `apply` and state running, `applyQueue()` at once, then every 10 s, and on the `jobscraper:apply-queue` event; text "Auto-apply · N waiting" or "Auto-apply running" (no elapsed time); a 404 -> "Auto-apply"; other errors keep the last number; the interval is cleared when the job ends or the component unmounts. run/profile pills unchanged.
- QuestionsInbox: retry reads the 202 body: toast = `enqueueMessage` (+ refusals), link "See the queue" (#/runs), event dispatched, then reload. 422 `profile_incomplete` -> "Finish your application details first."; other 422 keeps "Some of those applications can't go again yet..."; anything else "Could not add them to the auto-apply queue: ...". The 409 branch, the `busy` computed, the disabled-when-busy Retry button and the "Another job is running. Retry when it finishes." line are removed (the queue never blocks). The auto-mode confirm panel is unchanged.
- Verified: `npx vitest run` -> 3 failed | 994 passed | 1 skipped (998), all predicted retargets (below); `npx vite build --outDir <temp> --emptyOutDir` -> "built in 1.68s".
TESTER NOTES (old pin -> new expectation), all in `web/tests/QuestionsInbox.test.js` (the fake server answers retry with `{job:{...}}`; give it a `queued` list for the new copy):
- "shows the ready banner with a Retry button ..." (line 258): was 'Started. Dry run: everything is filled in and nothing is sent.'; now `enqueueMessage` of the stub body: with `{queued:[{job_id:'j1',position:1},{job_id:'j2',position:2}], started:false}` -> 'Added 2 to the auto-apply queue.'; with `started:true` -> '... Applying now.'; `paused:true` -> 'Added 2 to the auto-apply queue. The queue is paused; resume it in Runs.'; `disabled:true` -> 'Added to the queue. Auto-apply is switched off in this copy of JobScraper, so nothing will be sent from here.'
- "Q-70 D6: the retry toast links to the Developer tab ..." (line 265): link is now { label: 'See the queue', href: '#/runs' }.
- "a busy server disables Retry and says why; a 409 busy answer says the same" (line 291): replace with: Retry stays enabled when `busy: true`, no 'Another job is running' text anywhere; a 409 is shown as 'Could not add them to the auto-apply queue: ...'; a 422 `profile_incomplete` shows 'Finish your application details first.'
- New pins wanted (no Inbox auto-apply test exists today): Inbox with `GET apply/status {mode, missing: [], busy: true}` -> `.auto-apply` enabled; click -> POST apply `{job_ids:[id]}`, the card gone at once, toast 'Applying now.' (202 `{queued:[{job_id,position:1}], started:true}`) or 'Added to the auto-apply queue: 2nd in line.' (position 2) or the paused/disabled copy, toast link 'See the queue' #/runs, `jobscraper:apply-queue` dispatched; two roles clicked in a row -> two POSTs; Undo -> DELETE apply/queue/{id} then DELETE applications/{id}, role back; DELETE apply/queue 404 -> 'It has already started; stop it in Runs.' and the role stays out; 422 `refused` -> role back + refusal text; `missing` non-empty or status 500 -> button disabled; note text per mode. JobPill: kind apply running with `GET apply/queue {waiting:[a,b]}` -> 'Auto-apply · 2 waiting'; `{waiting:[]}` -> 'Auto-apply running'; 404 -> 'Auto-apply'; fake timers: re-read after 10 s; stops after the job ends / unmount. `applyQueue`, `removeFromApplyQueue`, `ordinal` (1st 2nd 3rd 4th 11th 12th 13th 21st), `enqueueMessage` are plain exports.
KNOWN LIMITS: nothing was exercised in a browser or against the real W1 routes. `startAutoApply` sends no `source` (W1: optional). The queue position in the toast is the server's `position` at enqueue time. U2/U3 listen for `jobscraper:apply-queue`; nothing in U1 listens for it except JobPill.
