Not exercised in a browser (no dev server in this lane).
- Behaviour: tabs `STAGE_TABS` (All first) with counts from `stageCounts` over the run+search filtered rows; `useView('applications', STAGE_TABS, remembered)`; remembered = `rememberedStage(localStorage[STAGE_KEY])` (old to_apply -> active, assessment -> interviewing, withdrawn -> rejected, nothing -> all); `#/applications/<unknown>` -> All (hash rewritten by useView). DEVIATION: a bare `#/applications` while the tab is kept alive goes to the latest picked tab (my hashchange listener), which rewrites the hash to `#/applications/<tab>`; useView alone would go back to the tab remembered at mount. `#/applications/<stage>/<job_id>` scrolls to and focuses that row (`li[tabindex=-1]`). Rows queued or running (`inFlight`: `last_attempt.kind` running|queued or `row.queue` non-null) are pinned on top of All and Active (running, then by `queue.position`), not draggable, MoveMenu disabled with "JobScraper is applying to this role. Stop it in Runs first." Remove's 409 shows `e.body.text` + `hint`. Page sub: "{active} active · {total} tracked at {c} companies". Empty: "Nothing here." + [Show all] (was "Clear filters"; same action, class `clear-filters` kept).
TESTER NOTES (old -> new):
- Strip: order is now All, Active, Applied, Interviewing, Offer, Rejected (`.stages .stage[data-status]`, `.stage-label`, `.stage-n` kept); no `to_apply`/`assessment`/`withdrawn`/config-added (`on_hold`) buttons; assessment counts under Interviewing, withdrawn under Rejected; first visit = `all`. Affects Applications.test "stage strip counts", "offers exactly the statuses config serves", "on_hold ... counted"; applications-flow "Active chip is the default" (All is the default now) and "falls back to Active when remembered stage not offered" (unknown remembered -> All, `withdrawn` -> Rejected, `to_apply` -> Active).
- Status select -> pill: `.app-row[data-job=X] select` is gone. Now `.app-row[data-job=X] .ui-move-trigger` (button, `aria-label` "Move {Role} at {Company}, now {Stage}", text via `.pill[data-status]`); click opens `[role=menu]` with `[role=menuitemradio]` Applied/Interviewing/Offer/Rejected; clicking one POSTs once. The B3 "held keyboard change" tests (applications-flow, 5) have no equivalent: MenuButton commits on Enter/Space/click only, arrows only move focus. Toast unchanged "Moved to {Stage}: {Role}" + Undo; live region `p.app-live[aria-live=polite]` gets "{Role} at {Company} moved from {Old} to {New}." (set after a nextTick: flush, then read); focus returns to that row's `.ui-move-trigger`, or to the active `.stage` button when the row left the tab. Also Applications.test "advancing applied -> interviewing" and "marking applied in the Inbox" (`select.element.value` -> `.ui-move-trigger .pill` text "Applied" or `.app-row[data-status]`).
- Row actions -> ⋯ menu: `.note-edit`, `.details-toggle`, `.row-remove`, `.row-actions`, `.row-actions-rule` are gone. Open `.app-row[data-job=X] .app-more-trigger` (aria-label "More actions for {Role} at {Company}") -> `[role=menuitem]` "Add note"/"Edit note", "Show details"/"Hide details", "Open the posting" (only with a safe url), "Remove from Applications" (only when <= 1 event). Remove confirm keeps `.remove-ask`, `.remove-q`, `.remove-yes`, `.remove-keep`; focus goes to Keep; Keep returns focus to the ⋯ trigger (was the Remove link). Note form keeps `#note-{id}`, `.note-input`, `.note-save`, `.note-cancel` (the textarea now gets focus on open). Detail panel keeps `#app-panel-{id}.app-detail[role=region]`; the menu item has no aria-expanded/aria-controls (q76 "toggle named for its row and wired to its panel" needs a new expectation). Files: q100-setup A13 (5 tests; the touch-target test should read the `.remove-ask` buttons and `.app-more-trigger`), q76-detail (Details 4 + attempts list 5: open via the menu), ux1 notes (3).
- Attempt pill: now inside `.app-row .app-state`, same `.pill.attempt[data-kind]` / `button.attempt-link` for needs_input. EXCEPTION: an `applied` row whose `last_attempt.kind === 'sent'` shows `.app-state .ui-badge.app-auto[data-tone=ok]` "Applied automatically" instead of the pill (q76 "sent reads Sent by auto-apply", "no attempt, no pill", "newest attempt only" use an applied row with `sent`: give that row another status, or expect the badge).
- Sort: `select.app-sort` -> `Segmented` (`.app-sort[role=radiogroup] > button[role=radio]`, "By company" / "Recent"); ux1 "Recent activity puts the latest change first". Recent = one `.company-group.ui-card` with no `data-company` and no header; the company name leads each row's `.row-meta` ("{Company} · {location}") after a `.mark`.
- Groups: `.company-group[data-company]`, `.group-name`, `.group-n` ("2 roles") kept; a `.group-sep` "·" sits between. Page sub `.count` text changed (Applications.test "groups by company": "4 applications at 4 companies" -> "3 active · 4 tracked at 4 companies", whole data set).
- "Saved" -> "To apply" (45222d6): Applications.test lines 62, 90 and "stage labels" (line 214), overview.helpers.test "pipeline counts closed apart" (line 60), Overview.test "counts each open stage" (line 337); the last two overlap lane LA's retarget.
- DnD in jsdom: the row `li.app-row` listens to `dragstart` / `dragend`; each `.stage` button to `dragenter`, `dragover`, `dragleave`, `drop`. dragstart calls `dataTransfer.setData('text/x-jobscraper-job', job_id)` and sets `effectAllowed='move'` (both in try/catch, so a stub may omit them) and keeps the row in component state; dragover/drop need no `dataTransfer` (drop reads `getData('text/x-jobscraper-job')` only when no dragstart was seen). Simulate: `await row.trigger('dragstart', { dataTransfer: { setData() {}, getData() { return '' } } })` -> every tab gets `.ui-drop`, `data-drop="ok"` on the other move targets and `"no"` on the own stage, All and Active; `await tab.trigger('dragover', { dataTransfer })` -> `data-drop="over"`; `await tab.trigger('drop', { dataTransfer })` -> one POST with that stage; `dragend` without a drop announces "Drop cancelled."; dragstart announces "Dragging {Role}. Drop it on a stage tab.". `draggable` is "false" for in-flight rows. Drag is off when `matchMedia('(max-width: 640px), (pointer: coarse)')` matches (jsdom has no matchMedia, so it is on).
FOR Q-120 U3 (the `.app-state` slot): in `ApplicationRow.vue`, `div.app-state[data-kind=last_attempt.kind]` between the title block and the age; inputs: prop `row` (`last_attempt`, `queue`), prop `saving`; helpers `inFlight(row)`, `pinRank(row)` (applications.js), `ordinal` (api.js). Today it renders: "Applied automatically" badge (applied + sent) | `button.attempt-link` for needs_input -> `openProfile('details')` | `span.pill.attempt[data-kind]` with `attemptLabel`, and for `queued` "Queued · {ordinal(queue.position)}" or "Queued". U3 replaces the pill branch for `to_apply` rows with the §3 StateChip states and adds "Try again"; Applications.vue should need no change (emit a new row event if Try again needs the tab).
FOUNDATION: nothing missing. MoveMenu exposes no `focus()`; I focus `.ui-move-trigger` through the component's `$el`.
KNOWN LIMITS: no browser check (the 1440/390 screenshots on 8768 are still to do); the phone tab strip scroll not seen; "Open the posting" in the ⋯ menu uses `window.open(url, '_blank', 'noopener,noreferrer')`.

### 2026-11-12 software-engineer → coordinator, tester
TYPE: handoff
RE: Q-120 WP-U2 (D-205; F-30); apply_queue_design §3.5, §6, §7.1-§7.3; `web/src/components/ApplyQueuePanel.vue` (new), `tabs/Runs.vue`, `tabs/Overview.vue`, `api.js`
BODY: Branch `eng/Q-120-U2-queue-panel` (from `int/Q-118` 59a3a47), commits f2b562b (panel + api.js controls), 3e7e5ce (Runs mount, "Run after this application", deferred message, Overview deferred toast). Coded against the as-built routes in the W1 handoff. No dev server and no queue route was called (the repository is mode auto); the panel was checked by the build and by the existing Runs tests (where the fake API answers 404 and the panel hides itself). I could not write a scratch test (the scope guard refuses it), so the states below are untested at runtime: pins are the tester's.
- **api.js (end block):** `pauseApplyQueue()`, `resumeApplyQueue()`, `clearApplyQueue()` (`{removed, queue}`), `stopApplyAttempt(attemptId)`. `applyQueue()` and `removeFromApplyQueue(jobId)` are U1's.
- **Panel contract:** no props. Emits `status` `{slot, runPending, available}` (only when it changes; an absent queue reports `{slot:'free', runPending:false, available:false}`) and `changed` (no payload) when the slot, the role in progress (`now.attempt_id`), the newest `recent[0].attempt_id` or `run_pending` change (never on the first load). Window event `jobscraper:apply-queue`: it reloads on it (except its own) and dispatches it after its own successful pause/resume/clear/stop/remove. Root is `section.apply-queue` with the attrs Runs passes (`runs-queue ui-card`); `inheritAttrs:false`, so a 404 renders only a comment node.
- **How Runs learns `slot`:** from the panel's `status` event (no second poller). `applyHolds = slot === 'apply'`; `blocked = running && !applyHolds`; the count picker, Dry run switch and the busy row use `blocked`. Label "Run after this application" while `applyHolds` (after "Starting…"). 202 `{deferred:true}`: no `track()`, notice `.notice.ok.run-deferred[role=status]` "The search run starts when the current application finishes." (shown while `run_pending` or deferred+applyHolds), the button is disabled while `run_pending`, and the queue event is dispatched so the panel re-reads. 409 while `applyHolds` or the current job kind is `apply`: "Auto-apply is busy (it may be keeping a dry-run form open). Pause the queue in Runs to free it."; other 409s as before. Overview: a deferred 202 shows that sentence as a toast with "Open Runs" and tracks nothing (its button is still disabled while any job runs, so this is reached only if the server defers).
- **Polling (tester, fake timers `toFake:['setTimeout','clearTimeout']` as Runs.test.js):** first `GET api/apply/queue` on mount; then `setTimeout` 3000 ms when `now || run_pending || slot==='apply' || (waiting && !paused)`, else 15000 ms; none while `document.hidden` (a `visibilitychange` to visible reloads at once); a timer poll is skipped (rescheduled) while a control's request is in flight; stops for good on a 404. Drive with `await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(3000); await flushPromises()`. The elapsed time uses a real `setInterval(1000)` only while `now` exists (not faked by that config).
- **Selectors / accessible names:** header `h3#aq-title` "Auto-apply queue" + `.ui-count` (waiting); header actions `button.aq-pause` "Pause queue" ("Pausing…"), `button.aq-clear` "Clear queue" (only when not paused); note `.aq-note` "The application in progress finishes first." (now, not paused). Skeleton `.aq-skeleton[role=status]` (first load); load error `.notice.error.aq-error[role=alert]` + "Try again"; later poll failure `.aq-stale[role=status]`. Paused: `.notice.warn.aq-paused[role=status]` with `.aq-paused-text` (strong), `.aq-paused-hint`, `.aq-paused-count` "{n} waiting", `button.primary.aq-resume` "Resume" (absent when `paused.reason==='disabled'`), `button.aq-clear` (when something waits). Confirm: `div.aq-confirm[role=group]` text "Remove all {n} waiting roles from the queue? They stay in Applications under To apply." (n=1: "Remove the waiting role from the queue? It stays in Applications under To apply."), `button.aq-confirm-yes` "Clear queue", `button.aq-keep` "Keep them" (focused on open; Esc keeps and returns focus to `.aq-clear`). Result line `p.notice.aq-feedback` (`.ok` role=status / `.error` role=alert): "Removed {n} role(s) from the queue. They stay in Applications under To apply." / "Stopped. The queue is paused." / "That application had already finished. The queue is paused." / resume 409 disabled "Auto-apply is switched off in this copy of JobScraper (JOBSCRAPER_APPLY_QUEUE=0)." / resume 422 "Your application details are missing: {paths}. Fill them in under Profile → Application details, then resume." / remove 404 "{Company · Role} had already started or was no longer waiting, so it was not removed."
  - Now applying: `h4#aq-now-title`, then the only live region `div.aq-live[aria-live=polite]` > `ul.ui-list.aq-now-list` > `li.aq-now`: `.aq-name` "{Company} · {Role}", `StateChip` (`.state-chip[data-tone=checking]`, role img, name = the state word: Starting, Preparing the resume, Reading the form, Opening the site, Signing in, Creating an account, Checking email, Filling the form, Submitting; unknown states are sentence-cased), `.aq-elapsed` (aria-hidden, so the live region says only the step), `.ui-badge.aq-dry` "Dry run", `button.aq-stop` "Stop this application" ("Stopping…"). `interrupted`: chip "Interrupted" (`data-tone=attention`) + `.aq-interrupted` "Interrupted. JobScraper closes it when auto-apply next starts."; no Stop.
  - Lines: `.aq-run-pending` "A search run starts when this application finishes. The queue continues after it." (`run_pending`); `.aq-hold` "Waiting for the search run to finish. Auto-apply starts right after." (slot run) / "Waiting for the current job to finish." (slot other), only when something waits and the queue is not paused.
  - Up next: `h4#aq-next-title[tabindex=-1]` "Up next" + `.ui-count` {n}; `ol.aq-next` > `li.aq-item[data-job]`: `.aq-name` "{i}. {Company} · {Role}" (i = list index + 1), `.aq-added` "added {ago}", `.aq-dry`, `.aq-back` "Back at the front: {note}" (when `note`), `button.ghost.aq-remove` text "Remove", `aria-label` "Remove {Company} {Role} from the queue". After a removal focus goes to the Remove now at that index, else the "Up next" heading. Empty: `.aq-empty` "Nothing waiting. Choose Auto-apply on a role in the Inbox to add it here."
  - Recently finished: `ul.aq-recent` > `li.aq-done`: `.aq-name`, `.ui-badge.aq-outcome` (`attemptLabel(kind, mode)`; tones sent ok, dry_run info, unconfirmed/needs_input/stopped warn, else neutral; title = the attempt `text`), `.aq-when` "{ago}", `a.text-link.aq-open` "Open in Applications" `#/applications/all/<job_id>`. Empty: "Finished applications show here."
  - Whole panel empty (no paused, now, waiting, recent or run_pending): `.empty.aq-empty-all` "No applications queued. Choose Auto-apply on roles in the Inbox; JobScraper applies to them one at a time in the background while you keep browsing."
- **vitest** `cd web; npx vitest run`: 11 failed | 986 passed | 1 skipped (45 files; 4 files red). Classified: the 8 known Runs retargets (Runs.test.js 3: "sends the dry-run toggle and a changed company count", "lists date, companies, postings, matches and status per run; 0 matches says so", "the picker: tick runs, see the matches, show them in the Inbox"; q75-pages.test.js 3: "a successful run has no extra note", "an aborted run says why, in the pill's title and for screen readers", "counts get thousands separators, in the table and the failed link"; ui.test.js 2: "All sends every enabled company", "estimates how long a run takes from recent runs") + the 3 QuestionsInbox.test.js ones ("Q-70 D6: the retry toast links to the Developer tab to follow the run", "a busy server disables Retry and says why; a 409 busy answer says the same", "shows the ready banner with a Retry button that posts the ready job ids, no confirm in dry run"). Nothing new red. q75-shell.test.js "g then a letter..." failed once in the first full run (waitFor timeout under load, the known flake from LC's note), green alone and in the second full run.
- **Verify:** `npx vite build --outDir C:/Users/Admin/AppData/Local/Temp/q120-u2-dist --emptyOutDir` -> `built in 2.45s`.
- **Interpretations:** (1) "active" polling = something is going on (see Polling), not a prop: Runs is not kept alive, so the panel exists only while the tab is open. (2) `Up next ({n})` is drawn as the heading plus a `.ui-count` pill, so its text reads "Up next 2". (3) Pause/Clear sit in the card header when running and move into the Paused banner when paused (no duplicate controls). (4) The singular confirm and the "Removed {n} role(s)" line are my wording (the design gives only the plural confirm). (5) No `window.confirm`. (6) On a first-load failure other than 404 the card shows the error with Try again; a 404 shows nothing.
- **Missing primitive:** none. The Remove/Stop/Resume buttons use the global `button`, `.ghost`, `.primary` styles; the danger colour on "Stop this application" and the confirm's "Clear queue" is a scoped rule copied from Runs' `.cancel`. Not seen on a dev server or at 390px (no visual check made).
STATUS: open

### 2026-11-12 software-engineer → coordinator, tester
TYPE: handoff
RE: Q-120 WP-U3 (D-205, D-207); apply_queue_design §3.5/§7.1 Applications; q118_ui_polish_design §3; `web/src/attempts.js`, `web/src/components/ApplicationRow.vue`
BODY: Branch `eng/Q-120-U3-app-states` (from `int/Q-118` 7f54b7c), commit c130682 (+ this entry). Two source files only. No server was started and no `POST /api/apply` was called; untested at runtime (the scope guard refuses tests): pins are the tester's.
- **attempts.js (new exports):** `rowState(row)` -> `{kind, label, tone, title, step, reason, icon}` or null; `canRetry(row)`; `refusalMessage(body)` (the Inbox's 422 wording, re-created here because Inbox.vue keeps its own copy: the Coordinator can have the Inbox import it); `STEP_WORDS`; `IN_PROGRESS_REMOVE`. `enqueueMessage` already lives in `api.js` (exported), so the row imports it: no copy. `attemptLabel`/`modeLabel`/`needsInput` are unchanged in wording (the attempt LIST keeps "Sent by auto-apply", "Auto-apply stopped"...; `queued` added -> "Queued").
- **State table (row `.app-state > span.ui-badge.app-chip[data-kind][data-tone]`):** `row.queue` present and not running -> `queued` neutral "Queued · {ordinal(queue.position)}" ("Queued" without a position) · `running` info "Applying…" + muted `span.app-step` from `last_attempt.state` (Preparing the resume / Reading the form / Opening the site / Signing in / Creating an account / Checking email / Filling the form / Submitting; none for other states) · `sent` ok "Sent by auto-apply", but on an `applied` row (`appliedAutomatically`) ok "Applied automatically" with the check icon (`.app-auto`) · `unconfirmed` warn "Sent, not confirmed" · `dry_run` info "Dry run ready" · `needs_input` warn "Needs your answers" + `a.button.ghost.app-act.app-answer` "Answer in Profile" (`href="#/profile"`, click -> `openProfile('details')`) · `stopped` warn "Stopped: {text clipped to 60 chars}" ("Stopped" when no text), full text + hint in `title` · `cancelled` neutral "Cancelled" + muted `span.app-reason` (the text, clipped) · unknown kind neutral `attemptLabel`. Chip `title` = text + hint when the attempt has them.
- **Stale rule:** `stopped`, `cancelled`, `needs_input` show nothing once the role's status is not `to_apply` (the owner moved it on); the other kinds show on any status.
- **Actions:** `a.button.ghost.app-act.app-open` "Open the posting" (stopped and cancelled, only with a safe URL, new tab, noopener). `button.app-act.app-retry` "Try again" (text "Adding…" and disabled while in flight, and while `saving`) shown when `canRetry(row)`: kind in stopped / cancelled / needs_input AND status `to_apply` AND not queued/running. needs_input always offers it (the row cannot tell whether the answers are in; the server refuses with its text).
- **Try again:** `startAutoApply([job_id])`. Success (`queued` has the id): the toast `enqueueMessage` (+ refused texts), action "Undo" (`DELETE /api/apply/queue/{id}`; 404 -> toast "It has already started; stop it in Runs."), link "See the queue" `#/runs`; then `window` event `jobscraper:apply-queue`. The row also shows "Queued · {n}" at once (local `justQueued`, cleared when the row's attempt id / kind / queue place changes) and its Move menu and drag go off. Not queued -> the refused texts or `refusalMessage(r)`. 422 -> `refusalMessage(e.body)` ("Finish your application details first." for profile_incomplete, else the refusals' text + hint). Other errors -> "Could not add this role to the auto-apply queue: {error}".
- **Remove on a role being applied to:** the row does not make the DELETE (the tab does). In the row, the "Remove from Applications" item on a `running` row shows the toast "JobScraper is applying to this role right now. Stop it in Runs first." and emits nothing. A server 409 for any other case is the tab's existing `removeRow` catch (shows `body.text` + `body.hint`).
- **NEEDED IN Applications.vue (I may not edit it):** (1) it does not refresh on `jobscraper:apply-queue` nor poll. Add next to `onActivated`: `const REFRESH_EVENT = 'jobscraper:apply-queue'`; `const onQueue = () => load()`; `onMounted(() => window.addEventListener(REFRESH_EVENT, onQueue))`; `onBeforeUnmount(() => { window.removeEventListener(REFRESH_EVENT, onQueue); clearInterval(poll) })`; and a 10 s timer while any row is queued or applying: `let poll = null; watch(rows, r => { const live = r.some(inFlight); if (live && !poll) poll = setInterval(() => { if (!document.hidden) load() }, 10000); else if (!live && poll) { clearInterval(poll); poll = null } }, { immediate: true })` (`inFlight` from `../applications.js`; `load()` should not blank the list while reloading). (2) In `removeRow`'s catch, the 409 fallback when the body has no text: `e?.status === 409 ? IN_PROGRESS_REMOVE` (import from `../attempts.js`) instead of `describeError(e)`. Until (1) lands the row still shows "Queued" after Try again (local state) but not the later Applying / Applied changes.
- **Tester pins:** chips `.app-row[data-job=X] .app-state .app-chip` (`data-kind`, `data-tone`, text per table); `.app-step`, `.app-reason`, `.app-answer`, `.app-open`, `.app-retry`; the old `.pill.attempt` and `button.attempt-link` are gone from the row (the attempt LIST in the details panel still uses `.pill.attempt` and `attemptLabel`). 44px: `.app-act` is `--touch-min` high at <= 640px. Try-again conditions as above; with `justQueued` set the Move trigger is disabled with LOCKED_REASON.
- **vitest** `cd web; npx vitest run`: 58 failed | 939 passed | 1 skipped; the same run on the unchanged `int/Q-118` tree: 50 failed. NEW red (8, all q76-detail.test.js "the auto-apply pill on a row", they read the old row pill; retarget to the chip): "running reads Auto-apply running..." (now "Applying…"), "unconfirmed reads...", "dry_run reads...", "cancelled reads..." (now "Cancelled"), "stopped reads..." (now "Stopped" + text), "\"Needs your input\" is a button with the reason in its title" (now a span chip "Needs your answers" + the link "Answer in Profile"), "clicking it remembers Application details and goes to Profile" and "end to end in the app: the click lands..." (click `.app-answer`, an `a`). The other 50 are the old reds (Applications-area retargets, Runs, QuestionsInbox, Overview), identical lists in both runs.
- **Verify:** `npx vite build --outDir C:/Users/Admin/AppData/Local/Temp/q120-u3-dist --emptyOutDir` -> `built in 1.19s`.
- **Known limits:** no browser check at 1440/390; the `.app-state` column is capped at `min(34rem, 50vw)` (none at <= 640px) and the chip wraps long reasons. "Submitted · N answers assumed" (llm_first_fill §6) is not built: the slot wraps, so a second chip fits.
STATUS: open
- **U3 follow-up (Applications.vue, commit below):** the tab reloads quietly on the window event `jobscraper:apply-queue` and, via `setInterval(10000)`, while any row is `inFlight` (queued or running); the interval starts/stops with the rows (`watch(rows)`), is off while the tab is deactivated or unmounted, skips ticks while `document.hidden`, and the reload waits during the first load, a status save or a drag (`following` guards overlap). `removeRow`: a 409 with no `body.text` now reads `IN_PROGRESS_REMOVE`.
- **Tester (poll):** fake timers `toFake:['setInterval','clearInterval']`; mount with one row whose `last_attempt.kind` is `queued` (or `row.queue={position:1}`), then `await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(10000); await flushPromises()` -> one more `GET api/applications` (and `api/stats`); with no queued/running row no timer runs; `window.dispatchEvent(new Event('jobscraper:apply-queue'))` reloads at once.

### 2026-11-12 software-engineer -> coordinator (Q-118 wave-1 touch fixes)
TYPE: handoff
BODY: Branch `eng/Q-118-wave1-touch-fixes` (from `int/Q-118` dde8d43). Phone-width CSS plus one watch. ApplicationRow at <= 640px: `.app-row .app-move :deep(.ui-move-trigger)` and `.app-more :deep(.app-more-trigger)` get min-height and min-width `--touch-min`; `a.role-title` gets min-height `--touch-min` (inline-flex, so the text size does not change). Runs at <= 640px: `.failed-link` gets min-height and min-width `--touch-min` (its `::after` hit area stays). Applications: the open stage tab scrolls into view (`scrollIntoView({block:'nearest', inline:'nearest'})`) on load and on each tab change. The strip already scrolls sideways (`overflow-x:auto`, scroll-snap), and each `.stage` is `--touch-min` tall at <= 640px; I checked this in the CSS, not on a device. In the history table the run number is plain text, not a link; the only linked number is the Failed count. `.ui-check` at <= 640px (style.css) is `min-height: --touch-min` with padding-inline (`--touch-min` - 1.1rem)/2, and the input fills the label (`inset:0`), so the checkbox hit area is 44px tall and about 44px wide. No style.css change needed.
Verify: `npx vitest run` -> 58 failed | 939 passed | 1 skipped, the same count as `int/Q-118` without these changes (I ran both); `npx vite build --outDir C:/Users/Admin/AppData/Local/Temp/q118-touch-dist --emptyOutDir` -> "built in 1.33s"; the compiled CSS contains all three rules.
STATUS: open

### 2026-11-12 software-engineer → coordinator, tester
TYPE: handoff + request
RE: Q-118 LA (Overview); `web/src/tabs/Overview.vue`, `web/src/overview.js`; design `docs/architecture/q118_ui_polish_design.md` §2
BODY: Branch `eng/Q-118-LA-overview` (from `int/Q-118`), commit 0d069cd. Only Overview.vue and overview.js changed. No endpoint added. Vite build to a temp dir OK (`✓ built in 2.51s`). `npx vitest run`: 80 red on the branch vs 58 red on `int/Q-118` itself (the 58 belong to other lanes, e.g. "To apply" label, Applications strip); NEW reds from this lane: 21, all predicted retargets (below). Not visually checked in a browser (no dev server run); a scratch jsdom smoke (deleted) confirmed the DOM below.
WHAT CHANGED
- Layout: header (no run line any more; the Last run card carries it, per §2) -> SetupChecklist (`<SetupChecklist :runs :runs-known with-apply />`, unchanged) -> KPI row `div.ui-grid.ov-kpis[role=group]` of 4 `KpiCard.span-3` (`[data-kpi=new|active|interviews|reply]`) -> `div.ui-grid` with `div.ov-col.span-8` (To do, Pipeline) and `div.ov-col.span-4` (Last run, Past 7 days). Loading: `div.ov-stack[role=status][aria-busy=true]` with 4 KPI + 2 card `.ui-skeleton`s (the old `.overview [role=status][aria-busy]` hook still works).
- KPIs: New roles = `newRoleCount(jobs)`, "in your Inbox", href `#/inbox`. Active applications = rows not rejected/withdrawn (to_apply included), "Not rejected or withdrawn", `#/applications/active`. Interviews = interviewing + assessment rows, caption "{n} offer(s)" or "No offers yet", `#/applications/interviewing`. Response rate = `responseRate()` (rule unchanged: applied rows = status or any event in applied-or-later; heard = moved to a later stage or rejected after applying), value "{rate}%", caption "{heard} of {applied} heard back", hint (title + hidden text) "An interview, offer or rejection after you applied counts as a reply."; none sent: value null, caption "No applications sent yet."; no href. If `/api/applications` failed: the three application KPIs show caption "Unavailable".
- Pipeline: `ProgressSegments` link mode, label "Applications by stage"; legend links `#/applications/applied|interviewing|offer|rejected`, aria-label "{Label}, {n}" (Rejected: "Rejected, 2 (1 withdrawn)"); Rejected is `apart`, tone `closed` (muted). Count pill "{total} tracked" (= all rows). Stage mapping via `stageOf`: assessment -> Interviewing, withdrawn -> Rejected, `to_apply` in no segment: when > 0 a muted line "Also waiting to be applied to: {n} to apply" links `#/applications/active` (my addition: without it the bar does not add up to "tracked"; drop it if unwanted). Empty: "Nothing tracked yet. Save a role in the Inbox and it shows up here."
- To do (`section.ui-card` + `ul.ui-list`, rows `li.ui-list-row.todo-row[data-kind=questions|inbox|follow-up|run]`, `.failed` on a failed run): first row's action `.primary`, the rest default (no `.ghost`). Titles: "{n} question(s) waiting for your answer", "Triage {n} new role(s)", "Follow up on {n} application(s)" (button "Review" -> `#/applications/applied`; sub-list `ul.todo-sub` "Company · Role" + "{n} days", "and {k} more"), "Your last run failed" [Open Runs], "Your last run was {n} days ago" [Start a run] (error `p.notice.error.todo-error`). Caught up: `.ov-caught-up` "You're all caught up." Questions error: `.notice.warn` with a plain "Try again" button.
- Last run card: `.ui-badge` (Success/Failed/Aborted/Running via runs.js title/tone/icon) + ago; "{n} new roles · {c} companies checked" (`stats.companies_due`, omitted when absent); reason line for failed/aborted; [Open Runs] `a.button.ghost`; no run: "No runs yet."; running: badge "Running" + "Follow it on the Runs tab." Past 7 days: `dl.week-stats > .week-stat > dt + dd.num` labels "New roles found", "To apply" (still `week.saved`), "Applied", "Other updates"; zeros muted; quiet-week text unchanged.
- overview.js: `DEFAULT_STAGES` = applied, interviewing, offer; new `PIPELINE_STAGES`; `pipeline(rows)` now returns `{ segments:[{id,label,n}] (applied, interviewing, offer, rejected via stageOf), toApply, assessment, rejected, withdrawn, total }` (second argument dropped); new `kpis(rows, jobs, statuses)` -> `{ newRoles, active, interviews, offers, reply }`; `openStages`, `responseRate`, `pastWeek`, `followUps`, `lastRunState`, `nextUp` unchanged. Deferred-run toast kept.
TESTER NOTES (retarget; I did not touch tests)
- `Overview.test.js` (18 new reds, all selector/copy): `.next-card[data-kind]` -> `.todo-row[data-kind]`; `.next-title` -> `.ui-list-row-title`; `.next-body` -> `.ui-list-row-desc`; "Triage N new roles" (was "N new roles to triage"); "Follow up on N application(s)" (was "N application(s) to follow up"); `.follow-list li` -> `.todo-sub li` ("Co late · Role late" + "19 days" as two spans); follow-up href `#/applications/applied` (was `#/applications`); `.ghost` no longer on non-first actions; headings are `h3.ui-card-title` ("To do", was "Next up"); `.run-line` gone (Last run card instead); `.stage-tiles/.stage-tile/.tile-n/.response/.pipe-link` -> `.ui-progress-legend a` + `.ov-foot` + KPI `[data-kpi=reply]`; `.week-tile` -> `.week-stat` (label "To apply", was "Saved"); TabLoading text -> check `.ui-skeleton`; "Next up" text no longer exists; the failed-applications test now sees two `.notice.error` (Pipeline, Past 7 days) in cards, still with "Try again".
- `overview.helpers.test.js` (3 new reds): `openStages` fallback is now three stages; `pipeline` result shape as above (no `stages`/`closed`).
- Suggested pins: KPI hrefs/values; legend hrefs; assessment counted in Interviewing, withdrawn in Rejected with "(1 withdrawn)"; `SetupChecklist` still mounted with `with-apply`; response rate "75%" / "3 of 4 heard back" for the fixture rows in my smoke (6 rows incl. an assessment).
REQUESTS: (1) SetupChecklist: optional restyle as `.ui-card` (it is `.setup.surface` today, so it already matches in size and sits above the KPI row). (2) ProgressSegments: only the legend items are links, the coloured bar slices are not (the bar is `aria-hidden`); if the owner wants the bar itself clickable, the primitive needs `a` slices. No other request.
STATUS: open

### 2026-11-12 software-engineer -> coordinator, tester (Q-118 LD): Companies tab
TYPE: handoff
RE: Q-118 design §5, §7, §8, §10, §12 (A4); owner item 2
BODY: Branch `eng/Q-118-LD-companies` (from `int/Q-118`), commits 4240825 (companyRows.js + Companies.vue + WatchedCompanies.vue), fb9be46 (More menu trigger slot, 44px Clear filters on the phone). Files: `web/src/companyRows.js` (new), `web/src/tabs/Companies.vue`, `web/src/components/WatchedCompanies.vue`. `companyFilter.js` unchanged (the Runs "N failed" link still calls `requestCompanyFilter('failed')`; the tab maps it to the Failed segment). No endpoint added, no test, no static bundle. Not checked in a browser (no dev server); checked by a throw-away jsdom run (8 checks, deleted) and the Vite build.
- **Verify:** `npx vite build --outDir C:/Users/Admin/AppData/Local/Temp/q118-ld-dist --emptyOutDir` -> "built in 1.23s". `cd web; npx vitest run` -> 58 failed | 974 passed | 1 skipped (1033). 35 are this lane's predicted retargets (below); the other 23 are the Overview reds already on `int/Q-118` (Overview.test.js, overview.helpers.test.js). One earlier run also showed `q75-shell.test.js` "g then a letter" red once (the known timing flake; green on the next run).
- **Data:** `GET /api/companies/health` (`items[]`, `total`), `GET /api/companies/list` (`items[]`), `GET /api/settings` (`cycle_days`, a new call in the tab; if it fails, `cycleDays` is null). Nothing missing from the API.
- **Stages (companyRows.js).** Health rows are taken whole; only list items with status `failed` / `pending|searching|over_limit` become extra rows (found/watched are already health rows), so nobody is counted twice. Buckets: `checked` = state ok and `now - last_scan <= cycle_days*86400 s` (edge inclusive); `due` = ok and older, or ok with no `last_scan`; `readable` = ok when `cycle_days` is unknown (replaces checked + due); `failed` = state failed; `never` = state not_scanned; `not_found` = list status failed; `waiting` = list pending/searching/over_limit. Funnel: `list` = list items, `watched` = `health.total`, `readable` = checked+due+readable, `checked` = checked bucket (stage dropped when `cycle_days` is unknown; "in your list" dropped when there is no list). Legend order: Checked this cycle, Due for a check, Failed, Never checked (only > 0), No job board found, Waiting for search (only > 0); "All" item first (ProgressSegments `all-label`).
- **Helpers (pure, `web/src/companyRows.js`):** `partition(health, list, cycleDays=null, now=Date.now())` -> `{checked,due,readable,failed,never,not_found,waiting}` arrays of row models; `rows(health, list, cycleDays, now)` flat; `bucketOf(item, cycleDays, now)`; `countsOf(buckets)`; `funnelOf(health, list, counts, cycleKnown)` -> `{list,watched,readable,checked|null}`; `segmentsOf(counts, cycleKnown)`; `bucketFromState(state)` ('failed'->'failed', 'not_scanned'->'never', a bucket id as is, else ''); `nextSort(sort, column)`, `comparator(sort)`, `sorted(rows, sort)` (unknown roles/times sort last either way; ties by name), `DEFAULT_SORT = {key:'company', dir:'asc'}` (columns company | roles | checked; roles and checked start descending); `matchesQuery(row, q)`; `stampMs`, `boardName`, `causeOf`, `CAUSES`, `BUCKETS`. Row model: `{key, company, board, boardId, openRoles, lastChecked, checkedMs, bucket, badge, tone, reason, kind, paused, url, source, title}`.
- **DOM to retarget against.** Tab: `section.companies-tab`; `header.page-head` with `p.page-sub`; `.page-controls` > `button.edit-list` ("Edit list" / "Type or paste names"), `button.upload-list`, `button.primary.search-now`, MenuButton trigger `button.more-actions` (aria-label "More list actions"; items "Download found (.txt)", "Download not found (.txt)" (file `companies-failed.txt`, unchanged), "Full report (.csv)", "All watched companies (.csv)" (programmatic click on `api/companies/export.csv`), "Clear list…" (danger)); one hidden `input.file[type=file]` in `.page-controls`; `div.notice.warn.clear-ask[role=group]` with `.confirm-clear` and `.keep-list` (Keep focused first; Keep/Esc returns focus to `.more-actions`); `form.list-editor.ui-card` (editor unchanged: `.save-list`, `.cancel-list`, `textarea#list-text`); first run (no list, `health.total` 0): `button.drop` + `.empty` "No list yet."; drop overlay `div.drop-overlay.ui-drop[data-drop=over]` "Drop to replace your list" (drag events on the section; a drop anywhere calls `take`). Table (WatchedCompanies): `.watched` > `p.funnel` (`.funnel-step`, `.funnel-n.num`, `.funnel-arrow`), ProgressSegments filter ("Show companies"; legend `button.ui-progress-item[aria-pressed]`), `.toolbar` (`input.company-search`, `button.density`), cause chips only in the Failed view (`button.chip.is-filter.cause[data-cause]`, `.cause-fix`), `p.result-line` + `button.clear-filters`, `section.ui-card.table-card > table.ui-table.companies-table[data-stack]` (`th.ui-th[aria-sort]` with `button.ui-th-sort` for Company, Open roles, Last checked), `tbody tr[data-bucket]` > `td.co-cell .co-name`, `td[data-label=Job board]`, `td.col-num`, `td[data-label=Last checked]` (title = exact UTC), `td.status-cell > span.ui-badge[data-tone] + span.status-note`, `td.col-action > a.button.ghost.careers[aria-label="Careers page for {company}"]`. Compact = `table.is-compact`.
- **Retargets (35, all old selectors):** m16.test.js x10 (every "Companies tab" test clicks `.view-list` and reads `.list .row`, `.filter`, `.dl-found`, `.clear`, `.summary` stats: now no toggle, rows in the table, filters = the legend, downloads and Clear list in the More menu); m17.test.js x1 ("shows how many can be scanned and links the download": `.tile` x3 and `a.health-csv` are gone: legend/funnel and the menu item); q116-settings.test.js x1 (density: `.dir` + `compact` -> `table.companies-table` + `is-compact`; the `.density` button is unchanged); q75-pages.test.js x10 (Clear list in place x4: open More, "Clear list…", focus returns to `.more-actions`; "one set of counts (G25)" x2: stat tiles gone; WatchedCompanies G30 x4: A-Z strip, "your list" source chip and the source select are gone, Compact toggle -> `is-compact`); ui.test.js x9 (Companies tab x3: toggle and remembered view removed, the Runs request -> Failed legend button `aria-pressed`; Watched companies x6: letter headings, `.co` rows -> table rows, `.tile[data-state]` -> legend button, `select.sort`/`select.source` -> SortHeader / removed, `a.health-csv` -> menu item, `.why-text` details -> `.status-note` + `.cause`); ux1.test.js x4 (type or paste names: only the `.view-list` click goes; editor classes unchanged). Kept: the density key + event, `input.company-search` (and the `/` focus), `.cause`/`.cause-fix`, `.result-line`, the upload/search/clear calls and messages.
- **Pins to add (design §10):** no source select and no view toggle; funnel sentence numbers (with and without `cycle_days`, with and without a list); each legend item shows exactly its bucket; `partition()` cases (cycle edge at exactly `cycle_days`, `cycle_days` null merges into Readable, a found list name is not counted twice, waiting vs not_found); sort by Open roles (desc first, unknown last, `aria-sort`); first-run drop zone only with nothing watched and no list; More menu items and the Clear list confirm; drop overlay text.
- **Interpretations:** (1) the per-row `<details>` (error, failures in a row, last worked) is gone: the reason is the second line and the rest sits in the badge `title` (the design keeps the cause chips and the "What you can do" note, not the details). (2) "Download not found" keeps the file name `companies-failed.txt`. (3) No "your list" source marker is shown (the source filter goes; the design has no marker). (4) The first-run drop zone is a `button` that opens the shared hidden file input, not a `label` with its own input. (5) `Last checked` for a not-found name is its `searched_at`. (6) The page sub shows the plan cap only when the plan has one; the "no limit" wording is gone. (7) Clear list needs the tab idle (the menu item is disabled while a job runs), as the old button was.
- **Not done / look first in the browser:** 1440 and 390 screenshots (none taken); the 390px stacked row (company + Careers icon on line 1, then board, roles, last checked, then the status badge and its reason); `.table-card` has `overflow-x: auto` on desktop; the More menu opens inline under the trigger at the right edge of the head.

### 2026-11-12 software-engineer → coordinator, tester
TYPE: handoff + request
RE: Q-118 lane LE (Profile, "Resume and titles"); branch `eng/Q-118-LE-profile` (from `int/Q-118`), code commit 9e79580; files `web/src/tabs/Profile.vue`, `web/src/components/TitleEditor.vue` only
BODY: Built per design §6. Hash views, `useView`, the tablist roles/ids (`#profile-tab-*`, `#profile-panel-*`), the counts label and every deep link are unchanged; the tablist now carries `.ui-segmented` (full width at <= 640px). Resume present: one compact `section.ui-card.ui-drop.resume-card` "From your resume" ("read {when}" with `.parsed` + exact `title`), actions `button.replace` "Replace resume" (clicks the hidden `input[type=file]`) and `button.refresh` "Re-read resume" (same `refreshOnly`), `dl.facts` (`.experience` / `.graduation`, `dt` label + `dd` value), summary clamped to 3 lines with `button.ghost.summary-toggle` "Show more/less" (`aria-expanded`; shown only when the summary is over 160 chars, since jsdom cannot measure lines), fine print "PDF or DOCX, up to 5 MB. Profile version v · read by m" (`.version`, `.parsed-by`). No file name, no big drop zone; the card is the drop target (`data-drop=over`, overlay "Drop to replace your resume"). Before the first resume: `label.drop.ui-drop` zone + "No profile yet." + `button.refresh` "Re-read resume". Skills `section.ui-card.skills-card` (span-6): `.ui-count.skill-count` = total, `ul.chips.skills > li.chip` (body font, first 12), `button.ghost.more-skills` "Show all {n}" / "Show fewer" (`aria-expanded`), Interests `h4` + `ul.chips.interests > li.chip` (or "None set..."), caption "From your resume. Re-read it to update them." The titles `<ul>` and the "N skills / N job titles" lines are gone. TitleEditor is a `section.ui-card` (span-6 via a class from Profile; span-12 when no profile): `h3.ui-card-title.te-question`, `.ui-count.te-count`, chips `li.chip.is-removable.te-chip` with `button.chip-remove.te-remove`, "Reset to the titles..." is `button.ghost.te-reset`, "Recommended titles" is an `h4.te-sub`, [Recommend titles] is no longer `.primary`, suggestions are `ul.te-suggestions > li > button.chip.is-suggested.te-suggestion` (classes `.te-s-title`, `.te-s-why` kept; a visually-hidden "Add " precedes the title). All other `te-*` classes, texts, limits and API calls unchanged. Loading = `.ui-skeleton` cards (role=status, "Loading your profile…" / "Loading titles…"); a profile error keeps the notice + Try again and TitleEditor still shows.
EVIDENCE: `cd web; npx vitest run` -> 30 failed | 1002 passed (1033): the 23 red Overview/overview.helpers tests already red on int/Q-118 plus 7 NEW, all predicted retargets (below). `npx vite build --outDir C:/Users/Admin/AppData/Local/Temp/q118-le-dist --emptyOutDir` -> "built in 2.33s" (Profile chunk 83.34 kB). Not looked at in a browser (no screenshots taken).
TESTER, new reds and the retarget (old -> new):
- Profile.test.js `shows summary, skills, target titles, interests, version and source`: drop `.source` ('resume.pdf') and the `.titles li` expectation; `.parsed`, `.parsed-by`, `.version`, `.profile .summary`, `.skills li`, `.interests li` still hold. Pin: card text does not contain "resume.pdf"; no `ul.titles`.
- Profile.test.js `a dropped DOCX works too...` and `the drop zone and picker are disabled while a job runs`: the `drop()` helper finds `.drop`; with a profile present the drop target is `.resume-card` (`.drop` exists only with no profile). The disabled test's other asserts hold (`input[type=file]` disabled, `button.refresh` disabled).
- q44-refresh.test.js `says what the resume gave in plain lines...`: `h2.section-title` "What we read from your resume" is gone (card title is `h3.ui-card-title` "From your resume"); `.read-line` is now "read 24 Sep 18:05" without the file name; `.experience` / `.graduation` text is "ExperienceLess than a year" style (dt + dd, no colon); `.skill-count` moved to the Skills card (text = the number); `.title-count` gone. `.version`, `.parsed-by`, `.summary` still inside `.resume-card`.
- q44-refresh.test.js `experience and graduation...` (`.experience` text, see above) and `singular wording` (`.skill-count` = "1"; `.title-count` removed).
- q44-refresh.test.js `shows the first 12 skills; "and k more"...`: `.more-skills` is now a `button.ghost` "Show all 15" (the total, not the remainder); it STAYS after expanding and reads "Show fewer" (`aria-expanded` true/false); 12 -> 15 -> 12 `.skills li`.
New pins suggested: `button.replace` opens the file input; "Show more" appears only for a summary over 160 chars and toggles `aria-expanded`; `.ui-drop[data-drop=over]` on dragenter plus the overlay text; skeleton `role=status` while loading; Skills card absent with no profile and TitleEditor spanning 12.
REQUESTS (outside my files): (1) `components/ResumeRefresh.vue` copy still says `choose "Re-derive profile from the stored resume"` (two notices, ~lines 157 and 160; q44-refresh `rr-stale` pins the sentence): the button is now "Re-read resume" per the design, so change the copy to `choose "Re-read resume"` and its test. (2) ResumeRefresh's own chips (`.rr-chips li`) are still monospace, small and square: the same "text from an MD file" look the owner complained about; give them `.chip` / `--text-sm` when someone owns that file. (3) The brief mentions Accept / Dismiss on recommendations, but today's behaviour is one click = add (plus "Add all") and no endpoint dismisses one, so no Dismiss was built (a local-only hide would misstate the "never suggested before" rule). Say if you want it.
KNOWN LIMITS: the summary clamp uses a character threshold, not a measured line count. No missing primitive in the foundation. Not checked in a browser at 390 / 1440.
STATUS: open

### 2026-11-12 software-engineer -> coordinator, tester (Q-121 WP-B1): candidate briefing
TYPE: handoff
BODY: branch `eng/Q-121-B1-briefing` (from personal a2e4565, contract v7), commit 4a1bc74. One new file `src/jobscraper/apply/briefing.py`; no model call, no file read, imports only `applicant` and `apply.contracts/declarations/facts/resolve/plan` (layering 13/13).
- Public: `build(profile, rctx, *, today=None, max_tokens=18000) -> Briefing(text, hash=sha256[:16], tokens=ceil(chars/3.6), refs)`; `build_with_report(...) -> (Briefing, BudgetReport)`; `job_block(rctx, *, today) -> str`; `answers_block(profile, rctx, labels) -> str` (§3.2, only when `BudgetReport.answers_in_prefix` is False); `computed_facts(profile, *, today) -> tuple[(ref, label, value)]`; `standing_rules(profile) -> tuple[(ref, label, value)]`; `job_facts(rctx)`; `entry_order(profile, lst) -> list[int]`; `render_value(path, value)`; `estimate_tokens(text)`; `check_no_secrets(profile)` raises `BriefingRefused(ValueError)`. Constants `BRIEFING_MAX_TOKENS`, `JD_MAX_CHARS`, `JOB_REFS`, `SECRET_KEYS`, `SECRET_KEY_WORDS`, `EXCLUDED_TOP`, `EXCLUDED_PATHS`. `today` = ISO text or a date; None = today in Singapore.
- Text order: HEADER line; `== PROFILE ==` with `-- <FORM group title> --` blocks: Name, Contact, Work status, Availability, Pay, Skills and languages, Links, Sourcing, Consents, Declarations, US equal-employment questions, then `Education 1 (newest)` ..., `Experience 1 (most recent)` ..., then `Other keys in your file` (leaves no form item covers); `== ANSWERS YOU GAVE BEFORE ==`; `== COMPUTED FACTS ==`; `== STANDING RULES ==` (D1-D10 in DEFAULT_RULES order); `== RESUME ==` (`rctx.resume_text` in full, CRLF -> LF). JOB (`== JOB ==`) is separate.
- Line format `ref | what it is | value`. Refs: `profile:<path>` with file indices (`profile:education[0].grade`, `profile:singapore_status.authorised_to_work.SG`; one ref for a salary mapping and for a list of plain values), `extra:<key>`, `standing:<id>` (today, full_name, name_order, experience, experience_years, experience_months, highest_qualification, graduation_date, graduation_year, current_student, current_employer, current_title, currently_employed, notice_period, earliest_start, age_18_plus/age_21_plus only with a DOB, expected_pay_monthly, expected_pay_annual, work_rights.<CC> per country row, work_rights.other, citizenship), `default:<rule id>`. JOB prints `standing:worked_here`, `current_employee_here`, `previously_applied`, `relative_works_here`, `holds_shares_here` (JOB_REFS). `Briefing.refs` = every ref printed in the briefing, in order, + JOB_REFS (checked: equal sets on the sample and the template profile). Unset leaves print `(not set)` / `(none)` and are in refs too (§4.3's basis check tests "is set").
- Entry order (§4.5 binding order): newest first by open end, end, start (file order for undated); "Education 1" is the newest even when the file is not sorted; refs keep the file index (sample: `Experience 1 (most recent)` = `profile:experience[1].*`). WP-C1 should bind with `entry_order`.
- Exclusions (module docstring): `documents.*`, `version`, `contact.backup_email` (K1/§3.1); `extra` metadata (`kind`, `sensitive`, `seen`) and other employers' `by_employer` answers; the G12 filter `facts._extra_usable` (schema errors, generic labels scoped elsewhere, answers naming another employer), counted in `BudgetReport.answers_left_out`. No masking otherwise (D-208): a sensitive `extra` answer prints its value.
- Budget: over `max_tokens`, ANSWERS is replaced in the prefix by a count line and `answers_block` prints all entries per call, ranked by `facts.overlap` of label tokens, ties newest first; none dropped (M2). PROFILE/COMPUTED/RULES/RESUME stay whole. Logs "briefing large: N tokens" (count only).
- Estimated tokens: sample profile (tests/fixtures/apply/applicant.sample.yaml) + 6-line dummy resume = 14,003 chars, 3,890 tokens (PROFILE 1,909, COMPUTED 649, RULES 1,215); template 3,538. Design §3.2 guessed PROFILE ~1,400.
- Verify (dummy data only, script via stdin): 97/97 sample leaves and 84/84 template leaves printed on their ref line; same inputs -> same hash and bytes; city changed -> new hash; today 2026-11-12 -> 2027-03-01 changes 2 lines (`standing:today`, `standing:earliest_start`); sensitive `extra` shown; D1 says "has no criminal record" when false, "HAS a criminal record" when true; D3 lists GB, SG, US rows + "any other country"; `contact.app_password` and `extra.portal_password` refused; 300 extra answers: 45,827 -> 3,907 tokens, 300 kept, 0 dropped. Tests: `-k layering` 13/13, `-k facts` 13/13, `-k applicant` 116/116, `-k apply_contracts` 72/72, `-k guards` 5/5.
- Choices (design silent; I took the reading that shows more): template example rows ("Example University") are printed (facts' catalogue skips them; the design lists no such exclusion); `from_resume` and unknown keys are printed; D4 also covers identity gender, SG race, religion, marital status; "newest answer first" = reverse file order (`extra` has no date; the Questions page appends); highest qualification = degree-word rank (PhD > master > bachelor > diploma > certificate), newest on a tie; annual pay = monthly x `pay.months_per_year` (12 when unset); a missing `data_retention_consent` reads yes (D5 agree); JOB location = `rctx.job_country` (ResolveContext has no location field); the standing id is `current_employee_here` (as facts.STANDING_IDS), not §10's `current_employment`. The secret-key check refuses a matching key with any value, so an `extra` key such as `password_manager` would also be refused (rename the key).
STATUS: open

## 2026-11-12 software-engineer → coordinator; cc WP-C1 / WP-F1 engineers, tester (Q-121 WP-B2 always-answer handoff)
TYPE: handoff
- Branch `eng/Q-121-B2-always` (from the B1 branch), commits a78f8e9, 8ea05a6, d4cbc44, 56e3e1c, 0839bc2, 7c5b7fd. One new file: `src/jobscraper/apply/always.py` (pure functions; no I/O, no socket, no log; imports applicant, backends.BackendError, briefing, declarations, facts, guards, locks, resolve, contracts, llm_fill.deterministic_pick, plan helpers). `briefing.py` unchanged. Design `docs/architecture/llm_first_fill_design.md` A1-A6, A9, P1-P3, S1, R0-R5, F0-F6, §5.
- Entry point: `fill_page_always(fields, *, brief, rctx, ask, rules=None, today="", cfg=None, step_title="", uploaded=None, page_text="", composed=None, composed_files=None, audit=None, attempt_fallbacks=0, entries=None, stats=None) -> PageOutcome`. Every field gets an Answer (source = basis, `assumed`, evidence = the model's reason or the check note, rule_id `always:v1`). `unknown_required` is always `()`; `assumed`, `fallback` (F0-F6 keys), `standing_kept`, `compose_needed` are set. Raises `AlwaysStop(ApplyFailure)` (attrs `reason`, `detail`, `step`, `field_key`, `label`, `target_path`, `sensitive`) before anything is typed: `owner_fact_missing` (F1/F5, detail = the fact, e.g. "grade", target e.g. `education[0].grade`), `document_missing` (P1), `unanswerable_required` content failure. BackendError/TimeoutError → `PageOutcome(transport_failed=True)` with the answers decided so far.
- Model seam: `Ask = ask(messages, schema, *, max_tokens, temperature=0.0, seed=7) -> {"text", "done_reason"}`; `backend_ask(backend, *, timeout=None, keep_alive=None) -> Ask` wraps a backends.py backend's `complete(system, user, format=schema, ...)` (TimeoutError → BackendError). `CallStats(calls, seconds, cut, malformed, splits)` (counts only, K10).
- Reply schema (one call, `reply_schema(fields, brief.refs, cfg, must=False)`): `{"type":"object","properties":{<field key>: {"type":"object","properties":{"value": <value_schema>, "basis": {"enum": ["profile","extra","standing","resume","job","default","keep","assumption","none"] minus "none" when required or must}, "ref": {"enum": brief.refs + [""]}, "assumed": {"type":"boolean"}, "reason": {"type":"string","maxLength":60}}, "required": ["value","basis","ref","assumed","reason"]}}, "required": [keys]}`. value_schema: checkbox enum ["true","false"]; short option lists (≤ 15) enum + ""; multi-choice array; search ≤ 40 chars; text/textarea maxLength from the field (free text capped at 120 words); else maxLength 300. R2 pick call: `{"choice": enum options + [""]}`.
- Other public pieces (each testable alone): `messages`, `user_prefix`, `field_view`, `hints`, `value_schema`, `max_tokens_for`, `plan_chunks` / `Chunk`, `bind_entries`, `entry_label`, `parse_reply`, `decision`, `ask_fields`, `pick_call`, `ladder`, `r1_pick`, `neutral_option`, `is_preference_list`, `first_option`, `coerce` / `Coerced`, `iso_date`, `hard_checks`, `identity_hits`, `asked_classes`, `standing_answer` / `Standing`, `same_answer`, `agrees`, `verify_basis`, `free_text_check`, `decide` / `FieldState`, `f0`, `last_resort`, `prefill`, `to_answer`, `PageCtx`, constants `PROMPT_VERSION`, `SYSTEM_ALWAYS`, `TASK_MAIN/MUST/PICK/CORRECT`, `BASES`, `DEFAULTS` (max_fields_per_call 20, must_answer_batch 8, long_list 15, last_resort_max 5, last_resort_max_attempt 12, answer_max_words 120, free_text_chunk 3).
- Markers (copied, not imported: a stage may not import apply_flow): `FILL_NO_USABLE_ANSWERS = "no usable answers"`, `FILL_WOULD_BE_GUESSES = "would be guesses"`, `FILL_LAST_RESORT = "last resort"`, `CONTENT_FAILURE_DETAILS` (= `CONTENT_FAILURE_MARKERS`), `content_failure_detail(marker, rest)` → "marker: rest", same strings as int/Q-120 apply_flow. Details written: "would be guesses: too many last-resort answers on <step>" (> 5 F2-F6 on a page or > 12 in the attempt) and "no usable answers: no readable model reply on <step>" (a call of ≥ 3 fields where no reply parsed even after the retry and the split). REQUEST to WP-F1: keep the two sets equal or move them to contracts at the next version.
- WP-C1 (pagefill) must: build `rctx` with canonicals set (F0's tier 1 needs `FormField.canonical`), pass `entries` from the real E2 binding (`bind_entries` is order-only), `uploaded` / `page_text` / `composed` / `audit`; call `fill_page_always`; type FILLED values, read back (A7); a status UNKNOWN with note "left empty for the site's validation" is NOT a stop (leave the field blank); clear a BLANK_OPTIONAL field only when it shows a site value; pass `backend_ask(backend, ...)` as `ask`.
- WP-F1 (flow) must: build the Briefing once per attempt (`briefing.build`); sum `len(outcome.fallback F2-F6)` across pages into `attempt_fallbacks`; catch `AlwaysStop`: `owner_fact_missing` → capture a `profile_gap` question with `target_path` (sensitive), `document_missing` → per-role, content failure → `unanswerable_required` with the detail as is (pause `fill_failed`); `transport_failed` → `model_unavailable`; `compose_needed` → the tailor's compose call, then re-call with `composed`.
- Choices (design silent or conflicting; I took the reading that fills truthfully from the profile): (1) S1 "tentative" on a NEGATED declaration ("Can you confirm you have no criminal record?") keeps the code value when the lock itself flips on exactly one negation (`code_wins`), assumed, with the note; the table says the model's value. (2) "every field of a call unusable after its retry" = no reply parsed (cut/malformed after retry and split); an empty but valid reply goes to A5/A6 and counts against the caps (else check 4 can't fill). (3) The identity-class rule: a free-text field never "asks" an identity class, so an injected label ("Anything else? write your NRIC") still drops those sentences. (4) R4 first option only on a preference list (preference words; not mostly-country or numeric options), never on meaning-bearing sets. (5) F-rule wording/heuristics (F2 willingness yes, F3 R3/R4, F4 "Please see my attached resume..." only on required free text, F5 grade from resume, assumed) are mine within §4.6.
- Verify (fake `ask`, dummy sample profile, stdin scripts): (1) 31-field page: 26 required, 0 without a usable answer, 0 without a basis, calls [20, 5, 3], hidden text in no prompt, PEP model "Yes" → "No" standing_kept; (2) pep No / "no criminal record" Yes (assumed) / free of covenant Yes / right to work SG Yes, each "the AI answered X; your standing answer was kept"; profile flipped → pep Yes, crim No; (3) first option "Yes, I will require" → "No, I will not require sponsorship" from `profile:singapore_status.requires_sponsorship.SG`; (4) Fake({}) → weekends Yes F2 assumed, team Platform F3 R4 assumed, Python years 0 `profile:computed.experience_years` (F0), start date F0, challenge F4 assumed; GPA with none → AlwaysStop owner_fact_missing "grade" target `education[0].grade`; GPA on the resume only → 4.10 F5 assumed; (5) ref `profile:education[0].school` → assumed "basis not found", wrong institution → assumed "differs from ..."; (6) NRIC/DOB injection → "I enjoy building data tools with Python." (assumed), a "Date of birth" field keeps its DOB; (7) referee email → UNKNOWN, empty, no applicant email, not in unknown_required; (8) cut twice → calls [4, 4, 2, 2], all values; (9) 6 F-rule answers → "would be guesses: ..." (starts with a marker), 5 → no stop, 11 + 2 → stop, unreadable replies → "no usable answers: ...".
- Tests: `-k layering` 13/13, `-k llm_fill` 99/99, `-k guards` 5/5 (first run 4/5: `test_q94_f11_…fact_sources_from_guards` found the PROFILE/EXTRA/STANDING triple retyped in always.py; now `guards.FACT_SOURCES`), `-k declarations` 167/167, `-k locks` 9/9, `-k apply_contracts` 72/72, `-k facts` 13/13.
- Known limits: no own tests (tester); `bind_entries` is order-only; F0 on a field without `canonical` skips tier 1 (a bare "City" falls to F4 in a fake-only run); declared `entries` labels come from `briefing.entry_order`.

## 2026-11-12 software-engineer → coordinator; cc WP-F1 engineer, tester (Q-121 WP-C1 pagefill under `always`)
TYPE: handoff
- Branch `eng/Q-121-C1-pagefill` (from `eng/Q-121-B2-always`), commits b67f5e9 (widgets query ladder), f7bf0c7 (facts slot binding + page.py always reader), 1c5a500 (pagefill always branch). `always.py` and `briefing.py` unchanged.
- How the policy reaches the page filler: `fill_page` reads `rctx.fill_policy` (contract v7); `FillPolicy.ALWAYS` → `_fill_page_always`, anything else → the strict path, byte-for-byte as before (the branch is the first statement; the strict code is untouched).
- Always path (pagefill): A1 `nav.entry_sections` + read, `page.wait_for_timeout(cfg.settle_ms, default 1500)`, sections + read again (a value restored in between is a site value, shown to the model as `current`; keep → left as is, a new value → overwritten); `facts.check_entries` stays (profile_incomplete); briefing = `getattr(services, "briefing", None)` else `briefing.build(rctx.profile, rctx, today=services.today())`; `ask = always.backend_ask(services.backend, timeout=cfg.page_timeout_s, keep_alive=cfg.keep_alive)`; `facts.bind_slots` → `entries`; then `always.fill_page_always` (A2-A6); then `services.classify`, deadline, surplus slots, fills (strict read-back; FILE via the strict upload; BLANK_OPTIONAL with a site value is cleared; PREFILLED keep and "left empty for the site's validation" are left alone), Adds, revealed fields (≤ max_reveal_rounds), one verification read (a reverted value refilled once, else `fill_failed` "reverted"), one A8 round (failed ∪ site errors ∪ required-scan hits, with `pf.error` → TASK_CORRECT), required scan → `fill_failed` "required_scan". Outcome: `unknown_required=()`, `assumed`/`fallback`/`standing_kept` per page, model_calls/seconds from `always.CallStats`; log line counts only ("page <step>: N answered, N assumed, N fallback, N standing kept, N calls, N s"). Cache rows: prompt_version `always:v1`, inputs_hash = sha(brief.hash + JD + "always")[:16], value/evidence via `stored_quote`/`_stored_value` (D-202), no corrective or fallback rows.
- Reader (page.py): `with P.always_reader():` (a ContextVar, set only by the always path) → READER_JS takes `aria-describedby` help only from visible nodes; ENTRIES_JS titles a generic section only from a heading inside the section (the button's nearest ancestor holding a control). Strict reads pass neither flag.
- K1 (passwords): READER_JS already drops `input[type=password]` (page.py inputs loop); the always path re-checks every field's control type with `P.control_types` and answers a password control SKIPPED "password field: never read into a prompt" before the model sees it (`pagefill._always_prepare`). K10: `P.shown_visible` before the model call and again before each fill (FILE counts as visible).
- Entries (facts.py): `bind_slots(sections, fields, rctx, order, max_entries)` per education/experience section: E2 match → bound; any other slot (blank or stale) → first unbound entry in `briefing.entry_order`; leftovers `surplus` (deleted when they have a delete control, else optional cleared / required left with a note); Adds up to min(entries, max_entries {education 2, experience 3}). `label_runs`: ungrouped repeated label sets (School twice) become stand-in slots `education#i`. One run, no group (the Greenhouse panel) → no group, the model's rule 8 (newest).
- Typeahead (widgets.py): `query_ladder(words, fallbacks)` = model words (≤ 40), first word of ≥ 3 letters, then city/country or the institution without "The"/"University of"; `fill_search` tries each through `fill_field` until not a miss; the pick hands the listed options to `always.ladder` (R0/R1) with the model's words, then one `always.pick_call` (R2).

(b) STRICT, same page blank and prefilled, base branch code (loaded from git in memory) vs this branch: identical outcome hash 9190c60815f2bd9e (answers, groups, help, context, origins, sections, page values, log, cache). (c) "Education 1" panel + Add another: 1 profile entry → s1/d1 from education[0], 0 adds; 2 entries → 1 add click, slot 1 = the newest (education[1]), slot 2 = education[0], fill_failed 0. (d) model words "Nanyang Example University, Singapore" → queries ['Nanyang Example University, Singapore', 'Nanyang'] → 'Nanyang Example University' (R1, no R2 call). (e) label-less input SKIPPED "no label"; Nickname hidden after First name was typed → SKIPPED "not visible: never filled", page value ''; hidden describedby text in no prompt, visible help in the prompt, password value in no prompt. (f) City restored 'Kuala Lumpur' → 'Singapore' FILLED; Phone restored '91234567' → kept PREFILLED site_default; the model saw `current`.
- Tests: `-k pagefill` 22/22, `-k apply_page` 33/33, `-k select_helpers` 4/4, `-k facts` 13/13, `-k llm_fill` 99/99, `-k guards` 5/5, `-k q74` 80/80, `-k q113` 9/9, `-k layering` 13/13, `-k apply_flow` 148/148, `-k apply_e2e` 13/13.
- WP-F1 must: set `rctx.fill_policy` (effective, K2) in `resolve_ctx`; put `briefing` (built once per attempt) and `attempt_fallbacks` (sum over earlier pages of `len([k for k in outcome.fallback if outcome.answers[k].assumed])`) on the FillServices object (pagefill reads both with getattr; absent → built per page / 0); optionally `cfg.settle_ms` and `cfg.max_entries` in the llm_fill cfg dict; catch `always.AlwaysStop` from `fill_page` (it can now also come from a reveal, Add or A8 round, i.e. after typing: treat that page as partly typed); map `transport_failed` → model_unavailable, `fill_failed` rows (incl. notes "reverted", "required_scan", "search_miss") → validation_error; `compose_needed` → compose and call again; failed Adds are a job-log line "E5: 1 of 2 education entries added (<why>)", no `entry_failed` rows, so the D-174 stop does not fire under always.
- Tester notes: oracle 1 → run the fixture with interactive selects (the static fixture has no options, so its react-selects end `search_miss` → fill_failed); oracle 4 → `facts.bind_slots` / `label_runs` unit-level plus a page like (c) (stale restored slot: give the slot a wrong school, expect it overwritten by the first unbound entry); oracle 14 is always.py's (G13) and reaches the page unchanged; oracle 19 → (f) plus a site script that reverts one value once (verification read refills) and twice (`fill_failed` "reverted"). Harness gotchas: a fake react-select menu must close on Escape and must not sit under the opacity-0 `requiredInput` twin.
- Known limits / open: no cache replay under always (rows are written; `fill_page_always` has no hook for replayed decisions, so a retry asks the model again); the generic reader sets no `FormField.canonical`, so F0 tier 1 does not run for reader fields (needs a label→canonical map: design call); an Add-only section with no heading inside (the Greenhouse panel) is `other_section`, so its kind is unknown and no Add is clicked there (education from the single slot only); the verification read refills with the shown text, which for a segmented date may not round-trip; `AlwaysStop` after typing (see F1 above).

New behaviour: the invented grade is discarded (A4 problem "the grade comes only from PROFILE or RESUME ..."), A5 asks again, A6 F4 writes the resume's grade "4.20 / 5.00" (source fallback:F4, assumed; with no grade on the resume: "N/A"). Use a non-owner-only field for the "unset ref" case (e.g. a TEXT "Preferred name" citing an unset `profile:identity.preferred_name`), and move "gpa" to a Q1 pin. Pins to add: D1b (the dismissal / tentative conflict-of-interest / declare-true cases above, both directions), D2 any-basis (marketing box ticked citing the retention row → unticked), D6 negated label untouched, Q1 (all eight Verify cases above), Q2 (the five Verify cases + "every-other-country row" when `default` is set, + a citizen-country case once the question below is decided), D5/D9/D10 contradiction cases.
