## 2026-11-12 software-engineer → coordinator / architect (Q-121 Q2 follow-ups)
TYPE: question
BODY: (1) Q2 as decided answers a country the profile does not list as "not authorised" even when it is the applicant's nationality (e.g. `nationality: SG` with only `{US: false}` → "Are you authorised to work in Singapore?" No, assumed; D-75 said Yes before). Should `singapore_status.nationality` count as an authorised row (Yes / no sponsorship, assumed per §3.3 line 3)? Not built. (2) A country whose right-to-work row is false while `requires_sponsorship.default: false` exists: I read the order literally (the default sponsorship row wins → No); say if "follows the right to work" should beat the default row. (3) The briefing's D5 line says "Letting the employer keep your data for future roles: yes" while D6 says "future roles ... No": the model sees both; suggest D6's text drop "future roles" or name marketing only (a briefing wording change; tell me and I will make it). (4) Declarations reader: "employed by" makes prior employment match relatives questions; the agreement path covers it when both are false, but if the owner worked at the employer and has no relatives there, "Do you have any relatives employed by the company?" is the model's again (the truths differ: no S1, no D1b). A reader change (relatives' "employed by" span beats prior employment's) belongs to the declarations owner: request, not built.
STATUS: closed (Coordinator rulings 1-3 built below; (4) recorded as a declarations follow-up)

## 2026-11-12 software-engineer → coordinator; cc tester (Q-121 rulings 1-3 built)
TYPE: handoff
BODY: Branch `eng/Q-121-always-fixes`, commits 4291492 (rulings 1-2), 722cb80 (ruling 3). Files: `apply/briefing.py`, `apply/always.py`; `applicant.work_right` (strict D-75 reader) unchanged.
- Rulings 1-2 (`briefing.work_right`, `briefing.work_right_basis`): right to work in X = X's own row; else X == `singapore_status.nationality` → Yes (src `nationality`, basis `profile:singapore_status.nationality`, NOT assumed); else the `default` row; else No (derived, assumed). Sponsorship in X = X's own `requires_sponsorship` row; else not authorised by any path → Yes (assumed only when that No was derived); else the nationality → No (basis the nationality field); else the sponsorship `default` row; else D-75 No. One corner where the rulings overlap: a citizen with `requires_sponsorship.default: true` and no row for their own country gets No (ruling 1 "sponsorship required = No" applied before 2c's default row); say if 2c should win there. S1 (`work_rights_standing`) now recomputes EVERY L4 work-rights answer with this rule (a country row gives the reader's own value; a default row can now lose to the nationality or to "not authorised"); F0 and F2 use the same rule. The briefing prints a line for the nationality country when no map lists it: "has the right to work (no row: a citizen of this country); needs no visa sponsorship (no row: a citizen of this country)".
- Ruling 3 (`_d5`, `_d6`): D5 "Keeping your details on file to be considered for future roles or openings: yes" only when `data_retention_consent` is true (else no; an unset value now prints no). D6 "Marketing emails, newsletters, promotional messages, talent community or pool, mailing lists, job alerts: No (or Yes when opted in). A question that mixes both (marketing emails about future roles) is a marketing question." Sample briefing `estimate_tokens`: 4037 before, 4075 after (+38).
- Verify (stdin, fake `ask` scripted to the wrong answer, sample profile): nationality SG, `authorised_to_work {US: false}`, no SG row: authorised SG → Yes, sponsorship SG → No, both source `profile:singapore_status.nationality`, not assumed. `{SG: false}`: authorised SG → No, sponsorship SG → Yes (source `authorised_to_work.SG`, not assumed). `{SG: true, US: false}` + `requires_sponsorship {default: false}`: US sponsorship → Yes (`authorised_to_work.US`, not assumed; A6 alone the same), SG → No (`requires_sponsorship.default`), Germany → Yes (`authorised_to_work.derived`, assumed). `authorised_to_work {default: true}` + `requires_sponsorship {default: false}`: Germany authorised → Yes (`authorised_to_work.default`), sponsorship → No (`requires_sponsorship.default`), neither assumed.
- Tests: `-k always` 37/38 (only the stale gpa pin, unchanged), `-k layering` 13/13, `-k llm_fill` 99/99, `-k declarations` 167/167, `-k facts` 15/15, `-k pagefill` 22/22, `-k apply_flow` (alone, Edge on local pages) 148/148. No other oracle pin encoded the old unlisted/nationality behaviour.
- Tester, pins to add: the nine outcomes above; the briefing nationality line; D5/D6 texts (no "future roles" in D6, the mixed-question clause, D5 yes only when retention is true); a citizen with an explicit false row stays No. Pin to flip: still only `test_always_fill.py::test_a_basis_that_does_not_exist_or_does_not_match_is_flagged_assumed_not_vetoed` ("gpa": now "4.20 / 5.00" from F4, assumed).
STATUS: open

### 2026-11-12 software-engineer -> coordinator, flow lane (Q-121 WP-H1 + WP-K1)
TYPE: handoff
BODY: branch `eng/Q-121-H1K1-backend-compose` (from personal 8856474). Commits 33c1959 (WP-H1, `backends.py`), 8a769b4 (WP-K1, `tailor/compose.py`, `tailor_flow.py`). No contracts, tests, apply/ or apply_flow touched.
WP-H1 public names (`jobscraper.backends`):
- `class PromptRefused(BackendError)`: raised BEFORE the availability probe and any request. Text never names the value, its form or the message; nothing is logged but "prompt refused: it holds a registered secret (value not shown)".
- `Backend.forbid(values: Iterable[str]) -> int` (on the base class, so Ollama and Off both have it): registers values; None and "" are skipped; a value with fewer than `MIN_FORBIDDEN_LEN` = 6 non-space characters raises `ValueError` (message gives the length only) and registers nothing from that call. Returns the count now registered. `Backend.clear_forbidden() -> None`. Checked forms: as written, JSON-escaped (ASCII and readable), URL-encoded (%20 and +, either hex case), and with all whitespace removed on both sides. Applies to `complete(system, user, ...)` and `warm(messages=...)`.
- `warm(backend, keep_alive=None, messages=None)` and `OllamaBackend.warm(keep_alive=None, messages=None)`: with `messages` ([{"role","content"}, ...], the stable prefix) one POST to `/api/chat` with `stream: false`, `num_predict: 1`, `temperature: 0`, `num_ctx` as `complete`, `think: false` when the model has thinking, `keep_alive` when given. Without `messages` the old `/api/generate` call, byte-identical. The module `warm` passes `messages` on only when given (a fake whose `warm(keep_alive)` takes one argument still works).
What the flow lane must call: (1) `backend.forbid([...])` the moment a secret exists: the generated account password in `sign_up` (and the backup-email loop), the vault password when read for sign-in, the IMAP app password when mail loads; (2) `backend.clear_forbidden()` in the attempt's `finally`; (3) catch `PromptRefused` (also surfaces from compose as `ComposeResult.error == "transport: PromptRefused"`, ok False) and end `failed(error, "prompt check")` BEFORE the existing generic `BackendError -> model_unavailable` mapping, since it is a subclass; (4) `backends.warm(backend, keep_alive, messages=[system_msg, user_prefix_msg])` while Edge opens, with the same system text and the same user-prefix text `always.backend_ask` will send (it joins user messages with "\n\n", so give `warm` that joined text as one user message). Register secrets BEFORE `warm`. A too-short secret makes `forbid` raise ValueError: treat as `error` "prompt check" too.
WP-K1 (`tailor_flow.compose`, new keyword-only, defaults keep today's behaviour):
- `tailor_flow.compose(cfg, req, *, backend, redact, timeout=None, policy="strict", enabled=True, required=True)`. `policy` "strict"|"always" (a FillPolicy str-enum works). Composing is allowed when `enabled` or (`policy == "always"` and `required`); otherwise `ComposeResult(False, error="off")` with no model call. The flow lane should pass `enabled=self.acfg.compose.enabled`, `required=<the field is required>`, `policy=<effective policy>` and may keep or drop its own COMPOSE_OFF pre-check (it must at least not stop required targets under always: apply_flow.py:1421 and `pagefill.py:799` / `locks.py:161` check the toggle themselves).
- Under "always" `compose` returns `tailor.compose.ComposeOutcome(ComposeResult)` with extra `flags: tuple[ValidationIssue,...]`, `assumed: bool`, `pruned: bool`, `neutral: bool`, `reason: str` ("" | "checks" | "no master" | "setup"). Clean first or second try: ok, no flags, assumed False. Failed twice: `prune` (drop each sentence that fails C2-C7 read alone; cut sentences from the end to fit max_words/max_chars); the rest is used (ok True, assumed True, pruned True, flags = the last attempt's issues) when it keeps at least 2 sentences, or nothing was dropped, and passes C2-C7 again; else `neutral_text(req)` ("I am applying for the <title> role at <employer>. My resume, attached, sets out my education and experience.", cut to limits; neutral True, assumed True). Missing master and a broken aliases file give the neutral text too (model not called). Transport failure stays ok False, `error="transport: <ExceptionName>"`.
- Hard (never kept): C5 private values, C7 placeholders, C6 other employers, empty, over the length limits. Flags (pruned, then reported): C2 numbers, C3 facts, C4 verbs, C1 minimum length. `compose.check(..., min_words=None)` gained a keyword-only `min_words`; `compose.prune(text, issues, req, master_text, dictionary, attested=())` and `compose.neutral_text(req)` are public.
- Record the audit row as `composed`, assumed from `outcome.assumed`, with `reason` as the note; the model and prompt hash are in the outcome.
Choices the design left open: minimum forbidden length 6 (design names none); URL-encoded form added on top of the design's three (per the delegation); "no separators removed" beyond whitespace; the "at least 2 sentences" rule also accepts a single untouched sentence when nothing was dropped (a one-sentence answer that was only too short); `compose.prune` takes `req, master_text, dictionary` besides `(text, issues)` because C5/C3 need them.
Verify: `-k backends` 22/22, `-k ollama` 9/9, `-k compose` 53/53, `-k tailor` 53/53, `-k layering` 13/13, `-k llm_fill` 99/99, `-k q74` 80/80. Fake-transport checks (httpx.MockTransport, dummy values): plain, JSON-escaped, URL-encoded, URL plus-form, whitespace-stripped secrets each PromptRefused with 0 requests and the secret absent from the exception; unrelated prompt payload byte-identical to `personal` (complete x2 + warm: 5 requests identical); `warm(messages=)` = 1 POST /api/chat; compose always+toggle off+required = pruned text + flags (fact_added, number_added, verb_upgrade); strict+toggle off = ok False error "off", 0 calls.
No test pins were added (tests are the tester's): needed for PromptRefused/forbid/min length/escaped forms, warm(messages=), ComposeOutcome paths (clean, pruned, neutral, no master, strict unchanged, gate matrix).
STATUS: open

## 2026-11-12 software-engineer → coordinator; cc web lane (W1/U1), tester (Q-121 WP-F1 + WP-S1 handoff)
TYPE: handoff
BODY: Branch `eng/Q-121-F1-flow` (from int/Q-121 a5885bd). Commits f962112 (config keys), 575bb18 (S1 store), c3c252e (flow + questions), 6c5058e (doctor), dc027f8 (retry candidates, departure 12). Files: `config.py`, `store.py`, `apply_flow.py`, `apply/questions.py`, `cli.py`. No contracts, tests, web, always/briefing/pagefill/backends/compose, guards/locks/declarations/llm_fill, `config/config.yaml` (still no `fill_policy` line: the code default is `strict`).
As built (interfaces for the web lane):
- Settings keys: `apply.fill_policy` (`always` | `strict`; anything else → `strict` + a logged warning) and `apply.always_trial_runs` (int ≥ 0; invalid or negative → 0 + warning; no upper clamp, the 0..20 range is the Settings form's). Both are in `config.SETTING_KEYS` and go through `ApplyConfig.with_settings` (a stored value wins over config.yaml, as the other apply keys). `ApplyConfig.fill_policy` default `"strict"`, `always_trial_runs` default 0. New `llm_fill` keys with defaults: `must_answer_batch` 8, `last_resort_max` 5, `last_resort_max_attempt` 12, `settle_ms` 1500, `briefing_max_tokens` 18000, `max_entries` {education: 2, experience: 3} (held as a tuple of pairs so the frozen config stays hashable; `dict(cfg.apply.llm_fill.max_entries)`).
- Effective policy (K2): `apply_flow.effective_policy(acfg, backend) -> (FillPolicy, why)`: ALWAYS only when configured `always`, `acfg.fill == "llm"` and `backends.is_local(backend)`; else STRICT with `why` = "fill_policy always ignored: model not on this computer" / "... apply.fill is not llm" (logged once per attempt). Settings' inactive text: use `backends.is_local_config(cfg.budget)` (no backend built).
- Store (schema 7, additive): `apply_answers.assumed INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0` (ALTER when missing; old rows 0). `store.assumed_counts(attempt_ids) -> {attempt_id: n}` (assumed rows with status filled/prefilled = the "N assumed" pill / attempt summary), `store.assumed_count(attempt_id)`. Questions: `questions.status` gains `'assumed'`; `upsert_question` with reason `assumed`: new row → `assumed`; a seen-again `answered` or `open` row → `assumed`; `dismissed` and `assumed` stay; a later non-assumed sighting of an `answered`/`assumed` row → `open`. `question_groups("assumed")` lists them (group status order open > assumed > answered > dismissed). `set_questions_status(key, "assumed"|"answered"|..., now, ids=)` accepts `assumed`. `store.assumed_answer(question_id) -> {attempt_id, job_id, field_key, value (decoded), reason (= the row's evidence), source, note, assumed}` or None: the `apply_answers` row of the question's latest sighting by (attempt_id, field_key) (no value is stored in `questions`, QD §7). Trial: `Store.ALWAYS_TRIAL_USED = "apply.always_trial_used"` (settings table), `always_trial_used() -> int` (0 unset/unreadable), `count_always_trial() -> int` (+1, commits). `retry_candidates` now also returns `owner_fact_missing` attempts (with `unanswerable_required`).
- Questions (`apply/questions.py`): `capture_assumptions(fields, answers, rctx, rules=None)` (reason `assumed`, note "assumed: <source>", label_norm = the qualified label); `capture_owner_fact(fact, rctx, *, field=None, field_key, label, target_path, step)` (always sensitive; `profile_gap` with the path when the path is a profile gap target, else `no_rule`; note "owner_fact_missing: <fact>"); `kept_value(kind, sent)` = Keep: the sent value in the shape an owner answer of that kind takes (yes_no → bool, multi → list, a file → None, else text). Keep = `kept_value(q.kind, store.assumed_answer(id)["value"])` through the existing answer path (saves to `extra`, row → `answered`); Change = the existing answer path with the owner's value. The card's "{reason}" = `assumed_answer(...)["reason"]` (the guarded evidence, redacted per D-202 when not checked).
- Audit: `audit_row` writes `assumed`; the attempt summary log line `counts()` appends ` assumed=N` only under always; each page logs counts only.
As built per §9 outcome (always):
- `model_unavailable`: transport (warm-up or page `transport_failed` not caused by a refusal) → as strict; queue pause, returned (unchanged Q-120 rule).
- `error` "prompt check": `PromptRefused` at the warm-up, on a page (detected by `_RefusalWatch` around the backend, since pagefill reports it as transport), from compose ("transport: PromptRefused"), a `BriefingRefused`, or `forbid` refusing a too-short secret; step `planning` before Edge opens. Not returned on its own (generic 3x error streak pauses).
- `unanswerable_required` content failure: `AlwaysStop` from always.py; a detail without a content-failure marker is wrapped with "would be guesses: ..." so no field label is ever the detail; queue pause `fill_failed`, not returned. The only other `unanswerable_required` under always is sign-up "account terms" (unchanged).
- `owner_fact_missing`: field captured (sensitive, `profile_gap` + target path when known), attempt failed with the fact as detail ("date of birth"); queue goes on; the same fact on the last 3 finished roles → `repeated_failures` "<fact> is missing from your profile"; never part of the generic streak. Retried by `retry_candidates` after the owner answers.
- `document_missing`: per role, nothing captured, queue goes on (generic same-reason streak still applies).
- `profile_incomplete`, `validation_error` (pre-Submit scan after one A8 round, or a required `fill_failed`), `page_unrecognised`, captcha/bot/2FA, owner-level: unchanged paths. A failed E5 Add is a log note under always (no `validation_error`).
Departures from the design (each the reading that submits less, or forced by a frozen file):
1. K2 also requires `apply.fill: llm` (the rules path has no always branch).
2. Secrets are registered under strict too (K1 has no policy; strict prompts never carry them, so strict behaviour is unchanged unless a secret is in a prompt, which is then refused and ends `model_unavailable` through the unchanged strict mapping). A too-short secret ends `error` "prompt check" under always; under strict it is logged and not registered.
3. Under always, the known vault passwords for this tenant's account rows and the IMAP passwords of the profile's mailboxes are registered BEFORE the warm-up (the warm-up already sends the briefing); IMAP registration is always-only (strict mail reads unchanged).
4. Composed texts and employer-specific (by_employer) declarations are not offered as "Answered for you" (Keep would save a per-job text as a standing answer).
5. D-202 redaction (`stored_quote`) stays under always: an unchecked model quote is stored redacted, so the card's reason can be "(redacted)".
6. A trial counts only when the attempt would have submitted (base mode auto, not a dry run); an unreadable counter counts as a trial. A trial attempt runs as a dry run (queue row mode updated to `dry_run`) and logs "Trial: filled, not sent (trial n of N); k answer(s) assumed".
7. An `open` question seen again as assumed becomes `assumed` (the design names only `answered`): the field was answered for the owner, so the card offers Keep/Change instead of an open question.
8. `doctor` prints a fill-policy line under strict too, and warnings under always: model not local (ignored), model not available, no work-rights rows, `authorised_to_work.default: true` with ≤ 1 nationality.
9. Compose `PromptRefused` maps to `error` "prompt check" under both policies (strict used to say `model_unavailable`; only reachable when a secret is in a compose prompt).
10. The neutral compose text is duplicated in `apply_flow.NEUTRAL_TEXT` (apply_flow may not import tailor/compose; used only when a required composed text comes back empty).
11. An `AlwaysStop` (owner fact, document, content failure; before or after typing) ends the attempt without that page's audit rows: always.py/pagefill raise before an outcome exists, and both are frozen for this WP. Earlier pages' rows stay; the owner-fact question carries the field. Fix needs the stop to carry the page's answers (always.py owner).
12. `retry_candidates` skips an attempt whose sightings are all reason `assumed` (an always content failure that captured "Answered for you" rows on earlier pages would otherwise show on the Questions page as "ready to retry" with nothing asked). Strict has no such reason, so strict candidates are unchanged.
Verify (stdin scripts; real `OllamaBackend` on 127.0.0.1:11434 with an `httpx.MockTransport` client answering every field from its `format` schema with basis `assumption`; the real Workday navigator on local fixture pages via `tests/test_apply_flow_llm.py` helpers; headless Edge; temp DB + test config, mode `dry_run` except the trial case; nothing written outside scope):
- (d) strict, wd_b "My Information" with the scripted FakeFillBackend: `failed unanswerable_required 'Address Line 2'`, one open question (profile_gap), required unknown ['Address Line 2'], submit=0 next=0 (as on int/Q-121).
- (a) always, same page: required unknown [], Address Line 2 `filled`, assumed; 13 "Answered for you" questions (status `assumed`); 13 chat requests + 2 warm-up prefix posts; the attempt then ends `page_unrecognised 'my_information shown again'` because the fixture's Next does not advance (fixture limit; Next clicked once, submit=0).
- (b) always via `run_queue` (force dry run), two queued roles, profile without `identity.date_of_birth`, a required "Date of Birth" text field injected into the first role's page: role 1 `failed owner_fact_missing 'date of birth'`, open question ('Date of Birth', profile_gap, identity.date_of_birth, sensitive True); role 2 ran (attempt 2, required unknown []); `pause_for(role 1)` = None; unit: 3x the same fact → (repeated_failures, "date of birth is missing from your profile"), 3 different facts → None.
- (c) always, vault patched to a planted password, an account row for the fixture tenant, the same value in `extra.portal_note` raw / JSON-escaped: `failed error 'prompt check'` at planning, 0 requests sent, 0 browser sessions; without the planted value: "prompt check: 1 secret(s) registered", 15 requests, 0 hold the secret.
- Trial: wd_a 7 steps, always, base mode auto, `always_trial_runs: 1`: `ready_to_submit`, attempt mode `dry_run`, submit_clicked_at None, page submit count 0, review page reached, pre-Submit scan passed, required unknown [], counter 1, log "Trial: filled, not sent (trial 1 of 1); 44 answer(s) assumed"; the next attempt's `choose_policy()` = False (policy always, sends).
- doctor (temp config, patched backend): strict → "fill policy    Ask me first (strict): ..."; always + local + trial 2 → "Always answer: ..." + "trial: the next 2 of 2 application(s) are filled but not sent"; always + non-local → strict line + "WARNING: fill_policy always is ignored: the AI model isn't on this computer"; `fill_policy: sometimes` → strict.
- Config: always/3/education 1 → `always 3 (('education', 1), ('experience', 3))`, hashable; bad values → strict / 0 with warnings; settings override wins. S1 migration: a v6 DB with rows (built by the a5885bd store) opens as 7, old rows assumed=0, reopen ok.
- Tests: `-k layering` 13/13, `-k apply_contracts` 72/72, `-k store` 97/98 and `-k apply_queue` 46/47 (the one red in both is the stale SCHEMA_VERSION pin below), `-k questions` 76/76, `-k config` 37/37, `-k settings` 24/24, `-k always` 37/38 (the known stale gpa pin), `-k pagefill` 22/22, `-k apply_cli` 23/23, `-k compose` 53/53, `-k web_` 135/135; alone: `-k apply_e2e` 13/13, `-k apply_flow` 148/148; full `python tests/run_tests.py` 1872/1874 (the two stale pins named here; Edge tests skipped there as known, hence the separate runs). After dc027f8: `-k questions` 76/76, `-k store` 97/98 (same stale pin).
Observation (not mine to change): `declarations.prior_employment(sample profile, "Example Co")` is True (the sample's "Example Logistics Pte Ltd" matches on the single word "Example"), so S1 answered "previously worked for Example Co" Yes, confident. Same reader under strict; a fixture employer name other than "Example Co" avoids it in pins, and the declarations owner may want a look (a one-word employer name matched against a longer past employer).
Tester pins to write (§13 oracles; same seam as the Verify above):
- 8 (K1): a planted vault password (account row + patched `vault.get`) in `extra` raw and JSON-escaped → `error` "prompt check" at planning, 0 requests, browser never opened; a generated sign-up password registered at `vault.generate` (fake navigator sign-up) appears in no request; a too-short secret under always → `error` "prompt check"; under strict → logged, attempt goes on; PromptRefused on a page call → `error` "prompt check" (not model_unavailable); compose "transport: PromptRefused" → `error` "prompt check"; `clear_forbidden` after every attempt (the next attempt's prompts are not refused).
- 9 (K2): always + `OllamaBackend("http://ollama:11434")` or a `:cloud` tag or `fill: rules` → rctx strict, log "fill_policy always ignored: ...", no warm-up messages; doctor lines above; Settings inactive text from `backends.is_local_config`.
- 10: page `transport_failed` (MockTransport 500) → `model_unavailable`, queue pause model_unavailable, role returned; 6 F2-F6 fallbacks on one page or 13 in the attempt → `unanswerable_required` with a detail starting "would be guesses", `is_content_failure` True, pause `fill_failed`, not returned; an AlwaysStop detail without a marker gets the marker (no field label in the detail).
- 18 (Keep): an assumed row → `question_groups("assumed")`, `store.assumed_answer(id)` returns the sent value + evidence; Keep = `kept_value(kind, value)` saved via the answer path → `extra` entry, status `answered`; next attempt the field is P3 (source `memory:`/extra, not assumed) and the question is not re-captured as assumed; an assumed row seen again on an `answered` question → `assumed`; `dismissed` stays.
- 20 (trial): N=2 → attempts 1-2 `ready_to_submit` in mode dry_run with the "Trial: filled, not sent" log and counter 1, 2; attempt 3 submits (fake navigator); a dry-run attempt never counts; a store whose counter cannot be read → trial.
- Also: `owner_fact_missing` capture (profile_gap + path, sensitive), the queue goes on, 3x same fact → repeated_failures naming it, mixed facts → no pause, owner-fact attempts in `retry_candidates`; `assumed_counts` only counts filled/prefilled rows; schema v6 → v7 migration keeps rows (assumed 0); config coercions (`fill_policy`, `always_trial_runs`, `max_entries` partial override); E5 Add failure under always = log note, no `validation_error`; a required compose target under always with `compose.enabled: false` is composed (no compose_stop), an empty text → the neutral text, assumed; pre-Submit hit under always → one refill round, still a hit → `validation_error`.
Stale pin (tester): `tests/test_apply_queue_store.py::test_a_v5_database_opens_as_v6_with_the_table_and_keeps_its_rows` asserts `SCHEMA_VERSION == 6` (now 7; it is the only red in `-k store` and `-k apply_queue`). Still stale: `test_always_fill.py::test_a_basis_that_does_not_exist_or_does_not_match_is_flagged_assumed_not_vetoed` (from the always fixes).

## 2026-11-12 software-engineer → coordinator; cc Vue lane (U1), tester (Q-121 WP-W1 handoff)
TYPE: handoff
BODY: Branch `eng/Q-121-W1-web` (from int/Q-121 d6daad9). Commits 50c5a16 (questions), 343875b (apply settings + status), 817103b (attempt rows). Files: `web/routers/questions.py`, `web/routers/apply.py`, `web/routers/applications.py`. Additive only: no field renamed or removed. No store/flow/config/contract edit was needed.
Routes (all mutating routes sit behind the existing `CrossSiteWriteGuard`: a foreign Origin or `Sec-Fetch-Site: cross-site` is 403):
- `GET /api/questions?status=open|answered|assumed|all` (new value `assumed`; anything else 422). New top-level `counts: {open, assumed, answered, dismissed}` (groups per status; every listing). Each group with `status == "assumed"` gains `assumed: [item]`, one item per variant still awaiting Keep/Change: `{question_id, label, kind, options[labels], sensitive, employer, company, role, job_id, attempt_id, value (raw, json), answer (one short line, null for none/file), reason (the model's reason, "" when none or redacted), source (e.g. "assumption", "fallback:F2"), basis (plain words), text (the card sentence), can_keep}`. `can_keep` false = the sent value cannot be saved as it is (outside the options, no value on record): disable Keep, offer Change.
- `POST /api/questions/{question_id}/keep` body optional `{etag?, employer?}` -> 200 `{answered: [id], already: false, ready, etag}`. Saves the sent value to `extra.<key>` (same helpers, etag guard and write lock as `/questions/answer`), only that row becomes `answered`. `etag` omitted = write the current profile (one click); sent = checked (409 `changed`/`unreadable`).
- `POST /api/questions/{question_id}/change` body `{value?, picks?, etag?, employer?}`: yes_no -> `value` true/false (also "yes"/"no"); text/date -> non-empty string; number -> number or numeric string; choice -> `picks: [one label]`; multi -> `picks: [labels]` (labels verbatim from `options`). Same response as Keep. 422 `{detail: "invalid", errors: [{path, message}]}` with nothing saved. A group asked under its own context (label "x | y") is saved for the employer that asked unless `employer` is sent.
- Status codes for both: 404 unknown id; 409 `dismissed` (put aside) / `not_assumed` (still an open question: use `/questions/answer`); Keep on an `answered` row = 200 `{answered: [], already: true}` (a double click); Change on an `answered` row applies again (idempotent for the same body; it is also the "change my kept answer" path).
- `GET /api/apply/settings` / `PUT`: two new writable fields `fill_policy` ("always" | "strict") and `always_trial_runs` (whole number 0..20; `true`, strings and floats refused), in `defaults` too. New read-only fields: `fill_policy_effective` ("always"|"strict"), `fill_policy_inactive` (null, or the plain reason `always` is configured but not in force), `always_trial_used`, `always_trial_left`, `always_trial_text` ("Off" | "Next 3 not sent" | "Next 3 not sent (2 left)" | "Next 3 not sent (done)"). Same all-or-nothing 422 `{detail: "invalid", errors: [{field, message}]}`; stored in the settings table (`apply.fill_policy`, `apply.always_trial_runs`) over config.yaml.
- `GET /api/apply/status` gains `fill: {fill_policy_effective, fill_policy_inactive, always_trial_runs, always_trial_left}` (for an Inbox "Trial: next N not sent" chip).
- Attempt rows (`attempt_view`: `last_attempt` in `GET /api/applications`, `GET /api/applications/{job_id}/attempts`) gain `assumed` (int) and `assumed_text` ("3 answers assumed" / "1 answer assumed" / ""); the Vue lane composes "Submitted · 3 answers assumed". `GET /api/apply/queue` `recent[]` items gain `assumed`, `assumed_text`. One `assumed_counts` query per list (no N+1). `owner_fact_missing` reads `kind: needs_input` (it waits for the owner), `document_missing` reads `kind: stopped`; both texts come from the contract's `REASON_TEXTS` with the detail filled in ("This form asks for your date of birth, which isn't in your profile or resume. Nothing was sent." / "This form needs a photo, and your profile has none. Nothing was sent.").
New parameter-free GET routes for `SWEEP_GET_ALLOWLIST`: none (all new routes are POST or take a parameter/query). `-k connections` 39/39.
Copy I wrote (the design gives none; Coordinator to review): `INACTIVE_NOT_LLM` "Always answer is off for now: form filling by the AI model is switched off in the configuration." (the design's "the AI model isn't on this computer" text is used as given for the other case); the `fill_policy` 422 "How forms are answered must be "always" (the local AI answers every field) or "strict" (it stops and asks)"; the trial 422 "Trial applications must be a whole number from 0 to 20; 0 means off"; the trial values above; the basis sentences (assumption: "there was nothing in your profile or resume to base it on"; fallback: "the form needed an answer and the AI did not give a usable one"; default: "it is one of your standing rules for this kind of question"; profile: "it comes from your profile"; resume/job likewise; standing: "it is worked out from your profile"; declaration: "it follows your declarations"; llm: "it is the AI's best guess"; other: "it is JobScraper's best guess"); the card sentence is design §6.3 verbatim ("JobScraper answered “{answer}”: {reason}. Keep it for next time, or change it."), with the basis (capitalised) standing in when no reason is on record, and "JobScraper answered this field, but the answer is no longer on record. Change it to set one." when no value is on record; 422 messages "answer Yes or No", "give a number", "give an answer", "choose an option", "JobScraper's answer is not one of this question's options; use Change", "...is not on record; use Change", "that employer did not ask this question".
Decisions and limits:
1. `apply.questions.kept_value` is a stage module web/ may not import (layering): `questions._kept_value` is a copy of the same reading with a looser word norm. Tester: pin parity (same inputs for yes_no/multi/text/list/dict/None, both functions).
2. `fill_state` in apply.py mirrors `apply_flow.effective_policy` (always needs `apply.fill == "llm"` and `backends.is_local_config(cfg.budget)`); web may not import the orchestrator. Pin: the Settings text and `effective_policy` agree for local / non-local / `:cloud` tag / `fill: rules`.
3. Changing `always_trial_runs` does not reset `apply.always_trial_used` (design §12: a trial runs while used < N). So after 3 used, choosing "3" again gives no new trial unless the owner picks a larger number. Question for the Coordinator: should the PUT reset the counter when the number is set? It is one `store.set_setting(Store.ALWAYS_TRIAL_USED, 0)`; I did not add it (no design line).
4. `GET /questions` lists the "Answered for you" detail only for groups whose status is `assumed`. A group that is `open` because another variant still waits shows its assumed variants after that one is answered (open wins, store rule).
5. Keep/Change mark only the one row `answered` (not its sibling variants): another variant with a different assumed value stays listed until the owner settles it.
Stale pins (tester): `tests/test_web_apply_settings.py::test_apply_settings_get_returns_six_keys_and_a_put_moves_values_not_defaults` asserts the exact key set `SIX | {writable, defaults}` (now plus `fill_policy`, `always_trial_runs`, `fill_policy_effective`, `fill_policy_inactive`, `always_trial_used`, `always_trial_left`, `always_trial_text`); update SIX and the set. Still stale on the base, not mine: `test_apply_queue_store::...v5_database_opens_as_v6...` (`SCHEMA_VERSION == 6`), the store twin, the `test_always_fill` gpa pin.
Pins to add (tester, `test_web_questions*`, `test_web_apply_settings`): (a) PUT fill_policy always then GET shows it and effective always; invalid value -> 422 naming `fill_policy`, nothing saved; trial bounds -1/21/"3"/true/2.5 refused; a non-local budget (`ollama_url` on another host) or `fill: rules` -> effective strict with the reason text; the status `fill` block; (b) an `assumed` question plus an `apply_answers` row (`seed_question(... reason="assumed")` + `add_answers(... assumed=True, evidence=...)`) listed under `status=assumed` with `counts`; Keep -> `extra.<key>.value`, row `answered`, gone from the list, second Keep `already`; Change yes_no "maybe" -> 422 and nothing saved, True -> stored; choice Change with an unknown label -> 422, a listed label -> `picks`; Keep of a choice value outside the options -> 422 and `can_keep` false; dismissed -> 409; open question -> 409 `not_assumed`; unknown id 404; stale etag 409 `changed`; foreign Origin 403; (c) the attempt list and `last_attempt` carry `assumed`/`assumed_text` (`assumed_counts` counts only filled/prefilled rows); `owner_fact_missing` -> `needs_input`.
Verify: scripted TestClient check on a temp DB, 45 checks: 44 pass, the one failure was my script indexing the wrong question row; the case re-run alone: Keep and Change on an open question -> 409 `not_assumed`. `-k layering` 13/13, `-k questions` 76/76, `-k web_` 134/135 (the stale exact-key pin), `-k connections` 39/39, `-k settings` 23/24 (same pin), `-k applications` 10/10, `-k apply_queue` 46/47 (the stale SCHEMA_VERSION pin).
STATUS: open

## 2026-11-12 software-engineer → coordinator; cc tester (`tst/Q-121-flow-pins`), web + Vue lanes (Q-121 review fixes F2-F5, F7, F9, F13, F14, trial ruling)
TYPE: handoff
BODY: Branch `eng/Q-121-F1-review-fixes` (from int/Q-121 8565ab9), one commit per item: 37e73e6 F2, 163254c F3, 2a45054 F4, 4a9781b F5, 54392f2 F7, f833afe F9 (config lows), c6fb4ab F13 + F14 (b)(c), 2147350 trial ruling (web). Files: `apply_flow.py`, `store.py`, `config.py`, `web/routers/apply.py`. Not touched: tests, contracts, always.py, pagefill.py, declarations.py, web/src, config/config.yaml.
Rules as built:
- F2: `_naming_wanted` is False for a question whose reason is `assumed`: no naming call, so its key is an `extra` entry that lists the exact label (rule 2), a stored row with the same label and kind (rule 3), or a new slug (rule 5); never the model's `same_as`. Keep and Change (`web/routers/questions.py` `_settle`) write `extra.<row key>` only, and that key is now the question's own.
- F3: `upsert_question` with reason `assumed` on an existing row: `answered` → `assumed`; `open`, `dismissed`, `assumed` keep their status; the row's `reason` is never rewritten by an assumed sighting (also for answered → assumed), so `retry_candidates` (HAVING a non-assumed reason) keeps a stopped role. A non-assumed sighting is unchanged (answered/assumed → open, reason updated).
- F4: `always_stop` details: owner fact = `owner_fact_detail(fact, label, step)` → "date of birth (“Date of Birth” on my_information)" (the form's label, never a value); document = "<label> (on <step>)"; content failure = the marker detail, plus " on <step>" when neither the step id nor its title is in it. `pause_for`'s same-fact rule reads the fact back with `owner_fact_of(detail)` (text before " ("; a pre-F4 detail is the fact alone). `AlwaysStop` carries no decided answers (attributes: reason, detail, step, field_key, label, target_path, sensitive), so the stopped page still has no audit rows; that needs an always.py change (the stop to carry `answers`), not made.
- F5: new nullable column `apply_attempts.entries_note` (additive ALTER in the v7 migration; SCHEMA_VERSION stays 7; NULL for strict and older rows). Under always `_FillServices.log` reads pagefill's "E5: N of M <section> entries added (<why>)" line; the attempt keeps "N of M <section> entries added" per section (latest wins; sections joined "; "; the why is not kept), writes it to the row and the summary log line gains ` entries="1 of 2 education entries added"` after ` assumed=N`. Read it with `store.entries_notes(attempt_ids) -> {attempt_id: str}` (next to `assumed_counts`, one query) or `store.attempt(id)["entries_note"]`. Web/Vue lanes: add it to the Applications row and attempt detail next to the assumption pill (e.g. "1 of 2 education entries added"; absent = nothing to show).
- F7: under always the known secrets (this tenant's vault passwords via a pure `detect(job)`, the profile mailboxes' IMAP passwords) are registered at the top of `body()`, before `resume()` (the tailor's prompt); when `detect` cannot name the site only the mailbox passwords are registered there. body_llm no longer registers them a second time. The lower-case vault lookup is unchanged: the profile's email as written is still tried first.
- F9: `trial_runs_value`: whole numbers 0..20 (3.0 / "3.0" → 3); above 20 or +inf → 20 (sends less); text, bool, NaN, 3.5, -inf, below 0 → 0; each with a warning; never raises. `max_entries`: a count that is not a whole number ≥ 0 (text, bool, NaN, inf, 2.5) keeps that section's default; above 20 → 20; 2.0 → 2; never raises (`.inf` no longer raises OverflowError).
- F13: `page_fallbacks` {step: count} on FillServices; `always_page_done` replaces the page's count and `attempt_fallbacks` = their sum; before the same page is filled again (the corrective pass after Next errors, the pre-Submit refill) `refill_base` sets `attempt_fallbacks` to the other pages' sum.
- F14 (b): the trial log line's counter read is guarded (a failure prints "?", the attempt goes on; `choose_policy` already counts an unreadable counter as a trial). (c): `compose_files`, `compose_meta`, `compose_assumed` are reset at the start of every attempt (batch `apply` as well as the queue).
- Trial ruling: `PUT /api/apply/settings` with `always_trial_runs` different from the effective value before the save sets `apply.always_trial_used` to 0 (existing `store.set_setting` + `Store.ALWAYS_TRIAL_USED`); the same value, or a PUT without that key, leaves it.
Behaviours a pin written before these fixes may have captured (tester, please align `tst/Q-121-flow-pins`): (1) an open question seen as assumed now stays `open` (was `assumed`) and keeps its reason; answered → assumed keeps its old reason (was `assumed`); (2) no naming model call for an assumed question, its key = exact-label key or slug (was possibly the model's `same_as`); (3) `owner_fact_missing` detail is "fact (“label” on step)" (was the fact alone), `document_missing` detail gains " (on step)", a content-failure detail without the step gains " on <step>"; (4) the F2-F6 attempt count no longer doubles on a refilled page; (5) a new trial size resets the used counter; (6) `always_trial_runs` above 20 reads 20 (was itself), 3.0 reads 3 (was 0); (7) the secrets' log line "prompt check: n secret(s) registered" now comes before tailoring, once per attempt; (8) the summary line may end with `entries="..."`; (9) compose bookkeeping no longer carries across batch jobs.
Proposed contract text for F4 (architect; `contracts.REASON_TEXTS` / a content-failure variant):
- Content failure (`unanswerable_required` whose detail starts with a marker), new text instead of the Profile hint: text "JobScraper would have had to guess too many answers on this form ({detail}). Nothing was sent." hint "Apply to this role by hand, or skip it. Answering more questions in Profile can help next time." (Needs a lookup keyed on `is_content_failure`, since REASON_TEXTS is keyed by reason only; the queue banner FILL_FAILED should say the same.)
- `owner_fact_missing` (detail now "date of birth (“Date of Birth” on my_information)"): text "This form asks for your {detail}, which isn't in your profile or resume. Nothing was sent." stays readable; hint unchanged.
- `document_missing` (detail now "Passport copy (on documents)"): text "This form needs a {detail}, and your profile has none. Nothing was sent." stays readable.
Addition after merging int/Q-121 (e3f8df8, the tester's flow pins; outbox conflicts kept both sides): 74d0a86 D1, ff5c8c0 D2 + D3 + D4 (one commit, one file).
- D1: `store.assumed_answer` decodes `value_json` only when the answer row's `field_type` is `multi_choice`, `checkbox` or `file` (the kinds `add_answers` stores as JSON); a text, number, date or single choice comes back exactly as sent ("3.50" stays "3.50").
- D2: the page filler's backend is wrapped in `_RefusalWatch` under both policies; a refused page prompt ends `error` "prompt check" under strict too (was `model_unavailable`). Strict pins touched: none existing (no strict apply_flow pin uses `PromptRefused`; `-k apply_flow` 148/148); the new `test_always_flow::test_strict_ends_a_refused_page_prompt_as_error_prompt_check_too` is green.
- D3: an unreadable trial counter logs "apply: WARNING the trial counter could not be read (<ExceptionName>); this attempt runs as a dry run" and the attempt is a trial (dry run), never a crash or a real submit.
- D4: `_RefusalWatch.failure` records the last failed call ("timed out" for a TimeoutError or a BackendError whose text says timeout, else "model unavailable"); when no row carries a "transport: ..." note, `_transport_cause` uses it, so a model that died (HTTP 500) reads `model_unavailable` "model unavailable on <step>", not "timed out".
For pins: E5 = column `apply_attempts.entries_note` (`store.entries_notes(ids)` / `store.attempt(id)["entries_note"]`), text "N of M <section> entries added" (sections joined "; "), summary line suffix ` entries="1 of 2 education entries added"` after ` assumed=N`. Compose refusal = `compose_step`: `res.error` starting "transport" and containing "PromptRefused" (compose writes `error="transport: PromptRefused"`) → `stage(applied=False)` then `ApplyFailure(ERROR, "prompt check", step=step)`.
Tester pins red on purpose (review changes; not edited, new expected value after →):
- `tests/test_always_flow_stops.py::test_owner_fact_missing_waits_with_a_named_question_and_the_next_role_still_runs`: `reason_detail == "date of birth"` → "date of birth (“Date of Birth” on one)" (F4).
- `tests/test_always_flow_stops.py::test_document_missing_is_per_role_captures_nothing_and_the_queue_goes_on`: `reason_detail == "transcript"` → "transcript (on one)" (F4).
- `tests/test_always_flow_stops.py::test_an_always_stop_page_keeps_the_attempt_the_reason_and_the_question_but_no_rows_for_that_page`: `reason_detail == "date of birth"` → "date of birth (“Date of Birth” on two)" (F4).
- `tests/test_store_always.py::test_question_status_moves_as_the_assumed_card_needs` line 171: `status() == "assumed"` → "open" (F3: an open row stays open on an assumed sighting).
- Older, not mine to change: `test_always_fill::test_a_basis_that_does_not_exist_or_does_not_match_is_flagged_assumed_not_vetoed` (red on the pre-change baseline too), `test_web_apply_settings::test_apply_settings_get_returns_six_keys_…` (exact key count), `test_apply_queue_store::test_a_v5_database_opens_as_v6_…` (SCHEMA_VERSION 7; green after the merge).
Verify (on ff5c8c0): `-k always_flow` 38/41 (the 3 F4 pins above); `-k store` 104/105; `-k questions` 76/76; `-k always` 141/145 (the 4 above); `-k apply_queue` 47/47; `-k web_` 134/135; `-k layering` 13/13; `-k apply_flow` alone 148/148; `-k apply_e2e` alone: see the reply. Before D1-D4: `-k config` 37/37, `-k settings` 23/24.

## 2026-11-12 software-engineer -> coordinator; cc tester (Q-121 WP-U1 handoff)
TYPE: handoff
BODY: Branch `eng/Q-121-U1-vue` (from int/Q-121 c3e1706). One commit, 598782f, holds all three screens (a staging slip merged the three planned commits into one; nothing was pushed). Files (all web/src): `api.js` (keepAssumed, changeAssumed, getQuestions doc), `attempts.js`, `components/QuestionCard.vue`, `QuestionsInbox.vue`, `ApplicationDetails.vue`, `ApplicationRow.vue`, `ApplyQueuePanel.vue`, `ApplySettings.vue`. No test, style.css, ui/* or backend edit.
Verify: `cd web; npx vitest run` -> 51 files, 1055 passed, 1 skipped, 0 failed (no retarget needed). `npx vite build --outDir C:/Users/Admin/AppData/Local/Temp/q121-u1-dist --emptyOutDir` -> "built in 1.26s". Also a throwaway 13-case check (deleted, not committed) of the cases below passed.
Screens, selectors and texts (tester):
1. Profile -> Application details -> Questions: `section#answered-for-you.assumed` (h3 "Answered for you", lead "These had no answer in your profile or resume, so JobScraper wrote one and used it. Keep an answer to use it next time, or change it."), below the open cards and above "Answered questions". Rendered only when `counts.assumed > 0` and the list has items, or the list failed (then `.assumed-error` role=alert + `.assumed-retry` "Try again"). Each item is `QuestionCard` with `assumed` prop: `article.q-card[data-key=assumed-<question_id>]`, `h4.q-title` = the question, chip `.q-assumed-chip` "Answered for you", `.q-who` "Asked by <company> · <role>", `.q-assumed-text` = the server's `text` (design 6.3 verbatim), `.q-basis` "Basis: <basis>." only when a reason exists, `.q-nokeep` "This answer can't be kept as it is. Use Change to give your own." when `can_keep` is false. Buttons `button.q-keep` "Keep" (disabled when `can_keep` false or a call is out; aria-label "Keep this answer: <title>") and `button.q-change` "Change". Change opens the question card's own input (yes/no radios, `select` for a choice, checkboxes for multi, text/date/number) plus `button.q-save` "Save answer" and `button.q-cancel` "Cancel". A sensitive item shows the Private tag and masks the answer as "•••" (sentence rebuilt: "JobScraper answered “•••”: <reason or basis>. Keep it for next time, or change it.").
   Calls: `GET api/questions?status=open` then `?status=assumed` only if `counts.assumed > 0` (a server without `counts` makes no third call). Keep -> `POST api/questions/<id>/keep` body `{etag}`; Change -> `POST .../change` body `{value, etag}` (yes_no bool; text/date string; number as the input yields) or `{picks:[label...], etag}` (choice/multi). Success: toast "Kept. JobScraper will use this answer from now on." / "Saved. JobScraper will use your answer from now on.", the Inbox emits `saved {etag}` (the parent reloads the profile, as for an answer) and reloads, the row leaves. 409 `not_assumed`/`dismissed`, 404 -> reload quietly, no message. 409 `changed`/`unreadable` -> emits `reload`, message under the card ("Your details changed since you opened this page. They are reloaded; check the answer and try again." / the unreadable-file sentence). 422 -> the server's `errors[].message` joined, under the card (`.q-error`). Other -> "Could not save the answer: <text>".
   Badge decision: assumed answers do NOT raise the Profile "Application details (N)" count or any attention badge (the inbox's `loaded` event gains an `assumed` count but Profile reads only `open`); the design is silent and the answer was already used. No "Keep all" (the design has none). Empty state: the group is not drawn at all (the design gives none; the section above already says "No questions waiting.").
   Deep link: `attempts.openAssumed()` sets sessionStorage `jobscraper.profile.focus = answered-for-you`, opens Profile details and fires window event `jobscraper:show-assumed`; the inbox scrolls to and focuses `#answered-for-you` after its list is drawn and clears the key.
2. Applications row chip (`rowState`, `ui-badge.app-chip[data-kind]`): kind `sent` with `assumed > 0` -> "Submitted · 3 answers assumed" (tone ok; the `assumed_text` is used as sent); `unconfirmed` -> "Sent, not confirmed · N answers assumed"; `dry_run` -> "Ready to submit · N answers assumed", or with `trial: true` on the attempt -> "Trial: filled, not sent" (+ " · N answers assumed"). Without `assumed` all labels are unchanged. `a.assumed > 0` adds `a.app-review` "Review answers" (opens Profile -> Answered for you). `needs_input` with `reason_code: owner_fact_missing` -> "Needs your input: <fact>" (fact parsed from the contract sentence "This form asks for your <fact>, which isn't in your profile..."; falls back to "Needs your answers"), with the existing `a.app-answer` "Answer in Profile" link (Application details, where the field is captured). `stopped` with `reason_code: document_missing` -> "Stopped: needs a <document>" plus `a.app-document` "Add it in Profile"; Try again stays. Runs -> Recently finished: the outcome chip reads e.g. "Sent by auto-apply · 3 answers assumed" (`withAssumed`).
3. Settings -> Apply, group "How applying works", after "Show the browser window": row `#setting-fill-policy` "How forms are answered" (description = the design's two sentences; value "Always answer" / "Ask me first"; when `fill_policy_inactive` is set the value is that text, warn tone, and the editor starts with `.fill-inactive` "<text> Forms are answered with Ask me first until then."). Editor: radiogroup `input[name=apply-fill-policy]` (values always, strict) with the Mode row's keyboard rules. Choosing Always opens `.fill-confirm` ("Answer every field automatically?", `button.fill-confirm-yes` "Switch to always answer", `button.fill-confirm-no` "Keep asking me first"); nothing is sent until confirmed; Esc or "Keep asking me first" drops it; Ask me first saves at once. Saves `PUT {fill_policy}`. Row `#setting-always-trial` "Trial applications" (value = `always_trial_text`; presets Off, 3, 5 `.preset`; number field "Applications to leave unsent" 0..20; helper). Saves `PUT {always_trial_runs}`. Both through `save()` -> `saved` event -> Settings.vue dispatches `jobscraper:apply-settings`. Rows absent when the settings payload lacks `fill_policy` / `always_trial_runs`. "Write cover letters" description gained the design's sentence.
4. Profile work-rights warning: `p.work-rights-note.notice.warn` above the Questions section, only when `GET api/apply/status` -> `fill.fill_policy_effective === 'always'` and the form is readable: "Your profile says you may work in every country. Check the 'every other country' answer." (design 3.3, verbatim) when `singapore_status.authorised_to_work.default === true` and at most one nationality; or "Your profile has no work-rights answers, so right-to-work questions will be answered by assumption." when `singapore_status` exists with neither `authorised_to_work` nor `requires_sponsorship` filled. Button `.work-rights-fix` "Check work rights" focuses the field (`singapore_status.authorised_to_work`). Nothing when strict, when `fill` is absent (older server or a 404), or when the profile has no `singapore_status`.
Strings I wrote (the design gives none): the lead under "Answered for you"; "Basis: ..."; the `.q-nokeep` line; the two toasts; the two 409 messages and "Could not save the answer: ..." (the last follows the existing card); the Keep/Change aria-labels; the confirm title/body/buttons for Always (body differs by mode: auto "From now on JobScraper fills every field itself, including ones your profile and resume don't cover, and submits the form with those answers. They are listed under Answered for you in Profile afterwards." / dry run "...Dry run is on, so the form is left open for you. The assumed answers are listed under Answered for you in Profile."); `.fill-inactive` follow-on sentence; the trial field label and helper ("Off: applications are submitted as usual. 0 to 20." / "The next 3 applications are filled and left unsent (2 left). After that they are submitted as usual. 0 to 20." plus " It only applies when forms are answered automatically." while the effective policy is strict); "Review answers", "Add it in Profile", "Stopped: needs a <document>", "Needs your input: <fact>"; both work-rights texts except the design's first; "Check work rights".
Missing from the API (the UI renders nothing for each until it exists): (a) a per-attempt `trial` flag on `attempt_view` / queue `recent[]` (the flow only logs it), so "Trial: filled, not sent" cannot show today; a plain dry run still reads "Dry run ready" / "Ready to submit · N ..."; (b) no work-rights-completeness field in `apply/status` or settings: I derive the two warnings client-side from the applicant payload plus `fill.fill_policy_effective`; (c) `owner_fact_missing` carries no profile path, so its link goes to Application details (the captured question is there), not the exact field. Also still open from the W1 note: whether the trial counter should reset when the number is changed (the helper only shows what the server's `always_trial_text`/`left` say).
Tester notes: no existing pin changed (no exact-key or exact-count pin hit); new pins worth writing: the three chip labels, the Always confirm sending nothing until confirmed, the absence cases (no `counts`, no `fill`, no `fill_policy`), Keep/Change error mapping, deep link key cleared.

## 2026-11-12 software-engineer → coordinator; cc tester, flow lane (Q-121 fix-pins round 2: Q1-DOB, D2-retention, rulings 3-4)
TYPE: handoff
BODY: Branch `eng/Q-121-always-fixes` (+ merged tst/Q-121-always-fix-pins), commits fb8fc87 (1), a823bee (2), 94be442 (3), b45dc25 (4); `apply/always.py` only.
- 1 Q1 bases (`fact_basis`, `fact_of`): an owner-only fact's value stands only when its basis points at THAT fact and verifies: date of birth → `profile:identity.date_of_birth`; national ID → `profile:singapore_status.nric_last4`; expected salary → `profile:pay.expected_salary[.*]` or `standing:expected_pay_monthly|annual`; grade → `profile:education[i].grade|grade_scale` or `resume`; passport number and bank details → no profile path (only `extra:`); every class: an `extra:` entry whose `labels` ask for the same fact. Any other basis (assumption, default, job, unknown, any other `standing:` such as today / earliest_start / experience_years, another field's profile path such as the phone number) → unusable → A5 → A6 owner-fact stop.
- 2 D2-retention (`_f2`): marketing words (+ "keep me informed / keep in touch") → `talent_pool_opt_in`; a future-roles / on-file / retention question without one → `data_retention_consent` is True (else No; a required box stays unticked for the site).
- 3 ruling 3 (`certification`, `policy_ack`, `_f2`): a certification = a verb (declare, certify, confirm, attest, acknowledge) + a truth object (true, accurate, correct, complete) and NO matched declaration class; a policy acknowledgement = "have you read / read and understood / do you understand / agree to / accept / acknowledge" + policy, terms, conditions, notice, statement, code of conduct/ethics (a class word in the policy's name does not count). A6 F2: certification → Yes unless `certify_true` is false; privacy notice → Yes unless `privacy_consent` is false; any other policy → Yes; all assumed (F2). F2's "none applies" now needs a matched class (`classify_field` a class, or `classify_all` non-empty incl. unsure); a marker-only or unclassed question falls through the ladder ("anything else → No" is unchanged for those). A4 with a set `certify_true` / `privacy_consent`: see 4.
- 4 ruling 4 (`consent_key`, `consent_standing`, first in `standing_answer`): a yes/no or checkbox question whose key is marketing → talent_pool_opt_in, retention → data_retention_consent, certification → certify_true, a privacy notice or a consent + privacy/data-processing words with no class → privacy_consent; when the profile SETS that key (true or false) S1 is confident: written, source `profile:consents.<key>`, not assumed, the model's disagreement a note (standing_kept). Unset, a negated label, or a required box that would stay unticked → none (D2/D5/A6 as before: unticked).
- Verify (stdin, fake `ask` scripted wrong, sample profile): (a) DOB null, model "2026-11-12" citing `standing:today` / `standing:earliest_start`, and "91234567" citing `profile:contact.phone_number` (DATE and TEXT) → STOP owner_fact_missing 'date of birth' each; NRIC last 4 citing the phone path → STOP 'NRIC last 4 characters'; salary none citing `standing:experience_years` → STOP 'expected salary'; salary set citing `standing:expected_pay_monthly` → '5000', not assumed; DOB set citing its path → filled. (b) retention false + opt-in true, required box, silent → unticked (empty for the site); retention true → ticked, `profile:consents.data_retention_consent`, not assumed. (c) "Do you declare that the above is true?" silent → Yes `profile:consents.certify_true`, not assumed; certify_true unset → Yes fallback:F2 assumed; "Have you read our conflict of interest policy?" silent → Yes fallback:F2 assumed; "Do you declare any conflict of interest?" model Yes → No (`default:no_declaration`, assumed: the reader is tentative there, D1b). (d) retention false, model Yes citing that row → No, source the row, not assumed. Also: certify_true true, model No → Yes not assumed; opt-in true, model unticks → ticked not assumed.
- Tests: `-k always` 72/74 (two stale pins below, both encode what rulings 3/4 change), `-k layering` 13/13, `-k llm_fill` 99/99, `-k declarations` 167/167, `-k facts` 15/15, `-k pagefill` 22/22, `-k apply_flow` (alone) 148/148.
STATUS: open

BODY: Stale (do not keep): (1) `tests/test_always_fix_pins.py::test_d1b_a_question_that_is_not_a_declaration_is_never_turned_into_no`, label "Do you declare that the above is true?", model Yes citing `default:no_declaration`: asserts `a.assumed` and "differs from default:no_declaration" in evidence; ruling 3+4 → Yes, source `profile:consents.certify_true`, NOT assumed, evidence empty (the "dismissed" label keeps the old assertions; the conflict-of-interest-policy half stays green). ## 2026-11-12 software-engineer → coordinator; cc tester (Q-121 consent pins round: rulings A-D built)
TYPE: handoff
BODY: Branch `eng/Q-121-always-fixes` (+ merged tst/Q-121-always-fix-pins 8ef1e3d), commits 6bd1bfb (A), 96cf809 (B), 9092024 (C), 22cbe09 (D); `apply/always.py` only.
- A (`consent_standing` kind "refused", `refused_stop`, `decide`): `data_retention_consent` / `certify_true` / `privacy_consent` set to false where no option states No (a required box, a list with no No) → never ticked, whatever the model cites (the row itself, `default:consent`). Required → `AlwaysStop(owner_fact_missing)`, target `consents.<key>`, detail "consent to keep your details on file (required by the form; your profile says no)" / "certification that your information is true (…)" / "consent to process your personal data (…)"; raised in A4, before anything is typed. Optional → blank, note "your profile says no to this consent: left unticked" (+ "the AI answered true"). A yes/no question with a No option keeps the round-2 rule: No from the row, not assumed, no stop. `talent_pool_opt_in` is not in A (D2/D6 unchanged: a required marketing box stays unticked for the site). Note: REASON_TEXTS[owner_fact_missing] reads "This form asks for your {detail}, which isn't in your profile or resume": the tail does not fit a refusal; a refusal-specific text is a contracts change (architect).
- B (`confirm_vote`, `owner_truth_text`, `TASK_CONFIRM`, `pick_call(task=)`): tentative reader + `Standing.truth` false (every matched class false) + a model answer that differs, any basis except a `default:` ref whose rule writes (`_d1b_decides`: D1b keeps it) → the model's answer is not written; ONE confirmation call. Task line: `WANTED is the applicant's truth. Pick the one option that, as the answer to QUESTION, states WANTED; mind any negation in QUESTION. Copy it exactly, or answer "". Reply with one JSON object: {"choice": "<option>"}.` Body = the R2 layout (QUESTION / WANTED / OPTIONS (JSON)); WANTED = "The applicant " + each class's no-sentence from `briefing.DECLARATION_STATEMENTS` (+ three employer classes), e.g. "The applicant has no conflict of interest or audit-independence issue with any employer."; options = the field's, or ["Yes","No"]. Budget: +1 call per such field (a second only on an unparseable reply, as R2), max_tokens 200, counted in `stats.calls/seconds` → `PageOutcome.model_calls`. Pick = the model's answer (and not the reader's) → the model's answer; anything else (the reader's, "", off-list, BackendError/TimeoutError) → the reader's. Always assumed, source the reader's (`default:no_declaration`), note "votes: the AI answered Yes; your standing answer No; the check said No: your standing answer written". Confident reader, a true class, and `code_wins` (one negation: the reader's answer is already written, never the model's) are unchanged.
- C (`last_resort`): a certification or a terms / code / policy acknowledgement goes straight to F2, never F0 (F0's tier-2 privacy row answered "Do you understand our terms and conditions?" No from `privacy_consent: false`). `policy_ack` → "privacy" only for privacy / personal data / data protection / PDPA wording.
- D (`consent_standing` kind "derived", `Standing.reason`): a certification with `certify_true` unset, or a policy acknowledgement whose key is unset or none (any terms / code / policy; a privacy notice with `privacy_consent` unset) → Yes / ticked, source `default:consent`, assumed, evidence "an acknowledgement or certification the form asks for: agreed (D5), assumed", the model's other answer a note ("the AI answered No; your standing answer was kept"). A plain consent ("Do you consent to the processing of your personal data?", unset) is not D: the model's answer stands.
- Verify (stdin, scripted `ask`, sample profile): (a) each of the three keys false, required box, model ticks citing `profile:consents.<key>` and `default:consent` → STOP owner_fact_missing, target `consents.<key>` (6/6); optional → 'false', note "the AI answered true; …" (6/6). (b) "Do you declare any conflict of interest?" model Yes (basis assumption): confirm "No" → No, assumed, 2 calls; confirm "Yes" → Yes, assumed; confirm "" or "Maybe" → No, assumed; WANTED as above. Confident control "Do you have any conflict of interest?" model Yes → No, not assumed, 1 call, no confirmation. Negated "I confirm I have no conflict of interest" model Yes → Yes (no confirmation: agrees with the reader). (c) `privacy_consent: false`: "Do you understand our terms and conditions?" / code of conduct / anti-bribery policy, silent, model No citing `default:consent` or the privacy row → Yes, `default:consent`, assumed; "Do you agree to our privacy notice/policy?" model Yes → No, `profile:consents.privacy_consent`, not assumed. (d) certify unset, model No (RADIO and box) → Yes / 'true', assumed, note "the AI answered No/false; …"; privacy notice with privacy unset, model No → Yes assumed.
- Tests: `-k always` 82/87 (the five stale pins in the tester request below, each encoding what A or D changes; the ruling-B pin passes, see (6) there), `-k layering` 13/13, `-k llm_fill` 99/99, `-k declarations` 169/169, `-k facts` 15/15, `-k pagefill` 22/22, `-k apply_flow` (alone) 148/148.
STATUS: open
