## 2026-11-12 software-engineer → coordinator; cc tester (Q-121 fourth fix: ruling A on list-shaped consents)
TYPE: handoff
BODY: Branch `eng/Q-121-always-fixes` (+ merged tst/Q-121-always-fix-pins 421ca8a), commit 522ea11; `apply/always.py` only.
- `consent_key` now also reads a single-choice list (dropdown / radio with options, `_list_shaped`). `option_stance(label)`: a "prefer not to say" wording (DECLINE_WORDS minus "decline") → neither; else refusing if any of `not, do not, don't, don’t, dont, decline, disagree, refuse, no, opt out, withhold` (tested first); else agreeing if any of `agree, accept, acknowledge, consent, yes, confirm, certify, understand, allow, permit, authorise, authorize, opt in, i do`; else neither. For an explicitly set key on a list: the first option whose stance matches the key wins (confident, source `profile:consents.<key>`, not assumed, the model's pick a note), in both directions; then `fit_bool` as before. Key false and no refusing option (incl. a list neither side recognises): retention / certify / privacy → the ruling-A stop when required (target `consents.<key>`), blank with note "…: left empty" when optional; marketing (`talent_pool_opt_in`) on a list → required: left empty for the site's validation (`empty_for_site`, no stop, A5/A6 skip it), optional: blank. Boxes and Yes/No questions are unchanged.
- Verify (stdin, `ask` scripted to the agree option): single "I agree" dropdown required → STOP owner_fact_missing target `consents.<key>` (3/3 keys); optional → blank_optional, note "the AI answered I agree; your profile says no to this consent: left empty"; pairs I agree/I do not agree, I acknowledge/I do not acknowledge, Accept/Decline, Yes/No (RADIO and DROPDOWN) with `privacy_consent: false` → the refusing option, `profile:consents.privacy_consent`, not assumed (8/8); key true + model "I do not agree" → "I agree", not assumed; "Option A"/"Option B" required, retention false → STOP; key unset → the model's pick stands (unchanged); marketing "I agree" list required, opt-in false → empty for the site, no stop; marketing pair → "No thanks".
- Tests: `-k always` 98/98, `-k layering` 13/13, `-k llm_fill` 99/99, `-k declarations` 169/169, `-k pagefill` 22/22, `-k apply_flow` (alone) 148/148.
- Risk to note: `consent_key`'s phrase lists (`_RETENTION` "other roles", "future roles", …) now also reach dropdowns; a required non-consent list such as "Which future roles interest you?" (options Engineering / Finance) with `data_retention_consent: false` would stop. The owner's profile has it true today. A narrower list gate (a consent verb in the label) is a design call; not built.
- Pins to add (tester): the cases above, esp. the marketing list (empty, no stop), the true direction, a "Prefer not to say" option never taken as the refusal, and the unset-key control on a single "I agree" list.
STATUS: open

## 2026-11-12 software-engineer -> coordinator; cc tester, architect (Q-121 review F6 handoff)
TYPE: handoff
RE: docs/reviews/Q-121-flow-code.md F6; apply_design §7.5 (company match); D-55, D-69; `src/jobscraper/apply/declarations.py`
BODY: Branch `eng/Q-121-F6-employer-match` (from int/Q-121 e1ea40d). Commit fdbb1e8 (declarations.py only).
- Rule as built (`company_match`, signature unchanged; `_matches_employer`, `prior_employment`, `current_employment` unchanged and so are aliases: each alias goes through the same rule). Tokens (`_match_tokens`): §7.1 `norm_label`, a leading "the" dropped, then the trailing run of `_COMPANY_TAIL` words (legal forms, group/holdings/holding/branch, singapore/sg) dropped while others remain, never a word right after "of". Match = equal lists, or one list a prefix of the other with every extra word in `_COMPANY_GENERIC` and not right after "of". A one-token name under 3 characters matches only an equal list.
- Lists (module top, beside `_COMPANY_NOISE`): `_COMPANY_FORMS` 35 (legal forms + structure words; built from `_COMPANY_NOISE` minus singapore/sg/bank), `_COMPANY_PLACES` 71, `_COMPANY_ENTITY` 17 (exactly the delegation's list), `_COMPANY_GENERIC` = their union (covers every `_COMPANY_NOISE` word), `_COMPANY_TAIL` 30. Added beyond the delegation's words: legal forms `private`, `pvt` (Singapore's "Private Limited" is "Pte Ltd"), structure `branch` ("Singapore Branch"), places beyond the named ones (asean, apj, japac, north, west, middle, hongkong, prc, taiwan, anz, zealand, nz, canada, latam, mena, africa, uae, dubai, five European countries, twelve more cities).
- `_COMPANY_NOISE` and `_company_tokens` are NOT changed: they are `employer_norm`, the stored key of `by_employer` overrides and question sightings, and the equality reader `_employer_spans`. Extending them would orphan stored keys, so the new lists reuse the noise set by union instead.
- Why the tail strip and the "of" guard: the delegation's rule applied on the existing normaliser makes "Bank of Singapore" -> [of] a prefix of "Bank of America" -> [of, america] (a generic extra): True. Tokens now keep mid-name words, and a place after "of" is the name. Only Singapore/SG (and legal/structure words) are stripped from both names, because stripping other places from both ends makes "Air China" equal "Air India".
Truth table (stdin script, both argument orders agree on every row): ("Example","Example Logistics") False; ("Example Co","Example Logistics") False; ("Northwind","Northwind Holdings Ltd") True; ("DBS","DBS Bank Ltd") True; ("Grab","Grab Holdings Inc.") True; ("Google","Google Asia Pacific Pte. Ltd.") True; ("Standard Chartered","Standard Chartered Bank (Singapore) Limited") True; ("Bank of America","Bank of Singapore") False; ("Morgan Stanley","Morgan Advanced Materials") False; ("Shell","Shell Eastern Petroleum") False; ("GS","GS Yuasa") False; ("Example","Example") True. Extra: ("Example Pte Ltd","Example Singapore") True; ("Standard Chartered Bank","Standard Chartered Singapore") True; ("DBS Bank Ltd","DBS Bank (Hong Kong) Limited") True; ("Bank of America","Bank of America Singapore Limited") True; ("Air China","Air India") False; ("Development Bank of Singapore","Development Bank of Japan") False; ("Example Bank","Example Capital") False; ("The Example Group","Example") True; ("Example Private Limited","Example") True; ("Example","Example Pte Ltd (Singapore Branch)") True; ("GS","GS Pte Ltd") True; ("Example",""): False; ("Grab","Grab Financial Group") False (see stale pin). Profile experience [Example Logistics (no end), DBS Bank Ltd (ended), Google Asia Pacific Pte. Ltd. (end "")]: employer "Example" -> prior False, current False; "DBS" -> prior True, current False; "Google" -> prior True, current True; "Example Logistics Pte Ltd" -> True/True; "Sample" with alias "Example" -> False/False. `employer_norm("Shopee Pte Ltd")` still "shopee".
Shell: the truthful answer for the real pair is Yes (Shell Eastern Petroleum (Pte) Ltd is Shell's Singapore operating company), but by name it is "Shell" + an industry word, the same shape as "Example Logistics". That belongs in aliases (aliases.yaml group / `employer_aliases`), not in the word lists.
Stale pin (tester; not edited): `tests/test_apply_contracts.py::test_company_match_rules` line 409 `assert m("Grab", "Grab Financial Group")` now fails ("financial" is not on the entity list). For the real company the truthful answer is Yes (Grab Financial Group is Grab's own financial-services arm), but the name rule cannot tell "<brand> Financial" from another firm without the word on the list. The pin's other three asserts (lines 410-412) hold (checked in the truth table). QUESTION (coordinator/architect): add "financial" to `_COMPANY_ENTITY` (same kind of word as capital/securities, keeps AD §7.5's own example) or keep the list and rewrite the pin? I did not add it (parameter choice). AD §7.5 line 854 still describes the old prefix rule and its Grab example: needs the architect's update either way.
Other callers whose behaviour changed (all toward "not the same company"): (1) `names_employer` / `names_this_employer` (D-90 4, Q-46c): a label naming only a shorter form of a longer employer name with a non-generic extra ("worked at Example" for employer "Example Logistics") no longer names this employer; its run width now uses `_match_tokens`. (2) `facts.other_employers` and `apply_flow.other_employers` (G12): a watchlist company that shares only a first word with this employer ("Example Logistics" vs "Example") is no longer exempt, so G12 now guards its name (correct); with `composed=True`, an own past employer "Example Logistics" no longer exempts a watchlist "Example", so a composed text naming "Example Logistics" can fail G12 unless the resume entries name it (fail-closed). (3) `facts._score` (E2 entry matching): a site entry whose employer differs by a non-generic word no longer matches a profile entry (leaves it unmatched). The alias-group join in `apply_flow.other_employers` follows the same rule.
Verify (baseline on e1ea40d -> after): `-k declarations` 167/167 -> 167/167; `-k always` 37/38 -> 37/38 (same red: `test_always_fill::test_a_basis_that_does_not_exist_or_does_not_match_is_flagged_assumed_not_vetoed`, gpa F4, another lane's); `-k llm_fill` 99/99 -> 99/99; `-k resolve` 54/54 -> 54/54; `-k layering` 13/13 -> 13/13; `-k apply_contracts` 72/72 -> 71/72 (the stale pin above); `-k facts` 15/15 -> 15/15. Callers' files after the change: `-k test_apply_flow_n6b` 20/20, `-k test_tailor_compose` 27/27, `-k test_q74_llm_fill_hooks` 21/21, `-k test_apply_contracts_v5` 16/16, `-k q46` 10/10, `-k apply_d70` 19/19, `-k widgets` 13/13, `-k apply_questions` 27/27. Not run: `-k apply_flow` / `-k apply_e2e` with Edge (gate them before the merge, as for every apply merge).
Pins to add (tester, e.g. `test_apply_declarations_f6.py`): the twelve delegation pairs plus Bank of America vs "Bank of America Singapore Limited" (True), "Air China" vs "Air India" (False), "Development Bank of Singapore" vs "... of Japan" (False), "Example Bank" vs "Example Capital" (False), "Example Pte Ltd" vs "Example Singapore" (True), "GS" vs "GS Pte Ltd" (True); symmetry of every pair; `prior_employment`/`current_employment` on the dummy profile above (Example -> False/False is the F6 case); an alias goes through the same rule ("Sample" + alias "Example" vs "Example Logistics" -> False); `names_this_employer("Have you worked at Example before?", ["Example Logistics"])` False and `(".. at DBS ..", ["DBS Bank Ltd"])` True; `employer_norm` unchanged ("Shopee Pte Ltd" -> "shopee", "Bank of Singapore" -> "of").
STATUS: open

## 2026-11-12 software-engineer -> coordinator; cc Vue lane, tester (Q-121 W2 web follow-up)
TYPE: handoff
BODY: Branch `eng/Q-121-W2-web` (from int/Q-121 7a547c9). Commits: 7e1d6ac item 1, (attempt-row commit) item 2, 3ce6377 item 3. Files: `web/routers/questions.py`, `apply.py`, `applications.py`, `apply/questions.py` (only `kept_value` + a new `_number`).
1. D1 fixed. `kept_value` / router `_kept_value`: kind `number` -> a number via `_number` (int when whole: "3", 3, 3.0 -> 3; else float: "2.5", "3.50" -> 3.5; a bool, list, file, text, "1,000", NaN, inf -> None); every other kind unchanged, so a "3.50" TEXT answer is kept exactly as sent (only the kind decides). The two copies are the same code; `-k web_always` agreement pin green. `can_keep` is now true only when Keep will succeed: the item's payload is applied to a COPY of the profile through the same `_apply_answer` and `applicant.validate`, and only errors that write adds (vs. the profile's own) turn it false. `_settle` and the check share `_prepare`. The number pin (`years_with_python` -> 3, `availability.notice_period_days` -> 30) is green.
2. Attempt rows (`last_attempt`, `/attempts`, queue `recent[]`) gain: `entries_note` (string|null, from `store.entries_notes`, one query per list), `trial` (bool) + `trial_text` ("Trial: filled, not sent" when true, else ""), `owner_fact_path` (string|null, only for `owner_fact_missing`). NOT DONE IN FULL, needs the store/flow lane: the store records neither a per-attempt trial marker (the flow only logs it; `apply_attempts` has no column) nor the profile path of an owner fact (the path lives on the captured question, and there is no store method from attempt to its sightings' `target_path`). The fields exist and read `row["trial"]` / `row["owner_fact_path"]` when the store provides them, so today `trial` is always false and `owner_fact_path` always null. Smallest fix: a nullable `apply_attempts.trial` column set where `at.trial` is known (or a note like `entries_note`), and `Store.attempt_question_paths(attempt_ids) -> {attempt_id: target_path}` (question_sightings join questions, reason profile_gap) merged into `attempt()`/`attempts_for_job`/`last_attempts`/`recent_attempts` rows as `owner_fact_path`.
3. Copy (final basis strings, `_BASIS_TEXTS`): assumption "there was nothing in your profile or resume to base it on" (unchanged: true only for a model guess, which is the only source `assumption` covers); fallback "the AI gave no usable answer, so JobScraper used a safe fallback"; default "JobScraper's default for this kind of question"; profile "it was taken from your profile, but JobScraper could not confirm that it fits"; resume "it was taken from your resume, but JobScraper could not confirm that it fits"; job "it was taken from the job posting, but JobScraper could not confirm that it fits"; standing "it is worked out from your profile, and JobScraper is not sure it fits"; declaration "it follows your declarations, and JobScraper is not sure it fits"; llm "it is the AI's best guess"; any other "it is JobScraper's best guess". (A profile/resume/job/standing/declaration source on an assumed row means the basis check or the reader failed, hence the "could not confirm".) The card sentence is unchanged. The lead line "These had no answer in your profile or resume" lives in web/src (Vue lane): it should say e.g. "JobScraper filled these in itself. Check them, then keep or change each."
4. Private answers: no change. The existing listings return values in clear by design: `GET /api/questions` answered groups return `current.value` for sensitive questions unmasked, and `GET /api/applicant` returns the whole profile; there is no server-side masking pattern, the Vue card masks (tester pinned that). The assumed item keeps `sensitive: true` so the card can mask `answer`/`text`/`value`; Keep reads the value from the store, Change takes the client's. D-201/D-202 concern the model and stored quotes, not this owner-only local API.
Tester: (a) fixture bug in `tests/test_web_always.py::test_keep_saves_each_kind_in_the_shape_the_resolver_reads` (red on this base, not from my change): `_answer_row` stores `field_type` = the question kind ("multi"), but since the flow's D1 store fix `assumed_answer` decodes the JSON list only for real FieldType values (`multi_choice`, `checkbox`, `file`), so the multi row's value comes back as the text '["Hybrid", "Remote"]' and Keep is refused 422. Use `field_type="multi_choice"` for kind multi (verified: with that, all five Keeps return 200 and `can_keep` is true). (b) pins to add: `_number` parity incl. "3.50" for text vs number, "1,000", NaN, True; `can_keep` false for a number-kind answer "abc" and for a number gap whose value the profile check refuses (e.g. -5 days); `entries_note` on the three surfaces; `trial` false and `owner_fact_path` null today (flip when the store lands); basis strings above.
Verify: `-k web_` 154/155 (the fixture red above), `-k questions` 79/79, `-k settings` 25/25, `-k connections` 39/39, `-k layering` 13/13, `-k applications` 10/10.
STATUS: open

TYPE: handoff
BODY: Branch `eng/Q-121-A1-BP` (from int/Q-121 4f9aac7). Commits: 9a1caa6 A1-B (`apply/briefing.py`), 0c51d5d A1-P (`apply/facts.py`). No other file touched; every public name and signature kept.
A1-B as built. (1) Each STANDING RULES label ends with `(class: ...)` built from `contracts.FieldClass` values (`RULE_CLASSES`, `class_tag(rule_id)`): D1 declaration; D2 declaration; D3 work_right, sponsorship; D4 self_id; D5 consent_privacy, consent_retention, certification, acknowledgement; D6 marketing; D7/D8/D9 ordinary; D10 declaration, self_id. Tag sits in the label field (`default:consent | Consents needed to apply (D5) (class: ...) | text`), refs unchanged. (2) D1 gains the C3 `other` sentence before "Answer so ...": when no declaration in the profile is true (the nine keys plus any unknown bool key; `willing_to_sign_nda`, health, holdings do not count) "The applicant has no other adverse history either: no dismissal or resignation in lieu, litigation or legal proceedings, an investigation, a sanction, a visa refusal or deportation applies (which: other); ..."; when any is true: "Other adverse history (...; which: other): the profile does not say; do not claim that none applies, and mark the answer assumed." (3) COMPUTED FACTS gains `standing:age | Age in years (computed from the date of birth) | 24` (sample profile), printed before `age_18_plus`/`age_21_plus`, absent without a date of birth (birthday boundary checked: DOB 2000-11-13 -> 25 on 2026-11-12, 26 on 2026-11-13). (4) The JD is placed by `briefing.job_block` (always.py only calls it, line 2662): new pure helper `briefing.fence_job_description(text, *, max_chars=6000) -> str` returns the fence open line, the first 6000 chars (or "(not set)") with every line whose first non-blank characters are `==` rewritten to `--` (indent kept), and the close line; constants `JD_FENCE_OPEN`, `JD_FENCE_CLOSE`. `job_block` uses it and no longer prints the "Job description (first 6000 characters):" caption. A1-A: nothing to call unless you place a JD yourself; use the helper then.
A1-B measures (sample profile, TODAY 2026-11-12, chars/3.6): briefing 4075 -> 4244 tokens (+169, all in the cached prefix: tags ~70, D1 line ~55, age ~15, rest ~30); JOB block 200 -> 218 (after the prefix). Two builds byte-identical (text, hash, refs); JOB block deterministic; order unchanged: HEADER, PROFILE, ANSWERS, COMPUTED, RULES, RESUME, then JOB/JD/per-call parts after the prefix. A1-A: rule 12 and TASK_MUST wording are yours; the tags already name the same class strings as the schema enum.
A1-P as built (`facts.py`). `OTHER_PERSON` is now `OTHER_PERSON_BASE` (the old 14 words) + `OTHER_PERSON_ADDED` (referrer(s), referral, referred, mentor(s), recruiter(s), colleague(s), friend(s), relative(s), "contact person(s)"; supervisor was already there). Narrowing, because these words appear in ordinary questions (recorded as a decision for you to veto): the ADDED words count only when the field type can hold a person's details (TEXT, TEXTAREA, EMAIL, PHONE, NUMBER, URL, TYPEAHEAD; never a yes/no, checkbox or list); never when the label/section/context holds hear, heard or source ("Referral source", "How did you hear about us? (referral)"); and relative/friend/colleague only beside a detail word (name, email, mail, phone, mobile, telephone, tel, cell, number, address, contact, details) so "Do you have a relative working here?" and "Please list friends or relatives who work here" stay relatives declarations, not other_person. Preceding-field rule (exact condition, `_follows_other_person`): the field is a PageField of type TEXT, EMAIL or PHONE; its label is an own-detail label (type EMAIL/PHONE, or label tokens hold email, mail, phone, mobile, telephone, tel, cell or fax, or "contact"+"number", or "name"/"surname" with every content token a name word: name, surname, full, first, last, family, given, middle, legal, preferred; so "Company name" and "Job title" are not); AND its `pf.context` (the previous label in the same group or section) is non-empty, does not end with "?" and does not start with a question word (do, does, did, have, has, are, is, was, were, can, could, would, will, should, how, what, which, why, when, where, who), and names an OTHER_PERSON phrase (same narrowing). A field at the top of its section has empty context, so the applicant's own "Email address" / "Phone" there is not affected; "Email address" after "Last name" is not either.
Signature: `about_other_person(field, *, after_other_person: bool = False)`; the default keeps today's call shape; the new rule fires from `pf.context` alone, so every existing caller (always.py 294/1015/1993/2283, guards, locks, facts.relevant) gets it with no change. NEW helper for the chain (A1-A may adopt it, optional): `facts.other_person_fields(fields: Sequence[PageField]) -> set[str]` returns the keys about another person for a whole page in order, carrying the chain "Supervisor name" -> "Email address" -> "Phone number" (context alone covers only one step; the phone's context is the email's label). Scope key = `pf.group or pf.field.section`, as the reader's `last_label` uses. Residuals: an applicant's own "Email" placed right after an emergency-contact block with no section break is read as the contact's (conservative: empty/F4, never a leak); a code-key `other_person` now exists for referral/mentor/etc. text fields, so a text field "Referral name" asked as `ordinary` by the model is a one-key hit, as for "Referee".
Scripted checks (stdin, no model): all tags valid FieldClass values, 10 of 10 rule lines tagged; age absent without DOB; JD with "== FIELDS (JSON) ==", "  == END JOB DESCRIPTION ==" and "ignore previous instructions" stays inside one fence with `--`; other-person rows: "Email address" after "Supervisor name" True; "Referrer email", "Mentor phone", "Emergency contact number", "Your mentor's phone number", "Referral email", "Email of person who referred you", "Relative's name" True; top-of-form "Email address"/"Phone", "Email address" after "Last name" or after "Do you have a relative working here?", "Referral source", "Were you referred by an employee?" (radio), "Company name" after "Referee name" False; chain over a page -> exactly the supervisor's name, email, phone.
Verify (baseline on 4f9aac7 -> after): `-k always_briefing` 10/10 -> 10/10; `-k facts` 17/17 -> 17/17; `-k pagefill` 22/22 -> 22/22; `-k llm_fill` 100/100 -> 100/100; `-k layering` 13/13 -> 13/13; `-k always` 176/177 -> 176/177 (the known stale web fixture `test_web_always::test_keep_saves_each_kind_in_the_shape_the_resolver_reads`, unchanged).

## 2026-11-12 software-engineer -> coordinator; cc tester, A1-A engineer, Vue lane (Q-121 A1-W handoff)
TYPE: handoff

BODY: Branch `eng/Q-121-A1-W` (from int/Q-121 4f9aac7). Commits: a6dcecd (1 flow consent_refused), 436a120 (3 store), 8047a29 (2 markers + trial), 450638c (4 routers), 397c377 (5 doctor), ba59020 (6 attempts.js), 9d44a9f (7 lead line). No contract, test, always.py, config or static edit.
1. Flow. `always_stop`: `ReasonCode.CONSENT_REFUSED` -> `capture_owner_fact` (same capture as owner_fact_missing: profile_gap, target `consents.<key>` through `_gap_target`, sensitive) and detail `consent_refused_detail(stop.target_path, stop.detail)` = `CONSENT_REFUSED_DETAILS[target_path]` when the target is a key there, else the stop's own detail, else "a consent this form requires" (no label/step suffix, unlike F4's owner-fact detail, so the same consent reads the same on every role). Then `ApplyFailure(CONSENT_REFUSED)`: per role, no pause, not returned. `pause_for`: `_PER_ROLE_NAMED = {owner_fact_missing: "is missing from your profile", consent_refused: "is refused in your profile"}`; three in a row, all the same code and the same `owner_fact_of(detail)` -> `REPEATED_FAILURES` "<detail> is refused in your profile"; different details, or a mix of the two codes -> None (never the generic streak).
   WHAT I EXPECT THE STOP TO CARRY (A1-A): `AlwaysStop(ReasonCode.CONSENT_REFUSED, CONSENT_REFUSED_DETAILS["consents.<key>"], step=<step title>, field_key=pf.field.key, label=pf.field.label, target_path="consents.<key>", sensitive=True)`, i.e. `_owner_fact`'s shape with the new reason; the strictest refused key of a combined box (C5) as `target_path`. Raised before anything is typed, like today.
2. Markers: `FILL_NO_USABLE_ANSWERS, FILL_WOULD_BE_GUESSES, FILL_LAST_RESORT = contracts.CONTENT_FAILURE_MARKERS`, `CONTENT_FAILURE_DETAILS = tuple(...)` (names kept for callers/tests); the import-time `AL.CONTENT_FAILURE_DETAILS` check is gone (F10). The job-log line of an `unanswerable_required` content failure now prints `CONTENT_FAILURE_TEXT`. Trial: `attempt()` writes `trial=1` on the row (batch: after create_attempt; queue: with the mode update) only when `choose_policy()` made it a trial.
3. Store: SCHEMA_VERSION 8; `_migrate` adds `apply_attempts.trial INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0` after a `PRAGMA table_info` check through `_add_column`, which also swallows "duplicate column name" (two processes opening at once). `trial` joins `_ATTEMPT_COLUMNS`. `attempt_question_paths(ids) -> {attempt_id: target_path}`: sightings JOIN questions, target_path set, `q.reason != 'assumed'`, newest sighting wins, chunks of 500, no DDL. `retry_candidates` includes `consent_refused`.
4. Routers: `_NEEDS_INPUT` += consent_refused; `content_failure(reason, detail)` = unanswerable_required whose detail starts (lstrip, lower) with a contract marker -> kind `stopped`, `CONTENT_FAILURE_TEXT` text and hint. `attempt_view(..., owner_fact_path=None)`: the passed path, else `row["owner_fact_path"]`, only for a FAILED owner_fact_missing or consent_refused (so a consent attempt also carries its `consents.<key>` path). `trial` = the row flag (any state); `trial_text` "Trial: filled, not sent" only when kind is `dry_run` (a trial that stopped earlier was not filled; W2 had it for any trial). `/applications`, `/applications/{id}/attempts`, `/apply/queue` recent[] pass `attempt_question_paths` (one query per list). Key set unchanged.
5. `doctor` prints, after the fill-policy lines, "profile shape  no values shown: true / false, set / not set, counts", then `consents:` (each FORM key true/false/not set), `declarations:` (each FORM key; lists and "decline" read "set"), `identity:` gender / nationality / date of birth set|not set, `work rights:` authorised_to_work and requires_sponsorship "default row set|not set, N country rows" (or "not set"), `address and education:` address_line1 / postal_code / education grade set|not set. Keys come from `applicant.FORM`, so a new consent or declaration appears by itself. No file: one line "no profile file yet (...)"; unreadable or not a mapping: one line with the exception CLASS only (an ApplicantError message may quote a value).
6. attempts.js: `refusedConsent(a)` parses the consent sentence; needs_input + consent_refused -> "Needs your agreement: <detail>" (fallback "Needs your agreement"); `contentFailure(a)` (kind stopped + unanswerable_required) -> the generic stopped chip with the contract text (clipped; full text in the title) and `noRetry`, so `canRetry` is false; `answerTarget(a)` = a valid `owner_fact_path`, else "consents" for consent_refused, else ''; rowState's needs_input carries `target`; `openAnswer(target)` sets sessionStorage `jobscraper.profile.field`, opens Profile details and fires `jobscraper:show-field` {path}. Trial chip uses `trial_text` when sent, else "Trial: filled, not sent". `missingFact` drops F4's trailing "(“label” on step)" so the chip stays "Needs your input: date of birth". All absent-safe (older server: unchanged labels).
7. QuestionsInbox lead: "JobScraper filled these for you; check they are right. Keep an answer to use it next time, or change it."
Verify (baseline on 4f9aac7 -> after): `-k layering` 13/13 -> 13/13; `-k store` 107/107 -> 104/107 (3 SCHEMA_VERSION pins below); `-k apply_queue` 47/47 -> 46/47 (same); `-k always_flow` 53/53 -> 53/53; `-k web_` 154/155 -> 154/155 (known keep fixture red); `-k q76` 14/15 -> 14/15 (known key-set pin); `-k applications` 10/10 -> 10/10; `-k questions` 79/79 -> 79/79; `-k apply_cli` 23/23 -> 23/23; `-k test_cli` 15/15; `-k apply_flow` alone 150/150; `-k apply_e2e` alone 13/13; vitest 1080 passed 1 failed (ux1 Companies, flaky on the base) -> 1080 passed 1 failed (the lead-line pin below); `npx vite build --outDir <temp>` "built in 3.17s". Scripted (stdin, temp DBs, tests' fakes read-only): consent_refused 14/14 (capture target, next role runs, no pause, not returned, x3 pause text, different details None, mixed codes None, owner-fact rule unchanged); trial 5/5 (batch and queue trial rows trial=1 mode dry_run, no submit; a plain dry run 0; content-failure log text); routers 13/13 (three surfaces; consent text and path; content failure stopped; assumed sighting excluded from the path; key set unchanged); migration v7 -> v8 with rows in every table, a reader holding a read transaction, then a second open (no change) and four concurrent opens (one column, meta 8): all pass; doctor with planted values 10/10, none printed, missing and malformed files one line each.

## 2026-11-12 software-engineer -> coordinator; cc tester (Q-121 A1-W item 6: "Answer in Profile" opens the field)
TYPE: handoff
BODY: Branch `eng/Q-121-A1-link` (from int/Q-121 bc5e9f9), code commit 4c235a7. Files: `web/src/components/ApplicationRow.vue`, `web/src/tabs/Applications.vue`, `web/src/components/ApplicationDetails.vue`. `ApplyQueuePanel.vue` has no such link (unchanged).
Mechanism (the one `attempts.js` already provides): the row's `.app-answer` link calls `openAnswer(state.target)`; the attempts-list button `.attempt-answer` (Applications.vue) calls `openAnswer(answerTarget(a))`. `openAnswer` stores the path in sessionStorage `jobscraper.profile.field`, sets the Profile view to details, goes to `#/profile`, and dispatches `jobscraper:show-field` {detail: {path}}. ApplicationDetails: `showWishedField(path?)` runs after every `load()` and on that event: reads and clears the key (always, so a stale wish never fires later), waits a tick, then `fillIn(path)` when `findItem` knows the path (opens a closed `<details>`, `scrollIntoView({block:'center'})` with no smooth behaviour so reduced motion is respected, focuses the first control; the same function as the "Fill in" links), else `goSection(id)` when it is a group id (the `consents` fallback for consent_refused with no path: scrolls to the group, focuses `h3#g-consents`), else nothing (page stays at the top). Listener added in onMounted, removed in onBeforeUnmount.
Without a path (older server, or a fact with no path): `answerTarget` is '' so `openAnswer('')` is exactly `openProfile('details')`: unchanged. The "Add it in Profile" document link is untouched. No new visible text.
Tester notes: selectors `a.app-answer` (row), `button.attempt-answer` (attempts list); after click `sessionStorage['jobscraper.profile.field']` holds the path until Application details has loaded, then it is removed; assert `document.activeElement` is the control inside `#<fieldId(path)>` (group heading `#g-consents`). Cases: `identity.date_of_birth` focuses the date control; `consents.data_retention_consent` its checkbox/select; `consents` focuses `#g-consents`; unknown path leaves focus alone and clears the key; empty target sets no key. Event path: with the form drawn, dispatch `new CustomEvent('jobscraper:show-field', {detail:{path}})` on window.

BODY: Branch `eng/Q-121-A1-A-always` (merged tst/Q-121-A1 and int/Q-121 up to 26fbf5c). One source file: `src/jobscraper/apply/always.py`. Steps 1-7 built: always:v2 schema/prompt (3da980a); code keys, T/H per class, two-key `decide`, class rows, protective ends, M2 (a)/(c), M3, M6 (67b65d8 + the triage commit after it).
Entry point the fake calls: `code_key(pf: PageField, ctx: PageCtx) -> tuple[Optional[FieldClass], str]` ((None, "") = no class; (ORDINARY, "") = positively ordinary, C4). Richer: `code_key_full(pf, ctx, standing=None) -> CodeKey` (klass, which, classes, ordinary, confident). Other public helpers: `consent_frames(text)`, `fact_which(pf)`, `self_id_which(text)`, `option_states(label, applies_yes, ctx)`, `implicit_negated(text)`, `IMPLICIT_NEGATORS`, `class_rule(...)`, `in_h(...)`, `protective_end(...)`, `confirm_class(...)`, `m3_tokens`, `CALLS_PAGE_EXTRA`.
Consent refusal: `AlwaysStop(CONSENT_REFUSED, CONSENT_REFUSED_DETAILS[key], step, field_key, label, target_path="consents.<key>", sensitive=True)`; a combined box names the first refused part in the order privacy, retention, certification, acknowledgement, marketing (marketing-only never stops).
Cache seam (tester group 28): `fill_page_always(..., classes=out_dict)` fills `out_dict[field_key] = "<class string>"` (the model's class, else code's key). Restore: `always.decision(key, row, via)` reads `row["class"]` (pin 28 at always level). A test can exercise both without a browser: call `fill_page_always` with a FakeAsk and a dict, then feed `{"class": out[k], "value": ..., "basis": ..., ...}` back through `decision` and `decide(pf, d, ctx, standing, key)`: a replay still goes through the two-key rule (not code-only).
Departures from §17 (each the reading that submits less, or a pin's): (1) a required checkbox the model leaves unticked is unusable (as `resolve.fit_bool`), so A5/A6 decide it; (2) ruling B's vote is replaced: both keys + H known writes T's option (assumed when the reader was tentative); H unknown -> A5, confirm, protective end; `confirm_vote` is kept but no longer called; (3) with H unknown a confirm pick is written only when it differs from every value the model gave and code vetoed (tester C1 / M5 pins); (4) R0's deterministic pick and F2's yes/no fit may not add a contrast word or drop a negation (M3 extended to R0's polarity mapping, the M3 pin needs it); (5) marketing verbs gain "add me", "include me"; acknowledgement verbs gain "have you read"; a read/understand lead with a named policy is an acknowledgement before the declaration reader (ruling 3 kept); (6) an optional vetoed field also gets A5 and its protective end; (7) a plain grade text with no profile grade stays F4 (resume grade or "N/A", assumed); class-of-degree wording with nothing anywhere stops; (8) the per-page cap also ends the page when it is spent with required fields still open after A5.
Probe table (stdin script, tests' FakeAsk, sample profile, no model/network; columns: model right / wrong / class forced ordinary / class missing): H1 "confirm 2 years experience" No|Yes|Yes|Yes (the model's, never a consent value); H1 privacy box refused: consent_refused in all four; H2 "Tick if PEP": unticked x4; H2 list: "None of the above" x4; H3 "Year of birth": 2002 x4; H3 "Geburtsdatum": 2002-03-14 x4; C1 "untrue ... bankrupt" and "deny ... PEP": empty x4 (H unknown, confirm pick ""); C2 "Email address": the profile email x4, "Phone type": the model's option x4; C3 "dismissed": No|No|empty|empty. No row states an adverse declaration, agrees to a refused consent, opts in to marketing, holds an invented owner-only fact or overrides a correct ordinary answer.
Known limits: `rule_want`'s consent branch and `marketing_want` use the narrowed frames; `consent_standing`, `refused_stop`, `consent_key`, `certification`, `policy_ack`, `_REFUSED_STOP` remain defined (dead under `always`; tests import some) for the tester to retire with their pins.

## 2026-11-12 software-engineer -> tester; cc coordinator, answering-rules engineer (Q-121 A1 cache class: pin group 28)
TYPE: handoff
BODY: branch `eng/Q-121-A1-cache` (from int/Q-121 301e908), `pagefill.py` only. Answers the open request above ("Q-121 A1-A cache class").
- Stored: `_always_cache_put` passes `classes=al.classes` to `always.fill_page_always` and writes `decision["class"]` (the `class[:which]` string, e.g. `declaration:pep`, `ordinary`) in every row; the row's `prompt_version` is `AL.PROMPT_VERSION` (`always:v2`; the docstring said v1). A model-answered field with no class gets NO row (never a class-less row).
- Restored: new `pagefill.always_row_decision(key, row) -> Optional[ModelDecision]` rebuilds `klass` / `which` (via `always.parse_class`), `via="cache:always"`, `assumed`, and the FillSource from the stored Answer source's head ("default:x" -> DEFAULT with ref "default:x"; "profile:..." -> PROFILE with the stored ref). It returns None (a MISS: ask again, never a code-only replay) for a row with no readable class, an unknown class string, another `prompt_version` (v1) or an unreadable source. v1 rows also miss at the store (prompt_version is in the cache key).
- Replay under `always` is STILL OFF: `_always_steps` never calls `cache_get` (the strict `_cached` path is untouched, byte-for-byte). To switch it on: in `_always_steps` / `_always_prepare`, call `services.cache_get(_job_id(run), sigs, AL.PROMPT_VERSION, services.model, <the same inputs hash `_always_cache_put` computes>)`, check `F.facts_used_hash` like `_cached`, run `always_row_decision` on each hit and hand the decisions to the answering seam. Not done: the seam has no entry point for pre-made decisions and the design leaves replay off.
- How to pin group 28 (no model, no Edge): `_always_cache_put` needs only a stub run (`types.SimpleNamespace(al=SimpleNamespace(brief, model_keys, corrected, fb, classes), cfg, rctx, fields, order, answers, failed, services, today, qv)`) with a fake `services.cache_put` that collects rows, and `pagefill._job_id` patched; build fields with `llm_fill_t2.pf`, answers with `Answer(..., tier=AnswerTier.LLM, confidence=0.9)`. Assert (1) rows carry `decision["class"]` equal to `al.classes[key]` and `prompt_version == "always:v2"`; (2) `always_row_decision` returns `klass`/`which` set and `via == "cache:always"`; (3) deleting `class`, setting `class="nonsense"` or `prompt_version="always:v1"` gives None; (4) a key missing from `al.classes` writes no row. A full-page pin: run a fake page through `fill_page_always(classes={})` and compare the dict with the stored rows.

## 2026-11-14 software-engineer -> coordinator; cc tester (Q-121 A1 fix1: review H1/H2/H3 + D-211 + rehearsal R1-R6)
TYPE: handoff
RE: D-211 P1-P5, F1-F4, C, M2/M3/M4/M7, item 8; rehearsal R1-R6; docs/reviews/Q-121-A1-code.md; `src/jobscraper/apply/always.py`, `src/jobscraper/apply/facts.py`
BODY: Branch `eng/Q-121-A1-fix1` (from int/Q-121 1393426). Commits 9c3538c (always.py: every lettered group; the edits interleave in one file, so one commit, not one per group), 1d6e946 (facts.py, item 8), cd1711e (M2 for relatives the reader does not class).
Rules as built:
- Negation tokens (`always.NEGATION_WORDS` / `NEGATION_PHRASES` / `has_negation`): not no never none without nor neither nobody nothing cannot deny denies denied denying false incorrect except excluding untrue unable prevent(s/ed/ing) stop stops stopping; any n't contraction (dont doesnt didnt isnt arent wasnt werent havent hasnt hadnt cant couldnt wont wouldnt shouldnt mustnt); "non" and non- words; un- words of 5+ letters except under*, understand*, undergo*, undergraduate(s), underlying, undertak*, until, union(s), unit(s), united, universit*, unique, universal, uniform(s), unix, unity; phrases free of / free from / other than / fail(s/ed/ing) to / rather than. NOT a negation: "denied" naming the event ("been denied entry", "denied a visa") and non- in a class noun (non-compete, non-solicitation, non-disclosure, non-executive, non-profit, non-governmental).
- `_as_asked` inverts a yes/no only for a leading subject-negation frame with no other negation (`_NEG_FRAME`: "have/are/do/did/is/was/were you (ever) never|not|no", "is it true that you ... no|never|not", "can/could you confirm (that) you ...", "(I confirm/declare/certify/attest (that)) I have/am/do/did/was no|never|not"); any other negation leaves the reading unknown.
- `decl_mode` (a false declaration): no reader option -> P3; a box with no negation -> P1 (both keys) / K (one key: the veto as built); a negation in the label or help, a tentative S1 reader, or options not plain Yes/No and not cleanly read -> P2; otherwise P1 / K. P1 = the override as built (D1b kept; D1b also in P2 when there is no negation). P2: the model's answer equal to the reader's option is written; anything else waits (no A5) for ONE TASK_CONFIRM call in A6 (`vote_end`): pick == first answer -> the first answer, pick == reader's option -> that option, both assumed with a "votes:" note; else P4. P3 (H unknown; also any class on a choice list whose H is unknown): only pick == first answer writes it ("check: <class>"). P5: a silent model on a P2 rule: only pick == reader's option writes. `confirm_class` (K mode, H unknown): only a pick that agrees with the first answer.
- DEPARTURE (for "not one row states an adverse declaration"): in P2 the first answer does NOT win the vote when no negation is in play and the reader knows it states the declaration (that is, a tentative reader only); then only pick == reader's option writes. With a negation in play the vote is as ruled.
- P4 (`p4_none_option`): an unticked box, a "None of the above" / "I have no ..." option (`clear_none`), or a plain No with a confident reader, each only with no negation; else empty / unticked.
- F1 (`fact_which`): owner facts never fire on `_FACT_ENTITY` words (company, employer, school, university, institution, office, branch, referee, reference, manager, supervisor, role, position, job, internship, emergency, next of kin, grew up, previous, former, brand, model, ...), except a salary with own words (expected, desired, your, my, current, last, previous, present, asking); never on address / phone / postal code in an experience, education or reference entry group (`_entry_group`); no grade key in an education entry; age only for "Age", "Your / Current age", "Age (years)", "How old are you".
- F2: code key only (address, postal code, phone, age), the model under another class, its value not the profile's (containment), the profile has the value -> ONE `TASK_OWN_FACT` call in A6: Yes -> the profile value; No / unusable -> the model's value, assumed. Equal / contained / no model value -> as built, no call. Owner-only facts keep the veto on either key.
- F3: a TEXT grade not in the profile or resume is H ("N/A" is not); a choice list's pick keeps the old digit test (fix pin Q1).
- F4 / R1 / R2: an owner-fact key comes only from the field's own label (never help or context); `asked_classes` adds the class `fact_which` names ("D.O.B.", "Geburtsdatum"); the model's own `owner_fact:<which>` also unlocks the identity check, except under a generic label (the M2 (a) injection pin); the profile DOB is written in the label's stated format (`date_in_asked_format`: "DD/MM/YYYY" -> 14/03/2002).
- C: retention unset -> the model's value, assumed, "check: consent_retention (not set in your profile)"; `_ACK_OBJ` + the above / this notice / the following / this declaration; exactly "I agree", "I accept", "I acknowledge", "I confirm", "Do you acknowledge?" (and a few more) are acknowledgements; F2 gives an unframed acknowledgement, or a true-and-complete certification, Yes / ticked (assumed) when the model is silent.
- M2: a yes/no, box or list naming a spouse / parent / manager takes the declaration key; other_person only for detail fields; when the declaration reader finds no class, a relation word (`_RELATED`) gives `declaration:relatives_at_company` (with a work word, `_EMPLOYED`) or `declaration:other` (cd1711e). M3: `_MKT_CONTACT` verbs with `_MKT_CONTACT_OBJ` objects; `_RETAIN_BARE_RE` ("please keep / store / retain my", "wish to be considered for", "keep my application on file"). M4: frames has your employment / has your contract / did you ever / do you have any outstanding / have you had; nouns judgment(s), judgement(s), terminated for cause, struck off, barred, banned, blacklisted, insolvency, garnish*. M7: R0 compares non-ASCII text exactly, never "" == "".
- R3: `work_option` reads "sponsorship needed / required", "visa is required", pass holders, student / dependant pass (not authorised); `_work_in_h` reads a fully read list whatever the label's grammar; a country-less list's truth text is "The applicant is a citizen of Singapore and needs no visa to work there."; a silent model on an unreadable work list gets ONE confirm pick, never one code reads as another truth. R4: rule 12 says `other` is ONLY adverse history and lists "Have you worked on similar roles before?" as ordinary; code: `declaration:other` + `default:no_declaration` with no code key and no adverse frame / noun -> A5 once with NOT_ADVERSE_PROBLEM, and that answer is taken as ordinary ("check: the AI read this as a declaration"). R5: rule 12 says owner_fact is the applicant's OWN even when the value is in BRIEFING, with the three examples. Prompt growth: +310 chars / +37 words (about +75 tokens). R6: nothing per-field precedes the shared prefix (system, BRIEFING, JOB, ANSWERS, PAGE are page-constant; `num_ctx` is fixed in backends.py); the low reuse is likely the GPU shared with another session evicting the KV cache (not checked further).
Calls: each P2 / P3 / P5 / F2 field adds ONE call in A6 and skips A5 (before: an A5 share plus one confirm); R4 adds one A5 round; R3 one confirm. Worst case on a page = one call per voted field, within the page cap (3 x chunks + 20).
Probe (stdin, the tests' FakeAsk, sample profile; cells = confirm right / wrong / unusable): H1 x8: R No~/-/-, W -/Yes~/-, O No~/-/-, M No~/-/-; H2 Criminal record / Bankruptcy / PEP and M1 noun labels the same; "Do you deny ... PEP?" R Yes~/-/-, W -/No~/-; French PEP R Non~/-/-, W -/Oui~/- (O / M: no key, the model's value: the accepted R2 / M8); M2 spouse / parent x7 (+ "work for us"): a declaration key on all; R / W / O / M No, wrong value with class ordinary / missing: confirm right No~, else empty, never Yes; plain "convicted" W No/No/No (no call with both keys); "Have you never been convicted?" W Yes~/No~/-; tentative "Do you declare any conflict?" W No~/-/-. H3 x14 (Company address, School address, Employer phone, School contact number, Employer postal code, Age at graduation, Mobile phone model, Phone brand, Street you grew up on, Pay grade of the role, Salary range of the role, Salary of your last internship, entry-group Address / Phone / Postal code): the model's value in every condition, never the profile's; Grade / CGPA / Final grade "First Class" -> the profile's 4.20 / 5.00; Home address / Mobile number / Age invented, class ordinary: check Yes -> profile, No / "" -> the model's value assumed (F2); D.O.B. (DD/MM/YYYY) -> 14/03/2002; Geburtsdatum -> 2002-03-14; Visa status: the citizen option in all nine rows. The ONLY adverse cells are model wrong AND confirm agreeing with it under a negation or with H unknown (H1, H2, "never convicted"): P2 / P3 as ruled ("never empty when both model calls agree"). Coordinator to confirm or tighten.
Tests: `-k always_a1` 112/119, `-k always` 286/300 (the 14 reds are exactly the pins listed below), `-k facts` 19/19, `-k pagefill` 22/22, `-k declarations` 194/195 (the consent pin below), `-k llm_fill` 100/100, `-k layering` 13/13, `-k apply_flow` (alone) 150/150. `tests/manual/always_rehearsal.py --fake --variant sample`: 0 NEVER violations, 9 of 15 pages ready, the three designed stops right, max 10 calls a page, no page over the cap. p5 "undischarged bankrupt" (un- word = a negation) and p6 "employed by a government body" (tentative reader) now end empty because the fake answers every confirm call with "" (P2 / P3: an unusable confirmation -> the protective end); with the real model the confirm call decides. p8 dismissed / litigation with class `ordinary` end empty as before (one key, known H).
Open for the Coordinator: (1) P2 / P3 write the model's adverse answer when the first answer and the confirmation both give it under a negation or with H unknown (ruled; confirm or tighten); (2) un- adjectives that narrow rather than negate ("undischarged", "unspent") count as negations per the list, so those questions always cost a confirm call; (3) a model's wrong `owner_fact:address` class on "Company address" vetoes its own correct value (one key) and ends F4 "N/A" / F0, not the model's value (rare: the model named owner_fact on 2 of 25 owner facts).
STATUS: open

## 2026-11-14 software-engineer -> coordinator; cc tester (Q-121 A1 fix1, final pass: D-211 open points 1-3)
TYPE: handoff
RE: the Coordinator's rulings on open (1)-(3); `src/jobscraper/apply/always.py`; merged int/Q-121 249fcac (122db76)
BODY:
- Open (1) as built (`vote_end`, `verify_meaning`, `adverse_text`, TASK_VERIFY): when the first answer and the confirmation agree on a value the reader's own reading takes as adverse (`in_h` True): with no negation in play -> not written (departure (a), kept); with a negation -> ONE meaning call, WANTED "If the applicant answers «V» to the question «Q», does that state that the applicant <the class's adverse statement from briefing.DECLARATION_STATEMENTS>? Answer yes or no." (label and value on one line, M2 (c)), options yes / no: "no" -> V written, assumed, note "votes: the AI's answer, the check and a meaning check agree (V)"; "yes" / "" / transport failure -> the protective end. P3 with no code reading (`in_h` None) writes the agreed pair, no third call (the ruled residual).
- Reader change for this (`_as_asked` + `_core_question`): a negation only in a note or aside after the question ("...? Please do not include spent convictions.", "(not including ...)") reads the question itself, so the reader has an option (R = No) and the rule is P2 (the label still holds a negation), not P3. Without it the Coordinator's probe row 2 is P3 and an agreed Yes is written with no meaning call.
- Open (3) (`decide`, `_SHARED_FACTS`): the model's `owner_fact:address` / `postal_code` / `phone` with no code key of the same fact is dropped: the model's value stands, assumed, "check: owner_fact:<which>". Owner-only facts (date of birth, national ID, passport, bank, grade, salary, other_id) and age keep the veto on the model's key alone.
- Also fixed, closing pins: C, a silent model on an unframed acknowledgement ("I agree", "I have read the above"; no policy / terms / notice / code / statement named) -> Yes / ticked assumed "check: acknowledgement" (named policies keep certify_true, not assumed); F4, a silent model on a field whose own label is "City" / "Town" / "Current city" (not in an entry group, not another person's) -> contact.city, assumed ("F4 city"), not "N/A".

## 2026-11-14 software-engineer -> coordinator, tester (Q-121 A1: U8b, both differing answers checked for meaning)
TYPE: handoff
RE: `src/jobscraper/apply/always.py` (`u_vote_end`, `verify_meaning`, `CALLS_PER_VOTE`); c218bf8
BODY:
- U8b as built applies when there is NO reader reading and the first answer and the confirmation are both usable and differ. The meaning call runs on the confirmation's value first; if that reply is unusable, the field goes to the protective end at once and the second call is skipped. Otherwise the meaning call runs on the first answer's value too. Outcomes:
  - The confirmation's check "no" and the first's "yes" -> the confirmation's value is written.
  - The first's check "no" and the confirmation's "yes" -> the first answer is written.
  - Anything else -> the protective end.
  - Both writes are assumed; the note names both answers (option labels only).
- A silent model is unchanged from U8: the confirmation's value is written after its own check says "no".
- A tick box follows the same rule. Its meaning call now asks what "ticking it" or "leaving it unticked" states.
- Cap: `CALLS_PER_VOTE` = 3 (the confirmation plus two meaning calls). A page of 16 bare-noun declarations where every first answer and confirmation differ costs at most 1 + 16 x 3 = 49 calls against a cap of 3 + 20 + 48 = 71. Measured with the fake: 45 calls (some nouns have a reading, so one call each). A 20-field chunk costs at most 61 against a cap of 83.
- Probes (FakeAsk, sample profile; value / pick calls; ~ = assumed), for "Criminal record" and "Bankruptcy" under class right / ordinary / missing:
  - First right (No), confirmation flipped (Yes): checks (first no, confirmation yes) -> No~/3; (no, no) -> -/3; (yes, yes) -> -/3; confirmation's check unusable -> -/2.
  - First wrong (Yes), confirmation right (No): (first yes, confirmation no) -> No~/3; (no, no) -> -/3; first's check unusable -> -/3.
  - Silent model with the right confirmation and check no -> No~/2.
  - The box "Tick if you are not a PEP" (no reading): ticked first + unticked confirmation, or the reverse, with the checks ticked "no" / unticked "yes" -> ticked~/3; both "no" -> unticked/3.
- Fake rehearsal (`--quiet-rows`): NEVER 1 (the accepted p24 Chinese bankruptcy question, model `ordinary`, no key); 14 of 24 pages ready.
- Pins for the tester. Add: the probe rows above, and a check that the confirmation's unusable check skips the second call. To flip: any U8 pin that expects the confirmation's value written after ONE meaning "no" when a first answer exists; it now needs the first's check "yes" too (3 calls).
- Tests at c218bf8: `-k layering` 13/13, `-k pagefill` 22/22, `-k apply_flow` alone 150/150. `-k always` 305/357: the same 52 reds as at 1c6106c (the 51 by rule in the fix3 entry, plus U8's test_a1_c1_a_wrong_model_value_*). U8b adds no red and turns none green.

## Handoff: Q-125 employer other names (eng/Q-125-employer-aka, from personal a8d3328)
TYPE: handoff
- Field: `experience[].also_known_as: [str, ...]`, optional. Valid = absent, null, `[]` or a list of 1..10 non-blank strings, each at most 80 characters (counted after strip). Anything else is one `FieldError` at path `experience[i].also_known_as` (message never repeats a value): "must be a list of company names" (string, number, mapping), "must be a list of non-empty company names" (a blank or non-string item), "at most 10 other names", "each name at most 80 characters". Constants `applicant.ALSO_KNOWN_AS`, `ALSO_KNOWN_AS_MAX_NAMES`, `ALSO_KNOWN_AS_MAX_LEN`.
- Form: the profile form IS metadata-driven. `FORM` experience sub-field `also_known_as` (label "Also known as", kind `list_text`, help text as specified) arrives through `form_json()`; `FieldInput.vue` renders entry sub-fields recursively by `kind` (chips with an add box, errors read from `experience[i].also_known_as`) and `applicantForm.covered()` counts a listed sub-field as shown (an unlisted one would show under "Other keys in your file"). No web change needed. A new entry starts with `also_known_as: null` (addEntry), which validates and reads as empty. A resume refresh keeps the field (`_merge_update` keeps keys outside the proposal).
- Matching (`apply/declarations.py`): new `entry_employer_names(entry)` (employer, then the list items that are strings; a wrong-typed value is ignored, never a crash, a bare string is not read letter by letter) and `_entry_matches`; `prior_employment` and `current_employment` use them, signatures unchanged, so `facts.profile_facts` (worked_here / current_employee_here), `resolve` (PRIOR_EMPLOYMENT) and `declarations.value_for` all pick it up. Each name is matched by `company_match` on its own (D-210 unchanged; the names add names). `current_employment` still needs the entry's `end` empty.
- Not changed, outside my scope: `apply/facts.py::other_employers(composed=True)` (line ~255) exempts the applicant's own employers from the G12 "other employers" block for a composed cover letter using only `employer`; a name listed in `also_known_as` that is also a watchlist company is still blocked there (conservative, safe). If the owner wants them exempt: use `declarations.entry_employer_names(e)` in that list comprehension. REQUEST to the owner of facts.py.
- Template: `config/applicant.example.yaml` has the field commented in the experience example (made-up names).
- Tester pins to add (tests/fixtures/apply/applicant.sample.yaml untouched; do not put the field on the shared sample, use a copy in the test): (1) `prior_employment` truth table: entry "Northwind Platforms Inc" vs posting "Northwind" False without the field, True with `["Northwind"]`; `current_employment` True only with `end` empty; `["NW Bank"]` vs "NW Bank Ltd" True and vs "NW Logistics" False; `["Example"]` vs "Example Logistics" False (D-210); match through a posting alias; (2) a string / number / dict / list-with-"" or None / 11 names / 81 characters gives the matching `FieldError` at `experience[0].also_known_as`, while 10 names and 80 characters, `[]` and `None` pass; (3) wrong-typed values never raise in `prior_employment`; (4) `form_json()` experience sub contains `also_known_as` with kind `list_text`, label and help; `tests/test_applicant_form.py` coverage still green; (5) `value_for(L.PRIOR_EMPLOYMENT, ...)` and `facts.profile_facts` worked_here / current_employee_here with the field; (6) optional vitest: `otherKeys` does not list `experience[0].also_known_as` once the form carries the sub-field.
- Vue lane: nothing required.
STATUS: open

  `standing:similar_roles | Experience in a similar, comparable or related role, or in this kind of work (class ordinary) | Yes if any role in standing:prior_roles, or a project in RESUME, relates to the job's field (JOB) or uses its main skills, and name it when free text is asked; No only when nothing is related`.
  The rule sits in COMPUTED FACTS, not STANDING RULES, because the STANDING RULES ids are `contracts.DEFAULT_RULES` (a contract; `verify_basis` refuses a `default:` ref outside it). REQUEST (architect): if the rule should be a STANDING RULES line (`default:experience`, class ordinary), that needs a DEFAULT_RULES id at the next contract version. No code override of an ordinary answer: `verify_basis` only marks a differing yes/no assumed (`_STANDING_JUDGED`: Yes backs `prior_roles` / `similar_roles` when a role is held; a No is "differs from ...", never a veto).
- Item 2 (National Service): `standing:ns_served | National Service (military service) | completed, so the applicant HAS served in the armed forces (National Service counts as military service)`; serving: "serving now, so the applicant IS serving in the armed forces (...)"; exempted / not_applicable: "..., so the applicant has not done National Service"; unset: no line. Printed after `standing:currently_employed`. F3 (no declaration No from government employment) unchanged. `verify_basis`: Yes backs it for completed / serving.
- Token delta (§3.2, sample profile, today 2026-11-14): 4279 -> 4476 (+197; COMPUTED FACTS 651 -> 847) of 18,000. Byte-deterministic: two builds give hash 80753112ab735ce1 both times; section order and the PROFILE / ANSWERS part of the prefix unchanged (PROFILE at the same offset); none of the new lines depends on `today` (the today-diff pin stays green).
- Item 3 (page 26 empties): the SENTENCES were the cause. Before, for a Singapore citizen the confirmation's truth was "The applicant has the right to work there and does not need visa sponsorship." (nothing about restrictions, so "Do you have any restrictions ...?" could take Yes = "I have the right"), and the meaning call asked "does that state that the applicant is not authorised to work there, or needs visa sponsorship to work there?" (a restriction is neither, so a "Yes" was not a contradiction). Scripted as the run: model No, confirmation Yes, both meaning checks "no" -> U8b has no consistent story -> empty. Now, only when the applicant is a citizen of (or a Singapore PR in) the one country asked: truth "The applicant is a citizen of Singapore and may work there with no restriction or limit; no visa sponsorship is needed." and contradiction "... is not authorised to work there, needs visa sponsorship to work there, or has any restriction or limit on the right to work there". A pass holder (EP) keeps the old sentences: the profile does not say a pass is unrestricted (architect: an EP is employer-tied, so a pass holder's "restrictions" question has no stated truth; today the vote decides). The job's country is named when the question names none ("in Singapore"), else "there".
  Probes (model No, truthful meaning call): "Do you have any restrictions on your right to work in Singapore?" and "Is your right to work in Singapore limited in any way?": confirmation No -> No (2 picks); confirmation Yes -> No (3 picks: two meaning checks); confirmation "" -> empty. US versions -> Yes; "Is your right to work in Singapore unrestricted?" -> Yes. A wrong value is never written in any row.

It returns a key that is NOT confident. SPONSORSHIP needs both a need word (need / needs / require / requires / have to / has to) and "apply for / obtain / sponsor (a / an / the / your / any) <pass>", where <pass> is work permit, work pass, employment pass, S pass, work visa or visa. That covers "Will you need your employer / Example Co to apply for a work permit for you in <country>?", "Do you need us to sponsor a work pass?" and "Will Example Co have to obtain an employment pass for you?". WORK_RIGHT covers four wordings from page 26: "prohibited from working", "on a temporary or student visa", "foreigner / foreign national ... requiring a <pass>" and "hold a ... <pass> ... ties you to one employer". No reading: `_work_rule` treats a keyed-only wording as unread unless an existing U9 frame reads it (none of these does), so the vote decides. Controls with no new key: "Do you have a valid visa?", "Are you a foreign national?", free text. "Have you applied for a work permit before?" was already keyed as a declaration before this change.

Model wrong + confirmation right -> the truth (U8b, 3 picks). Model right + confirmation right -> the truth (2 picks). Silent model + confirmation right -> the truth. Model and confirmation both wrong -> empty. In all of these, 0 contradicting values were written. With every check call unusable the field ends empty.
- Item 2, what it was: fix2-E only DROPPED the model's marketing class. That left code's retention key alone, and a single key can only veto. An unticked box does not contradict a TRUE consent (`in_h`: agreeing is never harmful when T agrees), so the model's unticked stood. The same happened under the model classes ordinary, missing and declaration. Only when the model itself said `consent_retention` did both keys agree and the box follow the profile. The fix: when code's key is `consent_retention` and the label has an explicit retention frame (`_RETAIN_BARE_RE`), the model's class counts as retention whatever it was. Probes: both labels x model class retention / marketing / ordinary / missing / declaration x the model said ticked / unticked / nothing. With consent true -> always ticked; with consent false -> always unticked. 0 calls, not assumed, source `profile:consents.data_retention_consent`. Before the fix, consent true + any class but retention + the model's unticked -> unticked.
- `--fake --variant sample --blind-class --parts 20,26`, before (e73864b) / after (534f977): page 20 is unchanged (rows 11-12 ticked both times: the blind fake ticks them itself, so only the probe shows the flip). Page 26: the 8 newly keyed rows (15, 16, 17, 20, 21, 24, 25, 27) go from the fake's answer, written unprotected with no vote, to empty. The runner's fake answers every confirmation with "" (unusable), so the vote cannot confirm them; a real model's confirmation would answer. Page 26 calls 14 -> 30 (cap 59 -> 107), NEVER 0 / 0, over-protection 0 / 0 (empty is acceptable on these rows), rows left empty where acceptable 6 -> 14. Cost: one confirmation per keyed field, plus one or two meaning calls when the votes differ.

## 2026-11-15 software-engineer -> coordinator, tester (Q-121 D-220 P places, R keep on file)
TYPE: handoff
Branch `eng/Q-121-A1-fix1`; int/Q-121 merged at ef9900c (fast-forward). Commits: 1c476dd (P), 0f97dd9 (R), always.py only. No config or contract change; `resolve.py`, `briefing.py`, `apply_flow.py` untouched.
- P, `always.work_place` (two keys, cached per field in `PageCtx.places`): `code_place` = code's reading (the resolver's names / aliases / D-76 capitals, plus "the States" and another country's demonym; unknown = a region code or word, `_PLACE_OPEN` ("outside", "other than", "any location", "any other country", "foreign country" ...), a residence phrase, or in / within + (articles skipped) a word that is no known country, generic word, `_NOT_A_PLACE` word or number). P4: unknown -> empty, NO call. P1 `pure_work_label`: only `_PURE_WORK_WORDS` + ONE job-country reference (Singapore / SG / S'pore / Republic of Singapore, "this country", "the country where / in which this role is based / located", "here") -> the job's truth, no call. P2 `place_call` otherwise, then P3 `quoted_place`: the quote must be a substring of the label (case / spaces / apostrophes folded), not followed at once by and / or / nor / plus; one leading "in" / "within" and "the" stripped, it must equal a country's name or alias (`_PLACE_ALIASES` + `resolve.COUNTRIES` names) or a job reference. Accepted only if code agrees: the job's country -> code finds no other country; another country C -> code finds exactly {C}; NONE -> only if the label holds no place word (a job-country NAME is one; "this country" is not). Anything else -> empty, "work rights: place not recognised". A transport failure in the call ends the page (F5, `transport_failed`).
- P also: `standing_place_agrees` drops the strict reader's work-rights standing answer when its countries differ from `work_place`'s (resolve reads "in the States" as SG; without this the SG standing option was the reading).
- THE PLACE CALL (for the fakes): system SYSTEM_ALWAYS (§3.5 (1)); user = `QUESTION (JSON): "<label>"` / `WANTED: the words of QUESTION that say where the work would be, or NONE` / `OPTIONS (JSON): ["NONE"]` / blank line / TASK_PLACE as the LAST line, starting `Quote the words of QUESTION that say where the work would be`. No BRIEFING, no profile. Schema `{"quote": string, maxLength 120}`; ONE attempt; the reply is read from `quote` only. `PlaceAsk` fits as written. The plain `FakeAsk` does not crash on it (it has QUESTION / WANTED / OPTIONS lines) but records it in `picks` / `pick_tasks` and answers `{"choice": ...}`: no `quote` -> unusable -> the field ends empty with the place note.
- CAP (P5): default page cap = 3 x chunks + 20 + 3 per voted field + 1 per E1 call + 1 per place call (the call raises it by one unless `max_calls_page` is a fixed config value). Comment at CALLS_PAGE_EXTRA.
- DELETED: `_COUNTRY_OVER_REGION` and its use (addendum 3 (i): "anywhere in Singapore" now ends empty, no call, as P6 rules); the old resolver-only body of `work_place`; under `always` a residence phrase ("the country where you live") no longer maps to contact.country (it is an unknown place). `_NOT_A_REGION` kept for code's reading only.
- MY READINGS (say if wrong): negation words, "pass", "permit", "way", "us" in a pronoun position (after for / with / join, or before "to") and "there" after is / are are in the pure list; the deictic job references count as the job country in P1, P3 and code's reading; demonyms and "the States" added to code's found countries; a leading "in" is stripped from a quote (a real model often copies it).
- R, `_consent_rule`: RETENTION without MARKETING -> MARKETING added unless `retention_pure(label, ctx, classes)`: every token in the frame (`_R_VERBS`, `_R_OBJECTS`, file / record / database / system, personal), the neutral list (future / other / similar / suitable + roles / positions / vacancies / opportunities / openings / jobs, consider(ed), durations: up, months, years, numbers), the agreeing and function words, or the employer's own names. With CONSENT_PRIVACY also in the classes (a combined box) the privacy frame (`_PRIVACY_OBJ`, the processing phrase) is not residue (my reading: else every required "privacy policy + keep my details" box stops on a profile without the opt-in). New code key `retain_frame` (consent_frames): a request led by the verb ("Retain my details ...", `_R_REQUEST_RE`) or a pure verb + object ("Retain my CV for up to 2 years for other vacancies"); fix9 item 2 (the model's class forced to retention) covers it too. DELETED `_H3_CONTACT`, `_H3_CONTACT_OBJ`, `retention_markets`.
- OPEN (question): r6 `test_n2_a_keep_on_file_label_with_any_residue...` stays red on ONE label, "Keep my CV on file and let recruiters from partner firms see it", only with BOTH consents true: D-211 M2 (`_key_on`: `facts.about_other_person` + `_RELATED` "partner") keys it declaration:other before the consent frames, the vote ends EMPTY. Never ticked wrongly (retention-only rows pass). Not changed: a ruling on M2 ("partner" before firm / company) or a new wording for the pin.

## 2026-11-15 software-engineer -> coordinator, tester (Q-121 D-221 L5 followers, D-222 S1 S2, home country, keep-on-file basis none)
- Branch `eng/Q-121-A1-fix1`; commits af5c266 (followers), fab354e (home country), 5ca1b2a (keep-on-file), 5a7a873 (S1 + S2, one commit). The home-country and keep-on-file commits came before S1 + S2 because they were built first; each commit is green on its own.
- Followers (D-221 L5): `_PURE_FUNCTION` gained the ruled prepositions and subordinators (under, as, per, via, upon, until, till, during, after, before, since, while, when, if, unless, although, though, because, so, than, then, also, too, either, both, each, every, all, some, yes). The place-openers stay with `_PLACE_OPEN`. `_PURE_CONDITIONS` (permanently, indefinitely, temporarily, immediately, freely, fully, unrestricted, full-time, part-time, long-term, short-term) goes into `_PURE_WORK_WORDS`. A hyphenated compound counts as its words. I judged all of them harmless in the pure form, because a duration or manner word names no place, so there is no second table. "subject to" is still rejected.
- Home country: `_HOME_PLACES` (home country, country of origin, native country) is read by `code_place` as an unknown place: empty with the place note, no call.
- Keep-on-file: `decide` left an optional box BLANK whenever the model's basis was none. New `consent_both_known`: when both keys name the consent (an explicit retention frame counts as the model's key, as in `decide`) and its profile truth is known, the box follows the truth through the class rule. The pure boxes are ticked from `consents.data_retention_consent`; the residue boxes stay unticked from `talent_pool_opt_in`.
- S1: `status_phrases()` is derived from `_CITIZEN`, the passes of `_FIX9_PASS`, `_PASSPORT`, the residency enum and `_STATUS_ONLY` (national, resident: no table held them). `code_place` drops the job country when every mention of it is a status adjective or the demonym and nothing else names a place. `pure_work_label` allows status words (`@st`).
- S2: `status_question` reads "<country / alias / demonym> + status", "<status> of (the) <country>" and a demonym alone before or / and / the end, on a yes/no or a box. A label that also holds a work-rights or sponsorship phrase, or a U9 work frame, is not a status question.
  - Keying: `decide` (and `code_key_full`) key it `work_right:status` whatever the key or the model's class. `work_place` gives its country with no place call. Two countries, a region (EU, ASEAN) or an unknown place: empty with the place note.
  - Truth (`status_truth`): citizen = the nationality. Singapore citizen-or-PR = the residency status. PR / resident of Singapore = the residency status. A Singapore pass = `work_pass` (none_required means No; EP / S Pass only for that pass; another pass value has no truth). Otherwise the nationality (a citizen of X is no PR of X and holds no pass there), else not held (assumed, `default:status_not_held`).
  - U9 is unchanged: no canonical frame, so U1 never writes. Every status answer is decided by the confirmation (truth stated) plus meaning calls: a right model costs 2 calls, a wrong one 3 (the confirmation and two meaning checks write the truth). "Passport number" and "NRIC" are unchanged owner facts.
- TESTER, three pins conflict with S2 by design (`-k always` 556/559):

  - (3) r8 `test_guard_..._never_answered_from_the_singapore_rows`: the written "No" cites `profile:singapore_status.nationality`, the nationality fact S2 names, which the guard's substring "singapore_status" catches. ## 2026-11-15 software-engineer -> coordinator, tester (Q-121 D-222 S3)
TYPE: handoff
- Branch `eng/Q-121-A1-fix1`, commit 01cd5a3 (always.py only; `resolve.py` untouched); int/Q-121 merged at c8a64d4 for the verify.
- S3: `_u9_status_frames()` builds 8 canonical status frames from the country phrases and aliases (c) and the demonyms (d), each a `fullmatch` on the normalised label (U9's own condition, nothing else in the label): "are you (a) <c|d> citizen/national", "are you (a) citizen/national of (the) <c>", "are you (a) <c> citizen or (a) permanent resident/PR", "are you (a) <c> permanent resident/PR", "are you (a) <d>", "are you (a) <d> or (a) <c> PR/permanent resident", "do you hold <c> citizenship/nationality", "do you hold a <c> passport". `reading_kept` gives a status rule (`which == "status"`) a reading only for such a frame (never for a box). A frame is a reading, so U1 / U10 apply unchanged: the model equal to the truth is written with no call, source = the fact (`profile:singapore_status.nationality` / `.residency_status`), not assumed; anything else goes to the confirmation with the truth stated, and the canonical reading vetoes a differing answer (U10). Nothing loosened: a non-pure status label has no reading and stays on the vote path.
- PR (Coordinator correction): a bare "<country> PR / permanent resident" is true only when `residency_status` is `pr` (a citizen: "No", source the residency fact); "citizen or PR" / "Singaporean or Singapore PR" stays true for a citizen or a PR. `_status_one` / `status_truth` already did this; unchanged.
- Vote-path source: when the written value equals the status truth and the label has no negation, the stored source is the status fact (it was `assumption` when the model's own answer was right); assumed stays True. The negation guard keeps "Are you not a Singapore citizen?" from citing the fact for a value it did not check.
